@echo off
setlocal enabledelayedexpansion
chcp 65001 >nul

if "%~1"=="" (
    set /p "ROOT=Введите корневую папку: "
) else (
    set "ROOT=%~1"
)

if not exist "%ROOT%" (
    echo Папка не найдена: %ROOT%
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

set "OUTPUT=%CD%\project_code.txt"
set "EXTS=.py .js .ts .jsx .tsx .html .css .scss .json .md .txt .java .c .cpp .h .hpp .cs .go .rs .php .rb .sql .yml .yaml .xml .sh .bat .cmd .ini .cfg .toml .vue .svelte"

> "%OUTPUT%" echo.

for /r "%ROOT%" %%F in (*) do (
    set "FILE=%%F"
    set "EXT=%%~xF"

    if /I not "%%~nxF"=="project_code.txt" (
        set "SKIP="
        echo !FILE! | findstr /I /C:"\node_modules\\" /C:"\.git\\" /C:"\venv\\" /C:"\.venv\\" /C:"\__pycache__\\" /C:"\dist\\" /C:"\build\\" /C:"\target\\" >nul && set "SKIP=1"

        if not defined SKIP (
            echo !EXTS! | findstr /I /C:"!EXT!" >nul
            if !errorlevel! == 0 (
                echo ============================================================>> "%OUTPUT%"
                echo FILE: %%F>> "%OUTPUT%"
                echo ============================================================>> "%OUTPUT%"
                type "%%F">> "%OUTPUT%"
                echo.>> "%OUTPUT%"
                echo.>> "%OUTPUT%"
                echo [+] %%F
            )
        )
    )
)

echo.
echo Готово! Всё сохранено в: %OUTPUT%
pause

import sys
import os

sys.path.append(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'src'))

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
from footprint_editor.ui.main_window import FootprintEditorWindow
from footprint_editor.core.models import Footprint


def main():
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    app.setStyle("Fusion")

    window = FootprintEditorWindow(footprint=Footprint(name="My_First_Component"))
    window.show()
    sys.exit(app.exec())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\footprint_editor\core\arc_geometry.py

import math


def arc_radius(cx, cy, sx, sy):
    return math.hypot(sx - cx, sy - cy)


def arc_start_angle(cx, cy, sx, sy):
    return math.atan2(sy - cy, sx - cx)


def arc_sample_points(cx, cy, sx, sy, angle_deg, segments=64):
    R = arc_radius(cx, cy, sx, sy)
    if R < 1e-9:
        return [(cx, cy), (sx, sy)]
    a1 = arc_start_angle(cx, cy, sx, sy)
    sweep = math.radians(angle_deg)
    pts = []
    for i in range(segments + 1):
        t = i / segments
        a = a1 + t * sweep
        pts.append((cx + R * math.cos(a), cy + R * math.sin(a)))
    return pts.
.

FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\footprint_editor\core\history.py
============================================================
import copy
from footprint_editor.core.models import Footprint


class History:
    def __init__(self, limit=200):
        self._undo = []
        self._redo = []
        self._limit = limit
        self._current = None

    def reset(self, footprint):
        self._undo.clear()
        self._redo.clear()
        self._current = copy.deepcopy(footprint)

    def push(self, footprint):
        if self._current is None:
            self._current = copy.deepcopy(footprint)
            return
        self._undo.append(self._current)
        if len(self._undo) > self._limit:
            self._undo.pop(0)
        self._redo.clear()
        self._current = copy.deepcopy(footprint)

    def can_undo(self):
        return len(self._undo) > 0

    def can_redo(self):
        return len(self._redo) > 0

    def undo(self):
        if not self.can_undo():
            return None
        self._redo.append(self._current)
        self._current = self._undo.pop()
        return copy.deepcopy(self._current)

    def redo(self):
        if not self.can_redo():
            return None
        self._undo.append(self._current)
        self._current = self._redo.pop()
        return copy.deepcopy(self._current).
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.graphics.factory import GraphicsFactory


class FootprintManager:
    def __init__(self, footprint, scene, canvas):
        self.footprint = footprint
        self.scene = scene
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.items = {}

    def add_model(self, model):
        item = GraphicsFactory.create(model, self.canvas)
        self.scene.addItem(item)
        self.items[model.uid] = item
        return item

    def remove_model(self, model):
        item = self.items.pop(model.uid, None)
        if item is None:
            return
        item.setSelected(False)
        item.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, False)
        item.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsFocusable, False)
        item.setEnabled(False)
        item.setVisible(False)
        if item.scene() is self.scene:
            self.scene.removeItem(item)
        self.scene.update()
        self.canvas.viewport().update()

    def get_next_pad_number(self):
        numbers = []
        for p in self.footprint.pads:
            try:
                numbers.append(int(p.number))
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                pass
        return str(max(numbers) + 1) if numbers else "1".
.

FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\footprint_editor\core\models.py

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from typing import List, Optional
import copy
from constants import Sizes


class PadShape(Enum):
    CIRCLE = "circle"
    RECT = "rect"
    OVAL = "oval"


class PadType(Enum):
    STANDARD = "standard"
    SMD = "smd"
    CONN = "conn"


class TextType(Enum):
    NORMAL = "normal"
    REFERENCE = "reference"
    VALUE = "value"


AVAILABLE_LAYERS = [
    "F.Cu", "B.Cu",
    "F.Adhes", "B.Adhes",
    "F.Paste", "B.Paste",
    "F.SilkS", "B.SilkS",
    "F.Mask", "B.Mask",
    "Cmts.User", "Eco1.User", "Eco2.User",
]


DEFAULT_LAYERS = {
    PadType.STANDARD: ["F.Cu", "B.Cu", "F.Mask", "B.Mask"],
    PadType.SMD:      ["F.Cu", "F.Paste", "F.Mask"],
    PadType.CONN:     ["F.Cu", "F.Mask"],
}


def default_layers_for_pad_type(pad_type):
    return list(DEFAULT_LAYERS.get(pad_type, DEFAULT_LAYERS[PadType.STANDARD]))


_next_uid = [1]


def new_uid():
    value = _next_uid[0]
    _next_uid[0] += 1
    return value


def _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo):
    cls = self.__class__
    result = cls.__new__(cls)
    memo[id(self)] = result
    for k, v in self.__dict__.items():
        if k == 'graphics_item':
            setattr(result, k, None)
        else:
            setattr(result, k, copy.deepcopy(v, memo))
    return result


@dataclass
class BaseObject:
    x: float
    y: float
    uid: int = field(default_factory=new_uid, kw_only=True)
    locked: bool = field(default=False, kw_only=True)

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo):
        return _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo)


@dataclass
class Pad(BaseObject):
    number: str
    shape: PadShape
    pad_type: PadType
    width: float
    height: float
    drill: Optional[float] = None
    layers: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def __post_init__(self):
        if not self.layers:
            self.layers = default_layers_for_pad_type(self.pad_type)


@dataclass
class Line(BaseObject):
    x2: float
    y2: float
    width: float = Sizes.DEFAULT_LINE_WIDTH
    layer: str = "F.SilkS"


@dataclass
class Circle(BaseObject):
    radius: float
    width: float = Sizes.DEFAULT_LINE_WIDTH
    layer: str = "F.SilkS"


@dataclass
class Arc(BaseObject):
    sx: float
    sy: float
    angle: float = 90.0
    width: float = Sizes.DEFAULT_LINE_WIDTH
    layer: str = "F.SilkS"


@dataclass
class BaseText(BaseObject):
    text: str
    size: float = 1.0
    visible: bool = True
    layer: str = "F.SilkS"


@dataclass
class Text(BaseText):
    text_type: TextType = TextType.NORMAL


@dataclass
class ReferenceText(Text):
    def __post_init__(self):
        self.text_type = TextType.REFERENCE
        self.locked = True


@dataclass
class ValueText(Text):
    def __post_init__(self):
        self.text_type = TextType.VALUE
        self.locked = True


@dataclass
class Footprint:
    name: str
    reference: str = "REF**"
    value: str = "VAL**"
    pads: List[Pad] = field(default_factory=list)
    lines: List[Line] = field(default_factory=list)
    circles: List[Circle] = field(default_factory=list)
    arcs: List[Arc] = field(default_factory=list)
    texts: List[Text] = field(default_factory=list).
.

FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\footprint_editor\graphics\arc_item.py
============================================================
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem, QStyle
from PySide6.QtGui import QPen, QColor, QPainterPath, QPainterPathStroker
from PySide6.QtCore import QRectF, QPointF, Qt
from footprint_editor.graphics.base import BaseGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.core.arc_geometry import arc_sample_points
from constants import Colors


class ArcGraphicsItem(BaseGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, arc_model, canvas, is_preview=False, parent=None):
        super().__init__(arc_model, canvas, parent)
        self.is_preview = is_preview
        self._path_cache = None
        self._path_key = None

    def _build_path(self):
        m = self.model
        key = (m.x, m.y, m.sx, m.sy, m.angle)
        if self._path_key == key and self._path_cache is not None:
            return self._path_cache

        path = QPainterPath()
        pts = arc_sample_points(m.x, m.y, m.sx, m.sy, m.angle, 64)
        if not pts:
            self._path_cache = path
            self._path_key = key
            return path
        path.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1])
        for px, py in pts[1:]:
            path.lineTo(px, py)
        self._path_cache = path
        self._path_key = key
        return path

    def boundingRect(self):
        b = self._build_path().boundingRect()
        extra = self.model.width / 2.0 + 0.5
        return b.adjusted(-extra, -extra, extra, extra)

    def shape(self):
        stroker = QPainterPathStroker()
        stroker.setWidth(max(0.5, self.model.width * 2.0))
        return stroker.createStroke(self._build_path())

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        if option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected:
            color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
        else:
            color = QColor(*Colors.TEXT_VISIBLE)

        pen = QPen(color, self.model.width if not self.is_preview else 0.05)
        pen.setCosmetic(False)
        pen.setCapStyle(Qt.PenCapStyle.RoundCap)
        if self.is_preview:
            pen.setStyle(Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)

        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
        painter.drawPath(self._build_path())

    def apply_drag_delta(self, start, dx, dy):
        if getattr(self.model, 'locked', False):
            return
        self.model.x = start[1] + dx
        self.model.y = start[2] + dy
        self.model.sx = start[3] + dx
        self.model.sy = start[4] + dy
        self._path_cache = None
        self.prepareGeometryChange()
        self.update().
.

FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\footprint_editor\graphics\base.py
============================================================
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QPointF


class BaseGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, model, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.model = model
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.model.graphics_item = self

        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setAcceptedMouseButtons(Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton | Qt.MouseButton.RightButton)

        self._drag_start_mouse = None
        self._is_drag_leader = False
        self._drag_moved = False
        self._group_drag_start = {}

        self._handle_radius = 1.0
        self._active_handle = None
        self._hover_handle = None
        self.setAcceptHoverEvents(True)

    def is_selected(self):
        return self.isSelected()

    def _collect_selected_items(self):
        self._group_drag_start = {}
        scene = self.scene()
        if scene is None:
            return
        for item in scene.selectedItems():
            cls_name = type(item).__name__
            if cls_name == 'PadGraphicsItem':
                self._group_drag_start[item] = ('pad', item.model.x, item.model.y)
            elif cls_name == 'LineGraphicsItem':
                self._group_drag_start[item] = ('line', item.model.x, item.model.y, item.model.x2, item.model.y2)
            elif cls_name == 'CircleGraphicsItem':
                self._group_drag_start[item] = ('circle', item.model.x, item.model.y)
            elif cls_name == 'ArcGraphicsItem':
                self._group_drag_start[item] = ('arc', item.model.x, item.model.y, item.model.sx, item.model.sy)
            elif cls_name == 'TextGraphicsItem':
                self._group_drag_start[item] = ('text', item.model.x, item.model.y)

    def get_handles(self):
        return []

    def update_handle_from_drag(self, handle_idx, pos):
        pass

    def _hit_test_handle(self, scene_pos):
        if not self.isSelected():
            return None
        handles = self.get_handles()
        view = self.canvas
        if not view:
            return None
        scale = view.transform().m11()
        if scale <= 0:
            return None
        radius = self._handle_radius * 3.0 / scale

        best_idx = None
        best_dist = float('inf')

        for i, pt in enumerate(handles):
            dx = scene_pos.x() - pt.x()
            dy = scene_pos.y() - pt.y()
            dist = (dx * dx + dy * dy) ** 0.5
            if dist < radius and dist < best_dist:
                best_idx = i
                best_dist = dist

        return best_idx

    def _notify_properties_panel(self):
        if hasattr(self.canvas, 'properties_changed'):
            self.canvas.properties_changed.emit(self.model)

    def hoverMoveEvent(self, event):
        scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.pos())
        idx = self._hit_test_handle(scene_pos)
        if idx != self._hover_handle:
            self._hover_handle = idx
            self.update()
        if idx is not None:
            self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.SizeAllCursor)
        else:
            self.unsetCursor()
        super().hoverMoveEvent(event)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.pos())
            handle_idx = self._hit_test_handle(scene_pos)

            if handle_idx is not None:
                self._active_handle = handle_idx
                self._drag_start_mouse = scene_pos
                self._is_drag_leader = True
                self._drag_moved = False
                self._group_drag_start = {}
                self.update()
                event.accept()
                return

            super().mousePressEvent(event)
            if self.isSelected():
                if not (event.modifiers() & Qt.KeyboardModifier.ControlModifier):
                    self._drag_start_mouse = scene_pos
                    self._is_drag_leader = True
                    self._drag_moved = False
                    self._collect_selected_items()
        else:
            super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        if self._is_drag_leader and (event.buttons() & Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton):
            current_mouse = self.mapToScene(event.pos())

            if self._active_handle is not None:
                grid = self.canvas.grid_size
                snapped_x = round(current_mouse.x() / grid) * grid
                snapped_y = round(current_mouse.y() / grid) * grid
                self.update_handle_from_drag(self._active_handle, QPointF(snapped_x, snapped_y))
                self._drag_moved = True
                self._notify_properties_panel()
                event.accept()
                return

            delta = current_mouse - self._drag_start_mouse
            grid = self.canvas.grid_size
            snapped_dx = round(delta.x() / grid) * grid
            snapped_dy = round(delta.y() / grid) * grid

            if snapped_dx != 0 or snapped_dy != 0:
                self._drag_moved = True

            for item, start in self._group_drag_start.items():
                item.apply_drag_delta(start, snapped_dx, snapped_dy)
            self._notify_properties_panel()
            event.accept()
        else:
            self._is_drag_leader = False
            super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        was_dragging = self._is_drag_leader and self._drag_moved
        self._is_drag_leader = False
        self._drag_moved = False
        self._drag_start_mouse = None
        self._active_handle = None
        self._group_drag_start = {}
        self.update()
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)
        if was_dragging and hasattr(self.canvas, 'content_changed'):
            self.canvas.content_changed.emit()

    def update_geometry(self):
        self.prepareGeometryChange()
        self.update()

    def apply_drag_delta(self, start, dx, dy):
        raise NotImplementedError.
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem, QStyle
from PySide6.QtGui import QPen, QColor, QPainterPath, QPainterPathStroker
from PySide6.QtCore import QRectF, QPointF, Qt
from footprint_editor.graphics.base import BaseGraphicsItem
from constants import Colors


class CircleGraphicsItem(BaseGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, circle_model, canvas, is_preview=False, parent=None):
        super().__init__(circle_model, canvas, parent)
        self.is_preview = is_preview

    def boundingRect(self):
        r = self.model.radius
        extra = 0.2
        return QRectF(self.model.x - r, self.model.y - r,
                      2 * r, 2 * r).adjusted(-extra, -extra, extra, extra)

    def shape(self):
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.addEllipse(QPointF(self.model.x, self.model.y),
                        self.model.radius, self.model.radius)
        stroker = QPainterPathStroker()
        stroker.setWidth(0.3)
        return stroker.createStroke(path)

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        if option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected:
            color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
        else:
            color = QColor(*Colors.TEXT_VISIBLE)

        pen = QPen(color, self.model.width if not self.is_preview else 0.05)
        pen.setCosmetic(False)
        if self.is_preview:
            pen.setStyle(Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)

        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
        painter.drawEllipse(QPointF(self.model.x, self.model.y),
                            self.model.radius, self.model.radius)

    def apply_drag_delta(self, start, dx, dy):
        if getattr(self.model, 'locked', False):
            return
        self.model.x = start[1] + dx
        self.model.y = start[2] + dy
        self.prepareGeometryChange()
        self.update().

from footprint_editor.core.models import Pad, Line, Circle, Arc, Text
from footprint_editor.graphics.pad_item import PadGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.graphics.line_item import LineGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.graphics.circle_item import CircleGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.graphics.arc_item import ArcGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.graphics.text_item import TextGraphicsItem


class GraphicsFactory:
    @staticmethod
    def create(model, canvas, is_preview=False):
        if isinstance(model, Pad):
            return PadGraphicsItem(model, canvas)
        if isinstance(model, Line):
            return LineGraphicsItem(model, canvas, is_preview)
        if isinstance(model, Circle):
            return CircleGraphicsItem(model, canvas, is_preview)
        if isinstance(model, Arc):
            return ArcGraphicsItem(model, canvas, is_preview)
        if isinstance(model, Text):
            return TextGraphicsItem(model, canvas)
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown model type: {type(model)}").
.

FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\footprint_editor\graphics\line_item.py

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem, QStyle
from PySide6.QtGui import QPen, QColor, QPainterPath, QPainterPathStroker, QBrush
from PySide6.QtCore import QLineF, QRectF, QPointF, Qt
from footprint_editor.graphics.base import BaseGraphicsItem
from constants import Colors


class LineGraphicsItem(BaseGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, line_model, canvas, is_preview=False, parent=None):
        super().__init__(line_model, canvas, parent)
        self.is_preview = is_preview
        if not is_preview:
            self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, False)

    def boundingRect(self):
        extra = 1.0
        rect = QRectF(self.model.x, self.model.y,
                      self.model.x2 - self.model.x,
                      self.model.y2 - self.model.y)
        return rect.normalized().adjusted(-extra, -extra, extra, extra)

    def shape(self):
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.moveTo(self.model.x, self.model.y)
        path.lineTo(self.model.x2, self.model.y2)
        stroker = QPainterPathStroker()
        stroker.setWidth(max(0.5, self.model.width * 1.5))
        return stroker.createStroke(path)

    def get_handles(self):
        return [
            QPointF(self.model.x, self.model.y),
            QPointF(self.model.x2, self.model.y2),
        ]

    def update_handle_from_drag(self, handle_idx, pos):
        if handle_idx == 0:
            if self._point_collision(pos.x(), pos.y(), is_start=True):
                return
            self.model.x = pos.x()
            self.model.y = pos.y()
        elif handle_idx == 1:
            if self._point_collision(pos.x(), pos.y(), is_start=False):
                return
            self.model.x2 = pos.x()
            self.model.y2 = pos.y()
        self.prepareGeometryChange()
        self.update()

    def _point_collision(self, x, y, is_start):
        eps = 0.0001
        if is_start:
            return abs(self.model.x - self.model.x2) < eps and abs(self.model.y - self.model.y2) < eps
        else:
            return abs(x - self.model.x) < eps and abs(y - self.model.y) < eps

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        if option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected:
            color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
        else:
            color = QColor(*Colors.TEXT_VISIBLE)

        pen = QPen(color, self.model.width if not self.is_preview else 0.05)
        pen.setCosmetic(False)
        pen.setCapStyle(Qt.PenCapStyle.RoundCap)
        if self.is_preview:
            pen.setStyle(Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)

        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.drawLine(QLineF(self.model.x, self.model.y, self.model.x2, self.model.y2))

        if self.is_selected() and not self.is_preview:
            for i, pt in enumerate(self.get_handles()):
                is_active = (i == self._active_handle)
                is_hover = (i == self._hover_handle)

                is_colliding = False
                if is_active:
                    if i == 0:
                        is_colliding = self._point_collision(pt.x(), pt.y(), is_start=True)
                    else:
                        is_colliding = self._point_collision(pt.x(), pt.y(), is_start=False)

                if is_colliding:
                    fill = QColor(255, 0, 0)
                    border = QColor(255, 255, 255)
                    radius_px = 6.0
                elif is_active:
                    fill = QColor(0, 200, 0)
                    border = QColor(255, 255, 255)
                    radius_px = 6.0
                elif is_hover:
                    fill = QColor(255, 255, 255)
                    border = QColor(0, 150, 255)
                    radius_px = 5.0
                else:
                    fill = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
                    border = QColor(255, 255, 255)
                    radius_px = 4.0

                scale = self.canvas.transform().m11() if self.canvas else 1.0
                radius = radius_px / scale if scale > 0 else radius_px

                p = QPen(border, 0)
                p.setCosmetic(True)
                painter.setPen(p)
                painter.setBrush(QBrush(fill))
                painter.drawEllipse(pt, radius, radius)

    def apply_drag_delta(self, start, dx, dy):
        if getattr(self.model, 'locked', False):
            return
        self.model.x = start[1] + dx
        self.model.y = start[2] + dy
        self.model.x2 = start[3] + dx
        self.model.y2 = start[4] + dy
        self.prepareGeometryChange()
        self.update().
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem, QStyle
from PySide6.QtGui import QColor
from PySide6.QtCore import QRectF, Qt
from footprint_editor.core.models import PadShape, PadType
from footprint_editor.graphics.base import BaseGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.graphics.text_path import build_text_path
from constants import Colors, Sizes


STANDARD_COLOR = (180, 160, 80)
SMD_COLOR = (220, 90, 90)
CONN_COLOR = (235, 150, 60)


class PadGraphicsItem(BaseGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, pad, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(pad, canvas, parent)
        self.setPos(pad.x, pad.y)
        self._text_path = None
        self._text_path_key = None

    def boundingRect(self):
        w = self.model.width
        h = self.model.height
        extra = 0.5
        return QRectF(-w / 2 - extra, -h / 2 - extra, w + 2 * extra, h + 2 * extra)

    def _text(self):
        target_h = min(self.model.width, self.model.height) * Sizes.PAD_NUMBER_FONT_RATIO
        key = (self.model.number, target_h)
        if self._text_path_key != key:
            self._text_path = build_text_path(self.model.number, target_h, bold=True, center=True)
            self._text_path_key = key
        return self._text_path

    def _base_color(self):
        if self.model.pad_type == PadType.SMD:
            return SMD_COLOR
        if self.model.pad_type == PadType.CONN:
            return CONN_COLOR
        return STANDARD_COLOR

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        if option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected:
            color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
        else:
            color = QColor(*self._base_color())

        w = self.model.width
        h = self.model.height
        drill = self.model.drill or 0.0

        painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
        painter.setBrush(color)

        if self.model.shape == PadShape.CIRCLE:
            painter.drawEllipse(QRectF(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h))
        elif self.model.shape == PadShape.RECT:
            painter.drawRect(QRectF(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h))
        elif self.model.shape == PadShape.OVAL:
            painter.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h), w / 2, h / 2)

        if drill > 0:
            painter.setBrush(QColor(*Colors.BACKGROUND))
            painter.drawEllipse(QRectF(-drill / 2, -drill / 2, drill, drill))

        path = self._text()
        if not path.isEmpty():
            painter.save()
            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            painter.setBrush(QColor(*Colors.PAD_NUMBER))
            painter.drawPath(path)
            painter.restore()

    def apply_drag_delta(self, start, dx, dy):
        if getattr(self.model, 'locked', False):
            return
        self.model.x = start[1] + dx
        self.model.y = start[2] + dy
        self.setPos(self.model.x, self.model.y).
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem, QStyle
from PySide6.QtGui import QColor
from PySide6.QtCore import QRectF, Qt
from footprint_editor.graphics.base import BaseGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.graphics.text_path import build_text_path
from footprint_editor.core.models import TextType
from constants import Colors, Sizes


class TextGraphicsItem(BaseGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, text_model, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(text_model, canvas, parent)
        self._path_cache = None
        self._path_key = None

    def _path(self):
        target_h = self.model.size * Sizes.TEXT_FONT_MULTIPLIER
        key = (self.model.text, target_h)
        if self._path_key != key:
            self._path_cache = build_text_path(self.model.text, target_h, center=True)
            self._path_key = key
        return self._path_cache

    def boundingRect(self):
        path = self._path()
        if path.isEmpty():
            return QRectF(self.model.x, self.model.y, 0.01, 0.01)
        b = path.boundingRect()
        return QRectF(self.model.x + b.left(), self.model.y + b.top(),
                      b.width(), b.height()).adjusted(-0.5, -0.5, 0.5, 0.5)

    def _color(self, selected):
        if selected:
            return QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
        if self.model.text_type == TextType.REFERENCE:
            return QColor(*Colors.REFERENCE)
        if self.model.text_type == TextType.VALUE:
            return QColor(*Colors.VALUE)
        return QColor(*(Colors.TEXT_VISIBLE if self.model.visible else Colors.TEXT_HIDDEN))

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        path = self._path()
        if path.isEmpty():
            return
        color = self._color(bool(option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected))

        painter.save()
        if not self.model.visible:
            painter.setOpacity(0.3)
        painter.translate(self.model.x, self.model.y)
        painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
        painter.setBrush(color)
        painter.drawPath(path)
        painter.restore()

    def apply_drag_delta(self, start, dx, dy):
        self.model.x = start[1] + dx
        self.model.y = start[2] + dy
        self.prepareGeometryChange()
        self.update().
.

FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\footprint_editor\graphics\text_path.py

from PySide6.QtGui import QFont, QPainterPath, QTransform


def build_text_path(text, target_height, bold=False, center=False):
    path = QPainterPath()
    if not text or target_height <= 0:
        return path

    font = QFont()
    font.setPointSizeF(20.0)
    font.setBold(bold)
    path.addText(0, 0, font, text)
    bounds = path.boundingRect()
    if bounds.height() > 0:
        scale = target_height / bounds.height()
        t = QTransform()
        t.scale(scale, scale)
        path = t.map(path)
        if center:
            b = path.boundingRect()
            path.translate(-b.center().x(), -b.center().y())
    return path.
.

FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\footprint_editor\kicad\exporter.py
============================================================
from footprint_editor.core.models import PadShape, PadType, TextType
from footprint_editor.kicad.units import mm_to_kicad
from footprint_editor.kicad.layers import layer_to_id


PAD_ATTR_MAP = {
    PadType.STANDARD: ('STD', '00E0FFFF'),
    PadType.SMD:      ('SMD', '00888000'),
    PadType.CONN:     ('CONN', '00808000'),
}


def _pad_block(pad):
    shape_map = {PadShape.RECT: 'R', PadShape.CIRCLE: 'C', PadShape.OVAL: 'O'}
    shape_char = shape_map.get(pad.shape, 'C')
    attr, mask = PAD_ATTR_MAP.get(pad.pad_type, PAD_ATTR_MAP[PadType.STANDARD])

    if pad.pad_type == PadType.STANDARD:
        drill_val = pad.drill if pad.drill else 0.8
        dr_line = f"Dr {mm_to_kicad(drill_val)} 0 0"
    else:
        dr_line = "Dr 0 0 0"

    return [
        "$PAD",
        f'Sh "{pad.number}" {shape_char} '
        f'{mm_to_kicad(pad.width)} {mm_to_kicad(pad.height)} 0 0 0',
        dr_line,
        f"At {attr} N {mask}",
        'Ne 0 ""',
        f"Po {mm_to_kicad(pad.x)} {mm_to_kicad(pad.y)}",
        "$EndPAD",
    ]


def _text_block(text, idx):
    x_k = mm_to_kicad(text.x)
    y_k = mm_to_kicad(text.y)
    size_k = mm_to_kicad(text.size)
    vis = 'V' if text.visible else 'I'
    lid = layer_to_id(text.layer)
    value = text.text if text.text else "Text"
    return (
        f'T{idx} {x_k} {y_k} {size_k} {size_k} 0 120 N {vis} {lid} N "{value}"'
    )


def _line_block(line):
    lid = layer_to_id(line.layer)
    return (
        f"DS {mm_to_kicad(line.x)} {mm_to_kicad(line.y)} "
        f"{mm_to_kicad(line.x2)} {mm_to_kicad(line.y2)} "
        f"{mm_to_kicad(line.width)} {lid}"
    )


def _circle_block(circle):
    x_c = mm_to_kicad(circle.x)
    y_c = mm_to_kicad(circle.y)
    r_k = mm_to_kicad(circle.radius)
    lid = layer_to_id(circle.layer)
    return f"DC {x_c} {y_c} {x_c + r_k} {y_c} {mm_to_kicad(circle.width)} {lid}"


def _arc_block(arc):
    lid = layer_to_id(arc.layer)
    return (
        f"DA {mm_to_kicad(arc.x)} {mm_to_kicad(arc.y)} "
        f"{mm_to_kicad(arc.sx)} {mm_to_kicad(arc.sy)} "
        f"{int(round(arc.angle * 10))} {mm_to_kicad(arc.width)} {lid}"
    )


def _texts_blocks(fp):
    lines = []
    text_idx = 2
    for text in fp.texts:
        if text.text_type == TextType.REFERENCE:
            idx = 0
        elif text.text_type == TextType.VALUE:
            idx = 1
        else:
            idx = text_idx
            text_idx += 1
        lines.append(_text_block(text, idx))
    return lines


def build_kicad_module_text(fp):
    lines = [
        f"$MODULE {fp.name}",
        "Po 0 0 0 15 00000000 00000000 ~~",
        f"Li {fp.name}",
        "Sc 00000000",
        "AR",
        "Op 0 0 0",
    ]

    lines.extend(_texts_blocks(fp))

    for line in fp.lines:
        lines.append(_line_block(line))

    for circle in fp.circles:
        lines.append(_circle_block(circle))

    for arc in fp.arcs:
        lines.append(_arc_block(arc))

    for pad in fp.pads:
        lines.extend(_pad_block(pad))

    lines.append(f"$EndMODULE  {fp.name}")
    return "\n".join(lines).
.
============================================================
FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\footprint_editor\kicad\importer.py

import re
import math
from pathlib import Path

from footprint_editor.core.models import (
    Footprint, Pad, PadShape, PadType, Line, Circle, Arc, Text, TextType,
    default_layers_for_pad_type,
)
from footprint_editor.kicad.units import kicad_to_mm
from footprint_editor.kicad.layers import (
    KICAD_LAYER_TO_INTERNAL, LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL, layer_from_id,
)


TEXT_PATTERN_SIMPLE = re.compile(
    r'^T(\d+)\s+([^\r\n"]+?)\s+"([^"]*)"',
    re.MULTILINE
)


def parse_kicad_mod(filepath):
    content = Path(filepath).read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore')
    return parse_kicad_content(content)


def parse_kicad_content(content):
    footprints = []
    pattern = re.compile(r'\$MODULE\s+(\S+)(.*?)\$EndMODULE', re.DOTALL)
    for match in pattern.finditer(content):
        name = match.group(1)
        body = match.group(2)
        fp = parse_module(name, body)
        footprints.append(fp)
    return footprints


def parse_module(name, body):
    fp = Footprint(name=name)

    t0_match = re.search(r'^T0\s+([^\r\n]+)', body, re.MULTILINE)
    t1_match = re.search(r'^T1\s+([^\r\n]+)', body, re.MULTILINE)

    if t0_match:
        ref_text = parse_text_line(t0_match.group(1), TextType.REFERENCE)
        ref_text.locked = True
        fp.texts.append(ref_text)
        fp.reference = ref_text.text

    if t1_match:
        val_text = parse_text_line(t1_match.group(1), TextType.VALUE)
        val_text.locked = True
        fp.texts.append(val_text)
        fp.value = val_text.text

    for text_match in TEXT_PATTERN_SIMPLE.finditer(body):
        idx = int(text_match.group(1))
        if idx in (0, 1):
            continue
        params = text_match.group(2).split()
        text_value = text_match.group(3)

        x = kicad_to_mm(params[0]) if len(params) > 0 else 0
        y = kicad_to_mm(params[1]) if len(params) > 1 else 0
        w = kicad_to_mm(params[2]) if len(params) > 2 else kicad_to_mm(400)
        h = kicad_to_mm(params[3]) if len(params) > 3 else w
        size = max(w, h) if max(w, h) > 0 else 1.0

        visible = 'I' not in params[4:8]

        layer = "F.SilkS"
        for tok in params:
            try:
                layer_id = int(tok)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            if layer_id in LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL:
                layer = layer_from_id(layer_id)
                break

        t = Text(
            x=x, y=y,
            text=text_value if text_value else "Text",
            size=size,
            visible=visible,
            text_type=TextType.NORMAL,
            layer=layer,
        )
        fp.texts.append(t)

    for ds_match in re.finditer(
        r'^DS\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
        body, re.MULTILINE
    ):
        x1, y1, x2, y2, thickness, layer_id = ds_match.groups()
        line = Line(
            x=kicad_to_mm(x1), y=kicad_to_mm(y1),
            x2=kicad_to_mm(x2), y2=kicad_to_mm(y2),
            width=kicad_to_mm(thickness),
            layer=layer_from_id(layer_id),
        )
        fp.lines.append(line)

    for dc_match in re.finditer(
        r'^DC\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
        body, re.MULTILINE
    ):
        xc, yc, xp, yp, thickness, layer_id = dc_match.groups()
        xc_mm = kicad_to_mm(xc)
        yc_mm = kicad_to_mm(yc)
        xp_mm = kicad_to_mm(xp)
        yp_mm = kicad_to_mm(yp)
        radius = math.hypot(xp_mm - xc_mm, yp_mm - yc_mm)
        circle = Circle(
            x=xc_mm, y=yc_mm,
            radius=radius,
            width=kicad_to_mm(thickness),
            layer=layer_from_id(layer_id),
        )
        fp.circles.append(circle)

    for da_match in re.finditer(
        r'^DA\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
        body, re.MULTILINE
    ):
        cx, cy, sx, sy, angle, thickness, layer_id = da_match.groups()
        arc = Arc(
            x=kicad_to_mm(cx), y=kicad_to_mm(cy),
            sx=kicad_to_mm(sx), sy=kicad_to_mm(sy),
            angle=float(angle) / 10.0,
            width=kicad_to_mm(thickness),
            layer=layer_from_id(layer_id),
        )
        fp.arcs.append(arc)

    pad_pattern = re.compile(r'\$PAD(.*?)\$EndPAD', re.DOTALL)
    for pad_match in pad_pattern.finditer(body):
        pad = parse_pad(pad_match.group(1))
        if pad:
            fp.pads.append(pad)

    return fp


def parse_text_line(text_str, text_type):
    text_match = re.search(r'"([^"]*)"', text_str)
    text = text_match.group(1) if text_match else ""

    params_str = text_str[:text_match.start()].strip() if text_match else text_str
    params = params_str.split()

    x = kicad_to_mm(params[0]) if len(params) > 0 else 0
    y = kicad_to_mm(params[1]) if len(params) > 1 else 0
    w = kicad_to_mm(params[2]) if len(params) > 2 else kicad_to_mm(400)
    h = kicad_to_mm(params[3]) if len(params) > 3 else w
    size = max(w, h) if max(w, h) > 0 else 1.0

    visible = 'I' not in params[4:8]

    layer = "F.SilkS"
    for tok in params:
        try:
            layer_id = int(tok)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if layer_id in LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL:
            layer = layer_from_id(layer_id)
            break

    return Text(
        x=x, y=y, text=text, size=size,
        visible=visible, text_type=text_type, locked=True,
        layer=layer,
    )


def parse_pad(pad_body):
    sh_match = re.search(r'Sh\s+"([^"]*)"\s+(\w+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)', pad_body)
    dr_match = re.search(r'Dr\s+(\d+)', pad_body)
    po_match = re.search(r'Po\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)', pad_body)
    at_match = re.search(r'At\s+(\w+)', pad_body)
    la_match = re.search(r'^La\s+([^\r\n]+)', pad_body, re.MULTILINE)

    if not sh_match or not po_match:
        return None

    number = sh_match.group(1)
    shape_str = sh_match.group(2)
    size_x = kicad_to_mm(sh_match.group(3))
    size_y = kicad_to_mm(sh_match.group(4))
    x = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(1))
    y = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(2))
    drill = kicad_to_mm(dr_match.group(1)) if dr_match else 0

    shape_map = {'R': PadShape.RECT, 'C': PadShape.CIRCLE, 'O': PadShape.OVAL}
    shape = shape_map.get(shape_str, PadShape.CIRCLE)

    pad_type = PadType.STANDARD
    if at_match:
        attr = at_match.group(1)
        if attr == 'SMD':
            pad_type = PadType.SMD
        elif attr == 'CONN':
            pad_type = PadType.CONN
        else:
            pad_type = PadType.STANDARD

    layers = []
    if la_match:
        for token in la_match.group(1).split():
            internal = KICAD_LAYER_TO_INTERNAL.get(token)
            if internal and internal not in layers:
                layers.append(internal)

    if not layers:
        layers = default_layers_for_pad_type(pad_type)

    return Pad(
        x=x, y=y,
        number=number, shape=shape, pad_type=pad_type,
        width=size_x, height=size_y,
        drill=drill if drill > 0 else None,
        layers=layers,
    ).
.

KICAD_UNIT_MM = 0.00254


def kicad_to_mm(value):
    return float(value) * KICAD_UNIT_MM


def mm_to_kicad(value):
    return int(round(float(value) / KICAD_UNIT_MM)).

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsView, QGraphicsScene, QGraphicsItem
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QPointF, Signal, QTimer
from PySide6.QtGui import QColor, QWheelEvent, QMouseEvent, QKeyEvent, QPainter
from constants import VK_W, VK_A, VK_S, VK_D, Colors, Sizes, Steps
from footprint_editor.ui import overlays


class FootprintCanvas(QGraphicsView):
    zoom_changed = Signal(float)
    canvas_clicked = Signal(float, float)
    mouse_moved = Signal(float, float)
    finish_drawing = Signal()
    content_changed = Signal()
    properties_changed = Signal(object)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._panning = False
        self._pan_start = None
        self.show_grid = True
        self._setup_view()
        self._setup_scene()
        self._setup_zoom()
        self._setup_pan()
        self._reset_ghosts()
        self.setFocusPolicy(Qt.FocusPolicy.StrongFocus)

    def _setup_view(self):
        self.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.setMouseTracking(True)
        self.viewport().setMouseTracking(True)
        self.setDragMode(QGraphicsView.DragMode.NoDrag)
        self.setRubberBandSelectionMode(Qt.ItemSelectionMode.IntersectsItemShape)
        self.setViewportUpdateMode(QGraphicsView.ViewportUpdateMode.FullViewportUpdate)
        self.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)
        self.setFrameShape(QGraphicsView.Shape.Box)
        self.setFrameShadow(QGraphicsView.Shadow.Plain)
        self.setLineWidth(1)
        self.setStyleSheet("QGraphicsView { border: 1px solid #ffffff; }")

    def _setup_scene(self):
        self.scene = QGraphicsScene(self)
        self.setScene(self.scene)
        self.scene.setSceneRect(-500, -500, 1000, 1000)
        self.grid_size = float(Steps.DEFAULT_GRID)
        self.grid_color = QColor(*Colors.GRID)
        self.background_color = QColor(*Colors.BACKGROUND)
        self.setBackgroundBrush(self.background_color)

    def _setup_zoom(self):
        self.base_scale = Sizes.BASE_SCALE
        self.scale(self.base_scale, self.base_scale)
        self.zoom_steps = Steps.ZOOM
        self.zoom_index = self.zoom_steps.index(1.0)
        self.zoom_level = 1.0

    def _setup_pan(self):
        self.pressed_keys = set()
        self.pan_speed_px = Sizes.PAN_SPEED_PX
        self.pan_timer = QTimer(self)
        self.pan_timer.setInterval(Sizes.PAN_TIMER_INTERVAL)
        self.pan_timer.timeout.connect(self._pan_step)
        self._scroll_acc_x = 0.0
        self._scroll_acc_y = 0.0

    def _reset_ghosts(self):
        self._snap_pos = None
        self.show_snap = True
        self._ghost_line_start = None
        self._ghost_line_end = None
        self._ghost_circle_center = None
        self._ghost_circle_radius = 0.0
        self._ghost_arc = None
        self._ghost_text_pos = None
        self._ghost_text_content = ""
        self._ghost_text_size = 1.0
        self._ghost_text_visible = True

    def apply_theme(self):
        self.grid_color = QColor(*Colors.GRID)
        self.background_color = QColor(*Colors.BACKGROUND)
        self.setBackgroundBrush(self.background_color)
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_grid_size(self, size_mm):
        self.grid_size = size_mm
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_show_snap(self, show):
        self.show_snap = show
        self.viewport().update()

    def snap_to_grid(self, x, y):
        g = self.grid_size
        return QPointF(round(x / g) * g, round(y / g) * g)

    def set_ghost_line(self, x1, y1, x2, y2):
        self._ghost_line_start = QPointF(x1, y1)
        self._ghost_line_end = QPointF(x2, y2)
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_ghost_line(self):
        self._ghost_line_start = None
        self._ghost_line_end = None
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_ghost_circle(self, cx, cy, r):
        self._ghost_circle_center = QPointF(cx, cy)
        self._ghost_circle_radius = r
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_ghost_circle(self):
        self._ghost_circle_center = None
        self._ghost_circle_radius = 0.0
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_ghost_arc(self, cx, cy, sx, sy, angle):
        self._ghost_arc = (cx, cy, sx, sy, angle)
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_ghost_arc(self):
        self._ghost_arc = None
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_ghost_text(self, x, y, text, size, visible):
        self._ghost_text_pos = QPointF(x, y)
        self._ghost_text_content = text
        self._ghost_text_size = size
        self._ghost_text_visible = visible
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_ghost_text(self):
        self._ghost_text_pos = None
        self._ghost_text_content = ""
        self._ghost_text_size = 1.0
        self._ghost_text_visible = True
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_interaction_enabled(self, enabled):
        for item in self.scene.items():
            item.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, enabled)
        if not enabled:
            self.scene.clearSelection()

    def set_drag_mode(self, mode):
        self.setDragMode(mode)

    def drawBackground(self, painter, rect):
        super().drawBackground(painter, rect)
        if self.show_grid:
            overlays.draw_grid(painter, rect, self)

    def drawForeground(self, painter, rect):
        super().drawForeground(painter, rect)
        cross_size = overlays.draw_axes(painter, rect, self)
        if self._snap_pos and self.show_snap:
            overlays.draw_snap_marker(painter, self._snap_pos, cross_size)
        overlays.draw_ghost_line(painter, self._ghost_line_start, self._ghost_line_end)
        overlays.draw_ghost_circle(painter, self._ghost_circle_center, self._ghost_circle_radius)
        if self._ghost_arc is not None:
            overlays.draw_ghost_arc(painter, *self._ghost_arc)
        overlays.draw_ghost_text(painter, self._ghost_text_pos, self._ghost_text_content,
                                 self._ghost_text_size, self._ghost_text_visible)

    def wheelEvent(self, event: QWheelEvent):
        if event.angleDelta().y() > 0:
            if self.zoom_index < len(self.zoom_steps) - 1:
                self.zoom_index += 1
        elif self.zoom_index > 0:
            self.zoom_index -= 1

        new_zoom = self.zoom_steps[self.zoom_index]
        factor = (self.base_scale * new_zoom) / self.transform().m11()
        self.scale(factor, factor)
        self.zoom_level = new_zoom
        self.zoom_changed.emit(self.zoom_level)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.MiddleButton:
            self._panning = True
            self._pan_start = event.position().toPoint()
            self.viewport().setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.ClosedHandCursor)
            event.accept()
            return
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            items = self.items(event.position().toPoint())
            selectable_items = [
                it for it in items
                if it.flags() & QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable
            ]
            if selectable_items and (event.modifiers() & Qt.KeyboardModifier.ControlModifier):
                selectable_items[0].setSelected(not selectable_items[0].isSelected())
                event.accept()
                return
            if not selectable_items:
                scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.position().toPoint())
                snapped = self.snap_to_grid(scene_pos.x(), scene_pos.y())
                self.canvas_clicked.emit(snapped.x(), snapped.y())
        elif event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.RightButton:
            items = self.items(event.position().toPoint())
            selectable_items = [
                it for it in items
                if it.flags() & QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable
            ]
            if len(selectable_items) > 1:
                top_item = selectable_items[0]
                if top_item.isSelected():
                    top_item.setSelected(False)
                    selectable_items[1].setSelected(True)
                    event.accept()
                    return
                else:
                    top_item.setSelected(True)
                    event.accept()
                    return
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.MiddleButton and self._panning:
            self._panning = False
            self.viewport().unsetCursor()
            event.accept()
            return
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.RightButton:
            self.finish_drawing.emit()
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        if self._panning:
            current_pos = event.position().toPoint()
            delta = current_pos - self._pan_start
            self._pan_start = current_pos
            self.horizontalScrollBar().setValue(self.horizontalScrollBar().value() - delta.x())
            self.verticalScrollBar().setValue(self.verticalScrollBar().value() - delta.y())
            event.accept()
            return

        scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.position().toPoint())
        snapped = self.snap_to_grid(scene_pos.x(), scene_pos.y())
        self._snap_pos = QPointF(snapped.x(), snapped.y())
        self.mouse_moved.emit(snapped.x(), snapped.y())
        self.viewport().update()
        super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def _pan_step(self):
        dx = dy = 0
        if VK_W in self.pressed_keys:
            dy -= 1
        if VK_S in self.pressed_keys:
            dy += 1
        if VK_A in self.pressed_keys:
            dx -= 1
        if VK_D in self.pressed_keys:
            dx += 1
        if dx == 0 and dy == 0:
            return

        self._scroll_acc_x += dx * self.pan_speed_px
        self._scroll_acc_y += dy * self.pan_speed_px
        ix = int(self._scroll_acc_x)
        iy = int(self._scroll_acc_y)
        self._scroll_acc_x -= ix
        self._scroll_acc_y -= iy

        if ix or iy:
            self.horizontalScrollBar().setValue(self.horizontalScrollBar().value() + ix)
            self.verticalScrollBar().setValue(self.verticalScrollBar().value() + iy).
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (QMainWindow, QDockWidget, QWidget, QLabel, QToolBar,

class FootprintEditorWindow(
    SessionService, HistoryService, ModuleLifecycleService,
    ToolsService, GridService, StatusService, KeyboardService,
    QMainWindow,
):
    def __init__(self, footprint=None):
        super().__init__()

        self._settings = QSettings(SETTINGS_ORG, SETTINGS_APP)
        apply_theme()

        self.grid_mode = self._load_grid_mode()

        self.footprint = footprint or Footprint(name="New_Footprint")
        self.current_module_name = self.footprint.name
        self.unsaved_modules = {}
        self._modified = False

        self.library_paths = []
        self.library_modules = {}
        self.current_library_path = None

        self.current_tool = Tool.SELECT
        self.line_start_point = None
        self.circle_center_point = None
        self.arc_center_point = None
        self.arc_start_point = None
        self.text_position = None

        self.history = History()

        self._apply_startup_geometry()
        self._update_window_title()

        self.commands = CommandHandler(self)

        self._init_canvas()
        self._init_toolbar()
        self._init_menu()
        self._init_docks()
        self._init_statusbar()
        self._init_shortcuts()
        self._wire_panels()

        self.commands.ensure_reference_and_value()

        self._load_library_paths()
        self.refresh_library_list()
        self._apply_grid_series(self.grid_mode, initial=True)

        self.resizeDocks([self.library_dock], [LEFT_PANEL_MIN_WIDTH], Qt.Orientation.Horizontal)
        self.resizeDocks([self.properties_dock], [RIGHT_PANEL_MAX_WIDTH], Qt.Orientation.Horizontal)

        self._apply_theme()

        self.history.reset(self.footprint)
        self._update_history_buttons()
        self._set_tool(Tool.SELECT)

        self._restore_last_session()

    def _apply_startup_geometry(self):
        screen = QGuiApplication.primaryScreen()
        if screen is None:
            self.resize(1280, 800)
            return
        avail = screen.availableGeometry()
        self.setGeometry(avail)
        self.setWindowState(self.windowState() | Qt.WindowState.WindowMaximized)

    def _init_canvas(self):
        self.canvas = FootprintCanvas(self)
        self.canvas.zoom_changed.connect(self._update_zoom_label)
        self.canvas.canvas_clicked.connect(self.commands.on_canvas_clicked)
        self.canvas.mouse_moved.connect(self.commands.on_mouse_moved)
        self.canvas.finish_drawing.connect(self.finish_drawing)
        self.canvas.scene.selectionChanged.connect(self._on_selection_changed)
        self.canvas.content_changed.connect(self.commit_history)
        self.canvas.properties_changed.connect(self._on_properties_changed)

        self.manager = FootprintManager(self.footprint, self.canvas.scene, self.canvas)

        self.aux_bar = AuxBar()
        self.aux_bar.undo_requested.connect(self.undo)
        self.aux_bar.redo_requested.connect(self.redo)
        self.aux_bar.select_requested.connect(lambda: self._set_tool(Tool.SELECT))
        self.aux_bar.delete_requested.connect(self.commands.delete_selected)

        self.tool_bar = DrawingToolBar()
        self.tool_bar.tool_selected.connect(self._on_drawing_tool)

        central = QWidget()
        v = QVBoxLayout(central)
        v.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        v.setSpacing(0)
        v.addWidget(self.aux_bar, 0)
        v.addWidget(self.canvas, 1)
        v.addWidget(self.tool_bar, 0)
        self.setCentralWidget(central)

    def _init_toolbar(self):
        toolbar = QToolBar("Верхняя панель", self)
        toolbar.setMovable(False)
        self.addToolBar(Qt.ToolBarArea.TopToolBarArea, toolbar)
        self.toolbar = toolbar

        toolbar.addWidget(QLabel("Сетка: "))
        self.grid_series_combo = QComboBox()
        for mode, label in GRID_SERIES_LABEL.items():
            self.grid_series_combo.addItem(label, mode)
        idx = self.grid_series_combo.findData(self.grid_mode)
        if idx >= 0:
            self.grid_series_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        self.grid_series_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(
            lambda i: self._apply_grid_series(self.grid_series_combo.itemData(i))
        )
        toolbar.addWidget(self.grid_series_combo)

        toolbar.addWidget(QLabel(" Шаг: "))
        self.grid_combo = QComboBox()
        self.grid_combo.currentTextChanged.connect(self._on_grid_changed)
        toolbar.addWidget(self.grid_combo)

        toolbar.addSeparator()
        self.zoom_label = QLabel(" 100% ")
        toolbar.addWidget(self.zoom_label)

    def _init_menu(self):
        menubar = QMenuBar(self)
        menubar.setNativeMenuBar(False)
        self.setMenuBar(menubar)

        file_menu = menubar.addMenu("Файл")

        lib_menu = file_menu.addMenu("Библиотека")
        act_lib_new = QAction("Создать", self)
        act_lib_new.triggered.connect(self.commands.create_library)
        lib_menu.addAction(act_lib_new)

        act_lib_open = QAction("Открыть", self)
        act_lib_open.triggered.connect(self.commands.open_library)
        lib_menu.addAction(act_lib_open)

        act_lib_manage = QAction("Управление", self)
        act_lib_manage.triggered.connect(self.commands.manage_libraries)
        lib_menu.addAction(act_lib_manage)

        mod_menu = file_menu.addMenu("Корпус")
        act_mod_new = QAction("Создать", self)
        act_mod_new.triggered.connect(self.commands.new_module)
        mod_menu.addAction(act_mod_new)

        act_mod_open = QAction("Открыть файл...", self)
        act_mod_open.triggered.connect(self.commands.open_module_file)
        mod_menu.addAction(act_mod_open)

        act_mod_save = QAction("Сохранить", self)
        act_mod_save.setShortcut("Ctrl+S")
        act_mod_save.triggered.connect(self.commands.save_current)
        mod_menu.addAction(act_mod_save)

        act_mod_save_as = QAction("Сохранить как...", self)
        act_mod_save_as.setShortcut("Ctrl+Shift+S")
        act_mod_save_as.triggered.connect(self.commands._save_current_as)
        mod_menu.addAction(act_mod_save_as)

        file_menu.addSeparator()

        act_exit = QAction("Выход", self)
        act_exit.setShortcut("Ctrl+Q")
        act_exit.triggered.connect(self.close)
        file_menu.addAction(act_exit)

        edit_menu = menubar.addMenu("Правка")

        act_undo = QAction("Отменить", self)
        act_undo.setShortcut("Ctrl+Z")
        act_undo.triggered.connect(self.undo)
        edit_menu.addAction(act_undo)

        act_redo = QAction("Повторить", self)
        act_redo.setShortcut("Ctrl+Shift+Z")
        act_redo.triggered.connect(self.redo)
        edit_menu.addAction(act_redo)

        edit_menu.addSeparator()

        act_copy = QAction("Копировать", self)
        act_copy.setShortcut("Ctrl+C")
        act_copy.triggered.connect(self.commands.copy_selected)
        edit_menu.addAction(act_copy)

        act_paste = QAction("Вставить", self)
        act_paste.setShortcut("Ctrl+V")
        act_paste.triggered.connect(self.commands.paste)
        edit_menu.addAction(act_paste)

        act_delete = QAction("Удалить", self)
        act_delete.setShortcut("Del")
        act_delete.triggered.connect(self.commands.delete_selected)
        edit_menu.addAction(act_delete)

        edit_menu.addSeparator()

        act_rotate = QAction("Повернуть на 90°", self)
        act_rotate.setShortcut("R")
        act_rotate.triggered.connect(self.commands.rotate_selected)
        edit_menu.addAction(act_rotate)

        edit_menu.addSeparator()

        act_select_all = QAction("Выделить всё", self)
        act_select_all.setShortcut("Ctrl+A")
        act_select_all.triggered.connect(self._select_all)
        edit_menu.addAction(act_select_all)

        view_menu = menubar.addMenu("Вид")

        self.act_show_grid = QAction("Показывать сетку", self, checkable=True)
        self.act_show_grid.setChecked(True)
        self.act_show_grid.setShortcut("G")
        self.act_show_grid.triggered.connect(self._toggle_grid)
        view_menu.addAction(self.act_show_grid)

        grid_menu = view_menu.addMenu("Шаг сетки")
        self._grid_group = QActionGroup(self)
        self._grid_group.setExclusive(True)
        self._grid_actions = {}
        for mode, label in GRID_SERIES_LABEL.items():
            act = QAction(label, self, checkable=True)
            act.setChecked(mode == self.grid_mode)
            act.triggered.connect(lambda _=False, m=mode: self._apply_grid_series(m))
            self._grid_group.addAction(act)
            grid_menu.addAction(act)
            self._grid_actions[mode] = act

        act_full = QAction("Полный экран", self)
        act_full.setShortcut("F11")
        act_full.triggered.connect(self.toggle_fullscreen)
        view_menu.addSeparator()
        view_menu.addAction(act_full)

    def _toggle_grid(self):
        self.canvas.show_grid = self.act_show_grid.isChecked()
        self.canvas.viewport().update()

    def _select_all(self):
        for item in self.canvas.scene.items():
            if hasattr(item, 'model') and not getattr(item.model, 'locked', False):
                item.setSelected(True)

    def _init_docks(self):
        self.properties_dock = QDockWidget("", self)
        self.properties_dock.setAllowedAreas(Qt.LeftDockWidgetArea | Qt.RightDockWidgetArea)
        self.right_panel = RightPanel()
        self.properties_dock.setWidget(self.right_panel)
        self.addDockWidget(Qt.RightDockWidgetArea, self.properties_dock)
        self._hide_dock_title(self.properties_dock)

        self.library_dock = QDockWidget("", self)
        self.library_dock.setAllowedAreas(Qt.LeftDockWidgetArea | Qt.RightDockWidgetArea)
        self.library_panel = LibraryPanel()
        self.library_dock.setWidget(self.library_panel)
        self.addDockWidget(Qt.LeftDockWidgetArea, self.library_dock)
        self._hide_dock_title(self.library_dock)

    def _init_statusbar(self):
        sb = QStatusBar()
        self.setStatusBar(sb)
        self.log_label = QLabel("Готово.")
        sb.addWidget(self.log_label)
        self.coords_label = QLabel("X: 0.0000, Y: 0.0000")
        sb.addPermanentWidget(self.coords_label)

    def _init_shortcuts(self):
        self.key_filter = GlobalKeyFilter(self)
        QApplication.instance().installEventFilter(self.key_filter)

    def _wire_panels(self):
        rp = self.right_panel
        rp.properties_panel.btn_apply.clicked.connect(self.commit_history)

        lp = self.library_panel
        lp.add_library_requested.connect(self.commands.add_library)
        lp.add_module_requested.connect(self.commands.new_module)
        lp.library_selected.connect(self.commands.select_library)
        lp.module_selected.connect(self.commands.select_module)
        lp.module_rename_requested.connect(self.commands.rename_module)
        lp.module_delete_requested.connect(self.commands.delete_module)

    def _hide_dock_title(self, dock):
        empty = QWidget()
        empty.setFixedHeight(0)
        dock.setTitleBarWidget(empty)

    def _apply_theme(self):
        app = QApplication.instance()
        app.setStyleSheet(Styles.TOOLTIP)
        self.setStyleSheet(Styles.MAIN_WINDOW)
        self.canvas.apply_theme()
        self.right_panel.setStyleSheet(Styles.RIGHT_PANEL)
        self.right_panel.apply_theme()
        self.library_panel.apply_theme()
        self.tool_bar.apply_theme()
        self.aux_bar.apply_theme()
        self.log_label.setStyleSheet(Styles.LOG_LABEL)
        self.canvas.scene.update()
        self.canvas.viewport().update()

    def _on_selection_changed(self):
        from footprint_editor.graphics.pad_item import PadGraphicsItem
        from footprint_editor.graphics.line_item import LineGraphicsItem
        from footprint_editor.graphics.circle_item import CircleGraphicsItem
        from footprint_editor.graphics.arc_item import ArcGraphicsItem
        from footprint_editor.graphics.text_item import TextGraphicsItem

        items = self.canvas.scene.selectedItems()
        has_sel = len(items) > 0
        self.aux_bar.set_delete_enabled(has_sel)

        if len(items) == 1:
            item = items[0]
            if isinstance(item, (PadGraphicsItem, LineGraphicsItem, CircleGraphicsItem,
                                 ArcGraphicsItem, TextGraphicsItem)):
                if getattr(item.model, 'locked', False):
                    self.aux_bar.set_delete_enabled(False)
                self.right_panel.properties_panel.set_object(item.model)
            else:
                self.right_panel.properties_panel.clear_panel()
        else:
            self.right_panel.properties_panel.clear_panel()

    def _on_properties_changed(self, model):
        panel = self.right_panel.properties_panel
        if panel.current_object == model:
            panel.refresh_values()

    def toggle_fullscreen(self):
        if self.isFullScreen():
            self.showNormal()
            self.setWindowState(self.windowState() | Qt.WindowState.WindowMaximized)
        else:
            self.showFullScreen()

    def refresh_library_list(self):
        self.library_panel.set_libraries(self.library_paths)
        self.library_panel.highlight_library(self.current_library_path)

    def save_library_paths(self):
        self._save_library_paths()

    def reload_current_library(self):
        self.commands.reload_current_library().
.

from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QPointF
from PySide6.QtGui import QPen, QColor, QPainterPath, QBrush
from constants import Colors, Sizes
from footprint_editor.graphics.text_path import build_text_path
from footprint_editor.core.arc_geometry import arc_sample_points


MAX_GRID_POINTS_VISUAL = 60


def _effective_step(span, grid_size, max_points):
    if span <= 0 or grid_size <= 0:
        return grid_size
    if span / grid_size <= max_points:
        return grid_size
    factor = 1
    while span / (grid_size * factor) > max_points:
        factor *= 2
    return grid_size * factor


def draw_grid(painter, rect, view):
    grid_size = view.grid_size
    scale = view.transform().m11()
    if grid_size * scale < Sizes.MIN_CELL_PX:
        return

    left = int(rect.left() // grid_size) * grid_size
    right = int(rect.right() // grid_size) * grid_size
    top = int(rect.top() // grid_size) * grid_size
    bottom = int(rect.bottom() // grid_size) * grid_size

    step_x = _effective_step(right - left, grid_size, MAX_GRID_POINTS_VISUAL)
    step_y = _effective_step(bottom - top, grid_size, MAX_GRID_POINTS_VISUAL)

    pen = QPen(view.grid_color, 0)
    pen.setCosmetic(True)
    painter.setPen(pen)

    y = top
    while y <= bottom:
        x = left
        while x <= right:
            painter.drawPoint(QPointF(x, y))
            x += step_x
        y += step_y


def draw_axes(painter, rect, view):
    pen = QPen(QColor(*Colors.AXIS), 0)
    pen.setCosmetic(True)
    painter.setPen(pen)
    painter.drawLine(QPointF(rect.left(), 0), QPointF(rect.right(), 0))
    painter.drawLine(QPointF(0, rect.top()), QPointF(0, rect.bottom()))
    return Sizes.CROSS_SIZE / view.transform().m11()


def draw_snap_marker(painter, pos, cross_size):
    pen = QPen(QColor(*Colors.SNAP_CROSS), 0)
    pen.setCosmetic(True)
    painter.setPen(pen)
    x, y = pos.x(), pos.y()
    painter.drawLine(QPointF(x - cross_size, y), QPointF(x + cross_size, y))
    painter.drawLine(QPointF(x, y - cross_size), QPointF(x, y + cross_size))


def draw_ghost_line(painter, start, end):
    if not start or not end:
        return
    pen = QPen(QColor(*Colors.GHOST_LINE), 0)
    pen.setCosmetic(True)
    pen.setStyle(Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)
    painter.setPen(pen)
    painter.drawLine(start, end)


def draw_ghost_circle(painter, center, radius):
    if not center or radius <= 0:
        return
    pen = QPen(QColor(*Colors.GHOST_LINE), 0)
    pen.setCosmetic(True)
    pen.setStyle(Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)
    painter.setPen(pen)
    painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
    painter.drawEllipse(center, radius, radius)


def draw_ghost_arc(painter, cx, cy, sx, sy, angle):
    if cx is None or sx is None:
        return
    pts = arc_sample_points(cx, cy, sx, sy, angle, 64)
    if len(pts) < 2:
        return
    path = QPainterPath()
    path.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1])
    for px, py in pts[1:]:
        path.lineTo(px, py)

    pen = QPen(QColor(*Colors.GHOST_LINE), 0)
    pen.setCosmetic(True)
    pen.setStyle(Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)
    painter.setPen(pen)
    painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
    painter.drawPath(path)


def draw_ghost_text(painter, pos, text, size, visible):
    if not pos or not text:
        return
    path = build_text_path(text, size * Sizes.TEXT_FONT_MULTIPLIER, center=True)
    if path.isEmpty():
        return
    color = QColor(*Colors.GHOST_TEXT)
    if not visible:
        color = color.lighter(50)
    painter.save()
    painter.translate(pos)
    painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
    painter.setBrush(color)
    painter.drawPath(path)
    painter.restore().
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication, QLineEdit, QDoubleSpinBox
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, QEvent, Qt


class GlobalKeyFilter(QObject):
    def __init__(self, window):
        super().__init__()
        self.window = window

    def eventFilter(self, obj, event):
        if event.type() in (QEvent.Type.KeyPress, QEvent.Type.KeyRelease):
            if event.type() == QEvent.Type.KeyPress and event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_Escape:
                return self.window.handle_global_key(event)

            fw = QApplication.focusWidget()
            if isinstance(fw, (QLineEdit, QDoubleSpinBox)):
                return super().eventFilter(obj, event)

            return self.window.handle_global_key(event)
        return super().eventFilter(obj, event).
.

import math
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QDialog
from constants import Tool, Sizes
from footprint_editor.core.models import Pad, PadShape, PadType, Line, Circle, Arc, Text
from footprint_editor.ui.dialogs.text_dialog import TextDialog
from footprint_editor.ui.dialogs.arc_dialog import ArcDialog


class DrawingCommandsMixin:
    def on_canvas_clicked(self, x, y):
        tool = self.w.current_tool
        {
            Tool.ADD_PAD: self.add_pad,
            Tool.ADD_LINE: self.add_line_point,
            Tool.ADD_CIRCLE: self.add_circle_point,
            Tool.ADD_ARC: self.add_arc_point,
            Tool.ADD_TEXT: self.add_text_at,
        }.get(tool, lambda *_: None)(x, y)

    def on_mouse_moved(self, x, y):
        from PySide6.QtCore import QPointF
        self._current_mouse_pos = QPointF(x, y)
        self.w.coords_label.setText(f"X: {x:.4f}, Y: {y:.4f}")

        tool = self.w.current_tool
        canvas = self.w.canvas
        grid = canvas.grid_size

        if tool == Tool.ADD_LINE and self.w.line_start_point:
            sx, sy = self.w.line_start_point
            canvas.set_ghost_line(sx, sy, x, y)
        elif tool == Tool.ADD_CIRCLE and self.w.circle_center_point:
            cx, cy = self.w.circle_center_point
            r = round(math.hypot(x - cx, y - cy) / grid) * grid
            canvas.set_ghost_circle(cx, cy, r)
        elif tool == Tool.ADD_ARC and self.w.arc_center_point and self.w.arc_start_point is None:
            cx, cy = self.w.arc_center_point
            canvas.set_ghost_line(cx, cy, x, y)
        else:
            canvas.clear_ghost_line()
            canvas.clear_ghost_circle()

    def add_pad(self, x, y):
        num = self.w.manager.get_next_pad_number()
        pad = Pad(number=num, shape=PadShape.CIRCLE, pad_type=PadType.STANDARD,
                  x=x, y=y, width=1.5, height=1.5, drill=0.8)
        self.w.footprint.pads.append(pad)
        self.w.manager.add_model(pad)
        self.w.canvas.set_interaction_enabled(False)
        self.w.commit_history()
        self.w.show_log(f'площадка #{num} установлена')

    def add_line_point(self, x, y):
        if self.w.line_start_point is None:
            self.w.line_start_point = (x, y)
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(x, y, x, y)
            return
        sx, sy = self.w.line_start_point
        line = Line(sx, sy, x, y, width=Sizes.DEFAULT_LINE_WIDTH)
        self.w.footprint.lines.append(line)
        self.w.manager.add_model(line)
        self.w.canvas.set_interaction_enabled(False)
        self.w.line_start_point = (x, y)
        self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(x, y, x, y)
        self.w.commit_history()
        self.w.show_log('линия установлена')

    def add_circle_point(self, x, y):
        if self.w.circle_center_point is None:
            self.w.circle_center_point = (x, y)
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_circle(x, y, 0.0)
            return
        cx, cy = self.w.circle_center_point
        grid = self.w.canvas.grid_size
        r = round(math.hypot(x - cx, y - cy) / grid) * grid
        if r > 0:
            circle = Circle(cx, cy, r, width=Sizes.DEFAULT_LINE_WIDTH)
            self.w.footprint.circles.append(circle)
            self.w.manager.add_model(circle)
            self.w.canvas.set_interaction_enabled(False)
            self.w.commit_history()
            self.w.show_log('окружность установлена')
        self.w.circle_center_point = None
        self.w.canvas.clear_ghost_circle()

    def add_arc_point(self, x, y):
        if self.w.arc_center_point is None:
            self.w.arc_center_point = (x, y)
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(x, y, x, y)
            return
        if self.w.arc_start_point is None:
            self.w.arc_start_point = (x, y)
            cx, cy = self.w.arc_center_point
            self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(cx, cy, x, y)
            self._show_arc_dialog()
            return
        self.w.arc_center_point = (x, y)
        self.w.arc_start_point = None
        self.w.canvas.set_ghost_line(x, y, x, y)

    def _show_arc_dialog(self):
        cx, cy = self.w.arc_center_point
        sx, sy = self.w.arc_start_point

        dlg = ArcDialog(self.w)
        dlg.angle_changed.connect(
            lambda a: self.w.canvas.set_ghost_arc(cx, cy, sx, sy, a)
        )
        self.w.canvas.set_ghost_arc(cx, cy, sx, sy, dlg.get_angle())

        if dlg.exec() == QDialog.Accepted:
            arc = Arc(cx=cx, cy=cy, sx=sx, sy=sy,
                      angle=dlg.get_angle(), width=Sizes.DEFAULT_LINE_WIDTH)
            self.w.footprint.arcs.append(arc)
            self.w.manager.add_model(arc)
            self.w.canvas.set_interaction_enabled(False)
            self.w.commit_history()
            self.w.show_log('дуга установлена')

        self.w.arc_center_point = None
        self.w.arc_start_point = None
        self.w.canvas.clear_ghost_arc()

    def add_text_at(self, x, y):
        self.w.text_position = (x, y)
        self.w.canvas.set_ghost_text(x, y, "", 1.0, True)

        dialog = TextDialog(self.w)
        dialog.text_changed.connect(
            lambda t: self.w.canvas.set_ghost_text(*self.w.text_position, t,
                                                    dialog.get_size(), dialog.get_visible()))
        dialog.size_changed.connect(
            lambda s: self.w.canvas.set_ghost_text(*self.w.text_position, dialog.get_text(),
                                                    s, dialog.get_visible()))
        dialog.visible_changed.connect(
            lambda v: self.w.canvas.set_ghost_text(*self.w.text_position, dialog.get_text(),
                                                    dialog.get_size(), v))

        if dialog.exec() == QDialog.Accepted:
            text_value = dialog.get_text() or "Text"
            tx, ty = self.w.text_position
            t = Text(tx, ty, text_value, dialog.get_size(), dialog.get_visible())
            self.w.footprint.texts.append(t)
            self.w.manager.add_model(t)
            self.w.canvas.set_interaction_enabled(False)
            self.w.commit_history()
            self.w.show_log('текст установлен')

        self.w.text_position = None
        self.w.canvas.clear_ghost_text().
.
============================================================
FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\footprint_editor\ui\commands\editing.py

import copy
from footprint_editor.core.models import new_uid
from footprint_editor.graphics.pad_item import PadGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.graphics.line_item import LineGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.graphics.circle_item import CircleGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.graphics.arc_item import ArcGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.graphics.text_item import TextGraphicsItem
from footprint_editor.ui.commands._shared import (
    model_center, translate_model, append_model,
    model_bounds, rotate_model, remove_from_footprint,
)


MODEL_ITEMS = (PadGraphicsItem, LineGraphicsItem, CircleGraphicsItem,
               ArcGraphicsItem, TextGraphicsItem)


class EditingCommandsMixin:
    def _selected_model_items(self):
        return [it for it in self.w.canvas.scene.selectedItems()
                if isinstance(it, MODEL_ITEMS)]

    def delete_selected(self):
        deleted_models = []
        for item in list(self.w.canvas.scene.selectedItems()):
            if not isinstance(item, MODEL_ITEMS):
                continue
            if getattr(item.model, 'locked', False):
                continue
            item.setSelected(False)
            deleted_models.append(item.model)

        for model in deleted_models:
            remove_from_footprint(self.w.footprint, model)
            self.w.manager.remove_model(model)

        self.w.canvas.scene.clearSelection()
        self.w.aux_bar.set_delete_enabled(False)
        self.w.right_panel.properties_panel.clear_panel()
        self.w.canvas.scene.update()
        self.w.canvas.viewport().update()

        if deleted_models:
            self.w.commit_history()
        self.w.show_log("элементы удалены")

    def copy_selected(self):
        self.clipboard = []
        for item in self.w.canvas.scene.selectedItems():
            if isinstance(item, MODEL_ITEMS) and not getattr(item.model, 'locked', False):
                self.clipboard.append(copy.deepcopy(item.model))
        if self.clipboard:
            self.w.show_log("(буфер обмена) элементы скопированы")

    def paste(self):
        if not self.clipboard:
            return
        self.w.canvas.scene.clearSelection()

        cx, cy = self._clipboard_center()
        dx = self._current_mouse_pos.x() - cx
        dy = self._current_mouse_pos.y() - cy
        grid = self.w.canvas.grid_size
        dx = round(dx / grid) * grid
        dy = round(dy / grid) * grid

        for model in self.clipboard:
            new_model = copy.deepcopy(model)
            new_model.uid = new_uid()
            new_model.locked = False
            translate_model(new_model, dx, dy)
            append_model(self.w.footprint, new_model)
            item = self.w.manager.add_model(new_model)
            item.setSelected(True)

        self.w.commit_history()
        self.w.show_log("(буфер обмена) элементы установлены")

    def _clipboard_center(self):
        sx = sy = 0.0
        n = 0
        for m in self.clipboard:
            px, py = model_center(m)
            sx += px
            sy += py
            n += 1
        return (sx / n, sy / n) if n else (0, 0)

    def rotate_selected(self):
        items = self._selected_model_items()
        if not items:
            return

        min_x = min_y = float('inf')
        max_x = max_y = float('-inf')
        for item in items:
            for px, py in model_bounds(item.model):
                min_x = min(min_x, px)
                min_y = min(min_y, py)
                max_x = max(max_x, px)
                max_y = max(max_y, py)

        cx = (min_x + max_x) / 2
        cy = (min_y + max_y) / 2

        for item in items:
            rotate_model(item.model, cx, cy)
            if isinstance(item, PadGraphicsItem):
                item.setPos(item.model.x, item.model.y)
            item.update_geometry()

        self.w.commit_history()
        self.w.show_log("элементы повёрнуты на 90°").
.
