import copy
import re
from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QDialog, QFileDialog, QMessageBox

from footprint_editor.kicad.importer import parse_kicad_mod
from footprint_editor.ui.dialogs.libraries_dialog import LibrariesManagerDialog
from footprint_editor.ui.dialogs.input_dialog import InputDialog
from footprint_editor.ui.dialogs.custom_question_dialog import CustomQuestionDialog


class LibrariesMixin:
    def add_library(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self.w, "Добавить библиотеку KiCad", "",
            "KiCad Library (*.mod);;All Files (*)"
        )
        if not path:
            return
        if path in self.w.library_paths:
            QMessageBox.information(self.w, "Информация", "Эта библиотека уже добавлена.")
            return
        self.w.library_paths.append(path)
        self.w.save_library_paths()
        self.w.refresh_library_list()
        self.w.show_log(f"Библиотека добавлена: {Path(path).name}")

    def open_library(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self.w, "Открыть библиотеку KiCad", "",
            "KiCad Library (*.mod);;All Files (*)"
        )
        if path:
            if path not in self.w.library_paths:
                self.w.library_paths.append(path)
                self.w.save_library_paths()
                self.w.refresh_library_list()
            self.select_library(path)

    def create_library(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self.w, "Создать библиотеку", "",
            "KiCad Library (*.mod);;All Files (*)"
        )
        if not path:
            return
        if not path.lower().endswith('.mod'):
            path += '.mod'
        Path(path).write_text(
            "PCBNEW-LibModule-V1\n# encoding utf-8\n$INDEX\n$EndINDEX\n$EndLIBRARY\n",
            encoding='utf-8'
        )
        if path not in self.w.library_paths:
            self.w.library_paths.append(path)
            self.w.save_library_paths()
            self.w.refresh_library_list()
        self.select_library(path)
        self.w.show_log(f"Библиотека создана: {Path(path).name}")

    def manage_libraries(self):
        dlg = LibrariesManagerDialog(self.w.library_paths, self.w)
        if dlg.exec() != QDialog.Accepted:
            return
        new_paths = dlg.get_library_paths()
        if new_paths == self.w.library_paths:
            return
        self.w.library_paths = new_paths
        if self.w.current_library_path not in self.w.library_paths:
            self.w.current_library_path = None
            self.w.library_panel.set_modules([])
        self.w.save_library_paths()
        self.w.refresh_library_list()

    def select_library(self, path):
        if path not in self.w.library_modules:
            try:
                footprints = parse_kicad_mod(path)
                self.w.library_modules[path] = {fp.name: fp for fp in footprints}
            except Exception as e:
                self.w.show_log(f"Ошибка загрузки библиотеки: {e}")
                return

        self.w.current_library_path = path
        names = list(self.w.library_modules[path].keys())

        self.w.library_panel.set_modules(names)
        self.w.library_panel.highlight_library(path)

        self.w.refresh_module_list_display()
        self.w._update_window_title()
        self.w.show_log(f"Библиотека: {Path(path).name}")

    def select_module(self, name):
        if name == self.w.current_module_name:
            return
        self.w.stash_current_module()

        if name in self.w.unsaved_modules:
            self.w.load_footprint(self.w.unsaved_modules[name], mark_unsaved=True)
            return

        if self.w.current_library_path and self.w.current_library_path in self.w.library_modules:
            module = self.w.library_modules[self.w.current_library_path].get(name)
            if module:
                self.w.load_footprint(copy.deepcopy(module))

    def reload_current_library(self):
        if not self.w.current_library_path:
            return
        try:
            footprints = parse_kicad_mod(self.w.current_library_path)
            self.w.library_modules[self.w.current_library_path] = {fp.name: fp for fp in footprints}
            names = list(self.w.library_modules[self.w.current_library_path].keys())
            self.w.library_panel.set_modules(names)
            self.w.refresh_module_list_display()
        except Exception as e:
            self.w.show_log(f"Ошибка перезагрузки: {e}")

    def rename_module(self, old_name):
        dlg = InputDialog(self.w, title="Переименовать корпус", label="Новое имя:", initial=old_name)
        if dlg.exec() != QDialog.Accepted:
            return
        new_name = dlg.get_text().strip()
        if not new_name or new_name == old_name:
            return

        lib_path = self.w.current_library_path
        if not lib_path or lib_path not in self.w.library_modules:
            return

        lib_data = self.w.library_modules[lib_path]
        if new_name in lib_data:
            QMessageBox.warning(self.w, "Ошибка", "Корпус с таким именем уже существует.")
            return

        is_current = (self.w.current_module_name == old_name)
        if is_current:
            self.w.footprint.name = new_name

        if old_name in self.w.unsaved_modules:
            self.w.unsaved_modules[new_name] = self.w.unsaved_modules.pop(old_name)
            self.w.unsaved_modules[new_name].name = new_name

        self._remove_module_from_library_file(lib_path, old_name)
        
        if is_current:
            self._replace_module_in_library(lib_path, new_name)
        else:
            module = lib_data.get(old_name)
            if module:
                module.name = new_name
                old_text = build_kicad_module_text(module)
                content = Path(lib_path).read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore')
                end_marker = "$EndLIBRARY"
                if end_marker in content:
                    idx = content.rfind(end_marker)
                    content = content[:idx] + old_text + "\n" + end_marker + "\n"
                    Path(lib_path).write_text(content, encoding='utf-8')

        if old_name in lib_data:
            lib_data[new_name] = lib_data.pop(old_name)
            lib_data[new_name].name = new_name

        if is_current:
            self.w.current_module_name = new_name
            self.w._update_window_title()

        self.w.reload_current_library()
        self.w.refresh_module_list_display()

    def delete_module(self, name):
        lib_path = self.w.current_library_path
        if not lib_path or lib_path not in self.w.library_modules:
            return

        dlg = CustomQuestionDialog(
            self.w, 
            title="Удаление корпуса", 
            message=f"Вы уверены, что хотите удалить корпус '{name}'?"
        )
        result = dlg.exec()
        if result != CustomQuestionDialog.ResultSave:
            return

        if name in self.w.unsaved_modules:
            del self.w.unsaved_modules[name]

        self._remove_module_from_library_file(lib_path, name)

        if name in self.w.library_modules[lib_path]:
            del self.w.library_modules[lib_path][name]

        if self.w.current_module_name == name:
            if self.w.library_modules[lib_path]:
                new_name = list(self.w.library_modules[lib_path].keys())[0]
                self.select_module(new_name)
            else:
                from footprint_editor.core.models import Footprint
                self.w.load_footprint(Footprint(name="Empty"))
                self.w.current_module_name = "Empty"
                self.w._update_window_title()

        self.w.reload_current_library()
        self.w.refresh_module_list_display()
        self.w.show_log(f"Корпус удалён: {name}")

    def _remove_module_from_library_file(self, path, module_name):
        content = Path(path).read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore')
        pattern = re.compile(
            r'\$MODULE\s+' + re.escape(module_name) + r'\b.*?\$EndMODULE[^\n]*\n',
            re.DOTALL
        )
        content = pattern.sub('', content)
        Path(path).write_text(content, encoding='utf-8').
.

import copy
import re
from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QDialog, QFileDialog, QMessageBox

from footprint_editor.core.models import Footprint, Text, TextType
from footprint_editor.kicad.importer import parse_kicad_mod
from footprint_editor.kicad.exporter import build_kicad_module_text
from footprint_editor.ui.dialogs.input_dialog import InputDialog


class ModuleFilesMixin:
    def ensure_reference_and_value(self):
        fp = self.w.footprint
        has_ref = any(t.text_type == TextType.REFERENCE for t in fp.texts)
        has_val = any(t.text_type == TextType.VALUE for t in fp.texts)
        if not has_ref:
            ref = Text(x=0, y=0, text=fp.reference, size=1.0, visible=True, text_type=TextType.REFERENCE, locked=True)
            fp.texts.append(ref)
            self.w.manager.add_model(ref)
        if not has_val:
            val = Text(x=0, y=1, text=fp.value, size=1.0, visible=True, text_type=TextType.VALUE, locked=True)
            fp.texts.append(val)
            self.w.manager.add_model(val)

    def new_module(self):
        name = self.w.get_next_module_name()
        dlg = InputDialog(self.w, title="Новый корпус", label="Имя корпуса:", initial=name)
        if dlg.exec() != QDialog.Accepted:
            return
        name = dlg.get_text().strip()
        if not name:
            return

        self.w.stash_current_module()
        
        new_fp = Footprint(name=name, reference=name, value="VAL**")
        self.w.load_footprint(new_fp, mark_unsaved=False)
        
        for t in self.w.footprint.texts:
            if t.text_type == TextType.REFERENCE:
                t.text = name
                if t.graphics_item:
                    t.graphics_item.prepareGeometryChange()
                    t.graphics_item.update()
                break
                
        if self.w.current_library_path:
            try:
                self._replace_module_in_library(self.w.current_library_path, name)
                self.w.reload_current_library()
                self.w.show_log(f"Создан корпус: {name}")
            except Exception as e:
                self.w.show_log(f"Ошибка сохранения: {e}")
        else:
            self.w.show_log(f"Создан корпус: {name}")

    def open_module_file(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self.w, "Открыть корпус", "",
            "KiCad Module (*.mod);;All Files (*)"
        )
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            footprints = parse_kicad_mod(path)
            if not footprints:
                QMessageBox.warning(self.w, "Пусто", "В файле нет модулей.")
                return
            self.w.stash_current_module()
            self.w.load_footprint(copy.deepcopy(footprints[0]))
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self.w, "Ошибка", f"Не удалось открыть файл: {e}")

    def save_current(self):
        if not self.w.current_library_path:
            self._save_current_as()
            return
        try:
            self._replace_module_in_library(
                self.w.current_library_path, self.w.current_module_name
            )
            self.w.reload_current_library()
            self.w.unsaved_modules.pop(self.w.current_module_name, None)
            self.w.mark_saved()
            self.w.show_log(f"Модуль сохранён: {self.w.current_module_name}")
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self.w, "Ошибка", f"Не удалось сохранить: {e}")

    def _save_current_as(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self.w, "Сохранить модуль", "",
            "KiCad Module (*.mod);;All Files (*)"
        )
        if not path:
            return
        if not path.lower().endswith('.mod'):
            path += '.mod'
        self.w.export_to_kicad(path)
        self.w.unsaved_modules.pop(self.w.current_module_name, None)
        self.w.mark_saved()
        self.w.show_log(f"Модуль сохранён: {Path(path).name}")

    def _replace_module_in_library(self, path, module_name):
        content = Path(path).read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore')
        pattern = re.compile(
            r'\$MODULE\s+' + re.escape(module_name) + r'\b.*?\$EndMODULE[^\n]*\n',
            re.DOTALL
        )
        content = pattern.sub('', content)
        module_text = build_kicad_module_text(self.w.footprint)
        end_marker = "$EndLIBRARY"
        if end_marker in content:
            idx = content.rfind(end_marker)
            content = content[:idx] + module_text + "\n" + end_marker + "\n"
        else:
            content = content.rstrip() + "\n" + module_text + "\n" + end_marker + "\n"
        Path(path).write_text(content, encoding='utf-8').
.

from footprint_editor.core.models import Pad, Line, Circle, Arc, Text


def rotate_point_90(x, y, cx, cy):
    dx, dy = x - cx, y - cy
    return cx - dy, cy + dx


def model_center(m):
    if isinstance(m, Pad):
        return m.x, m.y
    if isinstance(m, Line):
        return (m.x + m.x2) / 2, (m.y + m.y2) / 2
    if isinstance(m, Circle):
        return m.x, m.y
    if isinstance(m, Arc):
        return m.x, m.y
    if isinstance(m, Text):
        return m.x, m.y
    return 0, 0


def translate_model(m, dx, dy):
    if isinstance(m, Pad):
        m.x += dx
        m.y += dy
    elif isinstance(m, Line):
        m.x += dx
        m.y += dy
        m.x2 += dx
        m.y2 += dy
    elif isinstance(m, Circle):
        m.x += dx
        m.y += dy
    elif isinstance(m, Arc):
        m.x += dx
        m.y += dy
        m.sx += dx
        m.sy += dy
    elif isinstance(m, Text):
        m.x += dx
        m.y += dy


def append_model(fp, m):
    if isinstance(m, Pad):
        fp.pads.append(m)
    elif isinstance(m, Line):
        fp.lines.append(m)
    elif isinstance(m, Circle):
        fp.circles.append(m)
    elif isinstance(m, Arc):
        fp.arcs.append(m)
    elif isinstance(m, Text):
        fp.texts.append(m)


def model_bounds(m):
    from footprint_editor.core.arc_geometry import arc_sample_points
    if isinstance(m, Pad):
        return [(m.x - m.width / 2, m.y - m.height / 2),
                (m.x + m.width / 2, m.y + m.height / 2)]
    if isinstance(m, Line):
        return [(m.x, m.y), (m.x2, m.y2)]
    if isinstance(m, Circle):
        return [(m.x - m.radius, m.y - m.radius),
                (m.x + m.radius, m.y + m.radius)]
    if isinstance(m, Arc):
        return arc_sample_points(m.x, m.y, m.sx, m.sy, m.angle, 16)
    if isinstance(m, Text):
        return [(m.x, m.y)]
    return []


def rotate_model(m, cx, cy):
    from footprint_editor.core.models import PadShape
    if isinstance(m, Pad):
        m.x, m.y = rotate_point_90(m.x, m.y, cx, cy)
        if m.shape in (PadShape.RECT, PadShape.OVAL):
            m.width, m.height = m.height, m.width
    elif isinstance(m, Line):
        m.x, m.y = rotate_point_90(m.x, m.y, cx, cy)
        m.x2, m.y2 = rotate_point_90(m.x2, m.y2, cx, cy)
    elif isinstance(m, Circle):
        m.x, m.y = rotate_point_90(m.x, m.y, cx, cy)
    elif isinstance(m, Arc):
        m.x, m.y = rotate_point_90(m.x, m.y, cx, cy)
        m.sx, m.sy = rotate_point_90(m.sx, m.sy, cx, cy)
    elif isinstance(m, Text):
        m.x, m.y = rotate_point_90(m.x, m.y, cx, cy)


def remove_from_footprint(fp, model):
    for bucket in (fp.pads, fp.lines, fp.circles, fp.arcs, fp.texts):
        if model in bucket:
            bucket.remove(model)
            return.
.

from footprint_editor.ui.commands.drawing import DrawingCommandsMixin
from footprint_editor.ui.commands.editing import EditingCommandsMixin
from footprint_editor.ui.commands.module_files import ModuleFilesMixin
from footprint_editor.ui.commands.libraries import LibrariesMixin


class CommandHandler(DrawingCommandsMixin, EditingCommandsMixin,
                     ModuleFilesMixin, LibrariesMixin):
    def __init__(self, window):
        from PySide6.QtCore import QPointF
        self.w = window
        self.clipboard = []
        self._current_mouse_pos = QPointF(0, 0).
.

FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\footprint_editor\ui\dialogs\arc_dialog.py

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
                               QLineEdit, QPushButton)
from PySide6.QtGui import QDoubleValidator
from PySide6.QtCore import Signal
from constants import Styles
from footprint_editor.ui.dialogs.headerless_dialog import HeaderlessDialog


class ArcDialog(HeaderlessDialog):
    angle_changed = Signal(float)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent, title="Дуга")
        self.setMinimumWidth(320)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 14, 14, 14)
        layout.setSpacing(10)

        header = QLabel("Дуга")
        header.setStyleSheet(Styles.OBJECT_TITLE)
        layout.addWidget(header)

        row = QHBoxLayout()
        row.addWidget(QLabel("Угол (°):"))
        self.angle_edit = QLineEdit("90.0")
        validator = QDoubleValidator(-359.0, 359.0, 1, self.angle_edit)
        validator.setNotation(QDoubleValidator.Notation.StandardNotation)
        self.angle_edit.setValidator(validator)
        self.angle_edit.setStyleSheet(Styles.INPUT_BG)
        self.angle_edit.setMinimumWidth(140)
        self.angle_edit.setMinimumHeight(28)
        self.angle_edit.textChanged.connect(self._on_angle_text_changed)
        row.addWidget(self.angle_edit, 1)
        layout.addLayout(row)

        btns = QHBoxLayout()
        btns.addStretch()
        self.btn_ok = QPushButton("OK")
        self.btn_ok.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_OK)
        self.btn_ok.clicked.connect(self.accept)

        self.btn_cancel = QPushButton("Отмена")
        self.btn_cancel.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_CANCEL)
        self.btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)

        btns.addWidget(self.btn_ok)
        btns.addWidget(self.btn_cancel)
        layout.addLayout(btns)

    def _on_angle_text_changed(self, text):
        try:

        except ValueError:

        self.angle_changed.emit(value)

            return float(self.angle_edit.text().replace(',', '.'))

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QDialog
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from constants import Styles


class HeaderlessDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, title=""):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle(title)
        self.setWindowFlags(Qt.Dialog | Qt.FramelessWindowHint)
        self.setStyleSheet(Styles.DIALOG_BG)
        self._drag_pos = None

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            self._drag_pos = event.globalPosition().toPoint() - self.frameGeometry().topLeft()
            event.accept()
        else:
            super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        if self._drag_pos is not None and event.buttons() & Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            self.move(event.globalPosition().toPoint() - self._drag_pos)
            event.accept()
        else:
            super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        self._drag_pos = None
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event).
.

from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QTableWidget,
                               QTableWidgetItem, QPushButton, QHeaderView,
                               QFileDialog, QMessageBox, QLabel)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from constants import Styles
from footprint_editor.ui.dialogs.headerless_dialog import HeaderlessDialog


class LibrariesManagerDialog(HeaderlessDialog):
    def __init__(self, library_paths, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent, title="Управление библиотеками")
        self.resize(700, 400)

        self.library_paths = list(library_paths)
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 14, 14, 14)
        layout.setSpacing(10)

        header = QLabel("Управление библиотеками")
        header.setStyleSheet(Styles.OBJECT_TITLE)
        layout.addWidget(header)

        self.table = QTableWidget(0, 2)
        self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Имя", "Путь"])
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        self.table.setSelectionBehavior(QTableWidget.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows)
        self.table.setSelectionMode(QTableWidget.SelectionMode.SingleSelection)
        self.table.setEditTriggers(QTableWidget.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers)
        self.table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        self.table.setStyleSheet(
            "QTableWidget { background-color: #1e1e1e; color: #cfcfcf;"
            " gridline-color: #3c3f41; border: 1px solid #3c3f41; }"
            "QHeaderView::section { background-color: #2b2b2b; color: #a9b7c6;"
            " border: 0px; border-right: 1px solid #3c3f41; padding: 4px; }"
        )
        layout.addWidget(self.table)

        btns = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_add = QPushButton("Добавить...")
        self.btn_add.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_OK)
        self.btn_add.clicked.connect(self._add_library)

        self.btn_remove = QPushButton("Удалить")
        self.btn_remove.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_CANCEL)
        self.btn_remove.clicked.connect(self._remove_selected)

        btns.addWidget(self.btn_add)
        btns.addWidget(self.btn_remove)
        btns.addStretch()

        self.btn_close = QPushButton("Закрыть")
        self.btn_close.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_CANCEL)
        self.btn_close.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        btns.addWidget(self.btn_close)

        layout.addLayout(btns)

        self._refresh()

    def _refresh(self):
        self.table.setRowCount(0)
        for path in self.library_paths:
            row = self.table.rowCount()
            self.table.insertRow(row)
            name_item = QTableWidgetItem(Path(path).stem)
            name_item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, path)
            self.table.setItem(row, 0, name_item)
            self.table.setItem(row, 1, QTableWidgetItem(path))

    def _add_library(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self, "Добавить библиотеку KiCad", "",
            "KiCad Library (*.mod);;All Files (*)"
        )
        if path:
            if path in self.library_paths:
                QMessageBox.information(self, "Информация", "Эта библиотека уже добавлена.")
                return
            self.library_paths.append(path)
            self._refresh()

    def _remove_selected(self):
        row = self.table.currentRow()
        if row >= 0:
            del self.library_paths[row]
            self._refresh()

    def get_library_paths(self):
        return list(self.library_paths).
.

from pathlib import Path
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QPushButton
from PySide6.QtGui import QIcon
from PySide6.QtCore import Signal, Qt, QSize
from constants import Styles


BUTTON_SIZE = 40
ICONS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "resources" / "icons"


def _load_icon(name, fallback_text):
    path = ICONS_DIR / f"{name}.svg"
    if path.exists():
        return QIcon(str(path)), ""
    return QIcon(), fallback_text


class AuxBar(QWidget):
    undo_requested = Signal()
    redo_requested = Signal()
    select_requested = Signal()
    delete_requested = Signal()

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)

        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(8, 6, 8, 6)
        layout.setSpacing(8)

        self.btn_undo = self._make_button("undo", "↶", "Отменить (Ctrl+Z)")
        self.btn_undo.clicked.connect(self.undo_requested.emit)
        layout.addWidget(self.btn_undo)

        self.btn_redo = self._make_button("redo", "↷", "Повторить (Ctrl+Shift+Z)")
        self.btn_redo.clicked.connect(self.redo_requested.emit)
        layout.addWidget(self.btn_redo)

        self.btn_select = self._make_button("select", "■", "Выделение (Esc)", checkable=True)
        self.btn_select.clicked.connect(self.select_requested.emit)
        layout.addWidget(self.btn_select)

        self.btn_delete = self._make_button("delete", "🗑", "Удалить (Del)")
        self.btn_delete.clicked.connect(self.delete_requested.emit)
        layout.addWidget(self.btn_delete)

        layout.addStretch()

        self.setObjectName("AuxBar")
        self.setStyleSheet(Styles.AUX_BAR)
        self.set_select_active(True)
        self.set_delete_enabled(False)

    def _make_button(self, icon_name, fallback_text, tip, checkable=False):
        btn = QPushButton()
        icon, text = _load_icon(icon_name, fallback_text)
        if not icon.isNull():
            btn.setIcon(icon)
            btn.setIconSize(QSize(24, 24))
        else:
            btn.setText(text)
        btn.setFixedSize(BUTTON_SIZE, BUTTON_SIZE)
        btn.setToolTip(tip)
        btn.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        if checkable:
            btn.setCheckable(True)
        return btn

    def apply_theme(self):
        self.setStyleSheet(Styles.AUX_BAR)
        self._refresh()

    def set_undo_enabled(self, enabled):
        self.btn_undo.setEnabled(enabled)
        self.btn_undo.setStyleSheet(
            Styles.TOOL_BUTTON_INACTIVE if enabled else Styles.BUTTON_DISABLED
        )

    def set_redo_enabled(self, enabled):
        self.btn_redo.setEnabled(enabled)
        self.btn_redo.setStyleSheet(
            Styles.TOOL_BUTTON_INACTIVE if enabled else Styles.BUTTON_DISABLED
        )

    def set_select_active(self, active):
        self.btn_select.setChecked(active)
        self.btn_select.setStyleSheet(
            Styles.TOOL_BUTTON_ACTIVE if active else Styles.TOOL_BUTTON_INACTIVE
        )

    def set_delete_enabled(self, enabled):
        self.btn_delete.setEnabled(enabled)
        self.btn_delete.setStyleSheet(
            Styles.TOOL_BUTTON_INACTIVE if enabled else Styles.BUTTON_DISABLED
        )

    def _refresh(self):
        self.set_undo_enabled(self.btn_undo.isEnabled())
        self.set_redo_enabled(self.btn_redo.isEnabled())
        self.set_select_active(self.btn_select.isChecked())
        self.set_delete_enabled(self.btn_delete.isEnabled()).
.

from pathlib import Path
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QPushButton
from PySide6.QtGui import QIcon
from PySide6.QtCore import Signal, Qt, QSize
from constants import Styles, Tool


ICONS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "resources" / "icons"

TOOL_BUTTONS = [
    (Tool.ADD_LINE, "line", "╱", "Линия (1)"),
    (Tool.ADD_PAD, "pad", "⬤", "Площадка (2)"),
    (Tool.ADD_CIRCLE, "circle", "◯", "Окружность (3)"),
    (Tool.ADD_ARC, "arc", "◜", "Дуга (4)"),
    (Tool.ADD_TEXT, "text", "Т", "Текст (5)"),
]

BUTTON_SIZE = 40


def _load_icon(name):
    path = ICONS_DIR / f"{name}.svg"
    if path.exists():
        return QIcon(str(path))
    return QIcon()


class DrawingToolBar(QWidget):
    tool_selected = Signal(str)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)

        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(8, 6, 8, 6)
        layout.setSpacing(8)

        self.buttons = {}
        self._active = Tool.ADD_LINE

        for tool, icon_name, fallback, tip in TOOL_BUTTONS:
            btn = QPushButton()
            icon = _load_icon(icon_name)
            if not icon.isNull():
                btn.setIcon(icon)
                btn.setIconSize(QSize(24, 24))
            else:
                btn.setText(fallback)
            btn.setFixedSize(BUTTON_SIZE, BUTTON_SIZE)
            btn.setToolTip(tip)
            btn.setCheckable(True)
            btn.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            btn.clicked.connect(lambda _=False, t=tool: self._on_clicked(t))
            layout.addWidget(btn)
            self.buttons[tool] = btn

        layout.addStretch()
        self.setObjectName("DrawingToolBar")
        self.setStyleSheet(
            "QWidget#DrawingToolBar { background-color: #2b2b2b;"
            " border-top: 1px solid #1e1e1e; }"
        )
        self._update_styles()

    def _on_clicked(self, tool):
        self.set_active(tool)
        self.tool_selected.emit(tool)

    def set_active(self, tool):
        if tool is None:
            self._active = None
        else:
            self._active = tool
        self._update_styles()

    def clear_active(self):
        self._active = None
        self._update_styles()

    def _update_styles(self):
        for tool, btn in self.buttons.items():
            is_active = (self._active == tool)
            btn.setChecked(is_active)
            btn.setStyleSheet(Styles.TOOL_BUTTON_ACTIVE if is_active
                              else Styles.TOOL_BUTTON_INACTIVE)

    def apply_theme(self):
        self._update_styles().
.

FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\footprint_editor\ui\panels\layers_table.py

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QGridLayout, QCheckBox
from PySide6.QtCore import Signal


LAYER_LABELS = {
    "F.Cu": "Front",
    "B.Cu": "Back",
    "F.Adhes": "Adhes_Front",
    "B.Adhes": "Adhes_Back",
    "F.Paste": "SoldP_Front",
    "B.Paste": "SoldP_Back",
    "F.SilkS": "SilkS_Front",
    "B.SilkS": "SilkS_Back",
    "F.Mask": "Mask_Front",
    "B.Mask": "Mask_Back",
    "Cmts.User": "Чертёж",
    "Eco1.User": "Eco1",
    "Eco2.User": "Eco2",
}


CHECKBOX_QSS = (
    "QCheckBox { color: #d8d8d8; spacing: 8px; background: transparent; }"
    "QCheckBox::indicator { width: 14px; height: 14px;"
    " border: 1px solid #d8d8d8; background-color: #2b2b2b;"
    " border-radius: 2px; }"
    "QCheckBox::indicator:hover { border: 1px solid #ffffff; }"
    "QCheckBox::indicator:checked {"
    " border: 1px solid #ffffff; background-color: #365880; }"
)


class LayersTable(QWidget):
    layers_changed = Signal(list)

    def __init__(self, parent=None, multi=True):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.multi = multi
        self._updating = False

        layout = QGridLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        layout.setHorizontalSpacing(16)
        layout.setVerticalSpacing(4)

        self.checkboxes = {}

        rows = [
            ("F.Cu", "B.Cu"),
            ("F.Adhes", "B.Adhes"),
            ("F.Paste", "B.Paste"),
            ("F.SilkS", "B.SilkS"),
            ("F.Mask", "B.Mask"),
            ("Cmts.User", None),
            ("Eco1.User", None),
            ("Eco2.User", None),
        ]

        for row, (left, right) in enumerate(rows):
            cb_left = self._make_cb(left)
            layout.addWidget(cb_left, row, 0)
            if right is not None:
                cb_right = self._make_cb(right)
                layout.addWidget(cb_right, row, 1)

        layout.setColumnStretch(0, 1)
        layout.setColumnStretch(1, 1)

    def _make_cb(self, layer):
        cb = QCheckBox(LAYER_LABELS.get(layer, layer))
        cb.setProperty("layer_id", layer)
        cb.setStyleSheet(CHECKBOX_QSS)
        cb.stateChanged.connect(self._on_changed)
        self.checkboxes[layer] = cb
        return cb

    def set_layers(self, layers):
        self._updating = True
        try:
            layers = set(layers or [])
            for layer, cb in self.checkboxes.items():
                cb.blockSignals(True)
                cb.setChecked(layer in layers)
                cb.blockSignals(False)
        finally:
            self._updating = False

    def selected_layers(self):
        return [l for l, cb in self.checkboxes.items() if cb.isChecked()]

    def _on_changed(self, _state):
        if self._updating:
            return
        if not self.multi:
            sender = self.sender()
            if sender.isChecked():
                self._updating = True
                for cb in self.checkboxes.values():
                    if cb is not sender:
                        cb.blockSignals(True)
                        cb.setChecked(False)
                        cb.blockSignals(False)
                self._updating = False
        self.layers_changed.emit(self.selected_layers()).
.

class LibraryPanel(QWidget):

    add_module_requested = Signal()

    module_selected = Signal(str)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):

        self.setMinimumWidth(LEFT_PANEL_MIN_WIDTH)
        self.setMaximumWidth(LEFT_PANEL_MAX_WIDTH)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(6, 6, 6, 6)
        layout.setSpacing(6)

        self.btn_add_library, lib_header = self._make_header("Библиотеки", "Добавить библиотеку")
        self.btn_add_library.clicked.connect(self.add_library_requested.emit)
        layout.addLayout(lib_header)

        self.library_list_widget = QListWidget()
        self.library_list_widget.setStyleSheet(Styles.LIST_BG)
        self.library_list_widget.itemClicked.connect(self._on_library_clicked)
        layout.addWidget(self.library_list_widget, 1)

        self.btn_add_module, mod_header = self._make_header("Корпуса", "Добавить корпус")
        self.btn_add_module.clicked.connect(self.add_module_requested.emit)
        layout.addLayout(mod_header)

        self.module_list_widget = QListWidget()
        self.module_list_widget.setStyleSheet(Styles.LIST_BG)
        self.module_list_widget.itemClicked.connect(self._on_module_clicked)
        self.module_list_widget.setContextMenuPolicy(Qt.ContextMenuPolicy.CustomContextMenu)
        self.module_list_widget.customContextMenuRequested.connect(self._on_module_context_menu)
        layout.addWidget(self.module_list_widget, 1)

    def _make_header(self, title, tooltip):
        header = QHBoxLayout()
        header.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        label = QLabel(title)
        label.setStyleSheet(Styles.SECTION_LABEL)
        header.addWidget(label)
        header.addStretch()

        btn = QPushButton("+")
        btn.setFixedSize(24, 24)
        btn.setToolTip(tooltip)
        btn.setStyleSheet(ADD_BUTTON_STYLE)
        btn.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        header.addWidget(btn)
        return btn, header

    def apply_theme(self):
        self.btn_add_library.setStyleSheet(ADD_BUTTON_STYLE)
        self.btn_add_module.setStyleSheet(ADD_BUTTON_STYLE)
        self.library_list_widget.setStyleSheet(Styles.LIST_BG)
        self.module_list_widget.setStyleSheet(Styles.LIST_BG)

    def _on_library_clicked(self, item):
        path = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
        if path:
            self.library_selected.emit(path)

    def _on_module_clicked(self, item):
        name = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
        if name:
            self.module_selected.emit(name)

    def _on_module_context_menu(self, pos):
        item = self.module_list_widget.itemAt(pos)
        if not item:
            return

        name = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
        if not name:
            return

        menu = QMenu(self)
        menu.setStyleSheet(Styles.MAIN_WINDOW)

        act_rename = menu.addAction("Переименовать...")
        act_delete = menu.addAction("Удалить")

        action = menu.exec(self.module_list_widget.mapToGlobal(pos))
        if action == act_rename:
            self.module_rename_requested.emit(name)
        elif action == act_delete:
            self.module_delete_requested.emit(name)

    def set_libraries(self, paths):
        self.library_list_widget.clear()
        for path in paths:
            item = QListWidgetItem(Path(path).stem)
            item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, path)
            item.setToolTip(path)
            self.library_list_widget.addItem(item)

    def set_modules(self, names):
        self.module_list_widget.clear()
        for name in sorted(names):
            item = QListWidgetItem(name)
            item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, name)
            item.setToolTip(name)
            self.module_list_widget.addItem(item)

    def mark_unsaved(self, unsaved_names):
        for i in range(self.module_list_widget.count()):
            item = self.module_list_widget.item(i)
            name = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
            item.setText(f"{name} *" if name in unsaved_names else name)

    def highlight_library(self, path):
        if not path:
            self.library_list_widget.clearSelection()
            return
        for i in range(self.library_list_widget.count()):
            item = self.library_list_widget.item(i)
            if item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole) == path:
                self.library_list_widget.setCurrentItem(item)
                return
        self.library_list_widget.clearSelection()

    def highlight_module(self, name):
        if not name:
            self.module_list_widget.clearSelection()
            return
        for i in range(self.module_list_widget.count()):
            item = self.module_list_widget.item(i)
            if item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole) == name:
                self.module_list_widget.setCurrentItem(item)
                return
        self.module_list_widget.clearSelection().
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QFormLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
                               QComboBox, QLineEdit, QCheckBox,
                               QPushButton, QListView, QSizePolicy)
from PySide6.QtGui import QDoubleValidator
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from footprint_editor.core.models import (
    Pad, Line, Circle, Arc, Text, PadShape, PadType, TextType,
    default_layers_for_pad_type
)
from constants import Styles
from footprint_editor.ui.panels.layers_table import LayersTable


TYPE_NAMES_RU = {
    Pad: "Конт. площадка",
    Line: "Линия",
    Circle: "Окружность",
    Arc: "Дуга",
    Text: "Текст",
}

PAD_TYPE_NAMES = {
    PadType.STANDARD: "Стандарт",
    PadType.SMD: "SMD",
    PadType.CONN: "Conn",
}

PAD_TYPE_ORDER = [PadType.STANDARD, PadType.SMD, PadType.CONN]

CHECKBOX_QSS = (
    "QCheckBox { color: #a9b7c6; spacing: 8px; background: transparent; }"
    "QCheckBox::indicator { width: 14px; height: 14px;"
    " border: 1px solid #d8d8d8; background-color: #2b2b2b;"
    " border-radius: 2px; }"
    "QCheckBox::indicator:hover { border: 1px solid #ffffff; }"
    "QCheckBox::indicator:checked {"
    " border: 1px solid #ffffff; background-color: #365880; }"
)


class NumericLineEdit(QLineEdit):
    def __init__(self, value=0.0, decimals=4, min_val=-1e9, max_val=1e9, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._decimals = decimals
        self._min = min_val
        self._max = max_val
        validator = QDoubleValidator(min_val, max_val, decimals, self)
        validator.setNotation(QDoubleValidator.Notation.StandardNotation)
        self.setValidator(validator)
        self.setText(self._format(value))
        self.setMinimumWidth(100)
        self.setMinimumHeight(24)

    def _format(self, value):
        return f"{value:.{self._decimals}f}"

    def focusInEvent(self, event):
        super().focusInEvent(event)
        self.selectAll()

    def value(self):
        text = self.text().strip().replace(',', '.')
        if not text:
            return 0.0
        try:
            v = float(text)
        except ValueError:
            return 0.0
        return max(self._min, min(v, self._max))

    def setValue(self, value):
        self.setText(self._format(value))


class PropertiesPanel(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.main_layout.setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4)
        self.main_layout.setSpacing(6)
        self.main_layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop)

        self.header_label = QLabel("")
        self.header_label.setStyleSheet(Styles.SECTION_LABEL)
        self.header_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft)

        self.form_widget = QWidget()
        self.form_widget.setObjectName("PropertiesTable")
        self.form_widget.setStyleSheet(self._form_widget_qss())
        self.form_widget.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                                       QSizePolicy.Policy.Preferred)
        self.form_layout = QFormLayout(self.form_widget)
        self.form_layout.setContentsMargins(8, 8, 8, 8)
        self.form_layout.setLabelAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)
        self.form_layout.setFormAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop)
        self.form_layout.setFieldGrowthPolicy(QFormLayout.FieldGrowthPolicy.AllNonFixedFieldsGrow)
        self.form_layout.setRowWrapPolicy(QFormLayout.RowWrapPolicy.DontWrapRows)
        self.form_layout.setHorizontalSpacing(8)
        self.form_layout.setVerticalSpacing(4)

        self.buttons_widget = QWidget()
        btns = QHBoxLayout(self.buttons_widget)
        btns.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        btns.setSpacing(6)

        self.btn_reset = QPushButton("Отменить")
        self.btn_reset.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_CANCEL)
        self.btn_reset.clicked.connect(self._reset_changes)

        self.btn_apply = QPushButton("Применить")
        self.btn_apply.setStyleSheet(Styles.APPLY_BUTTON)
        self.btn_apply.clicked.connect(self._apply_changes)

        btns.addWidget(self.btn_reset)
        btns.addWidget(self.btn_apply)

        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.header_label)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.form_widget)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.buttons_widget)
        self.main_layout.addStretch()

        self.header_label.hide()
        self.form_widget.hide()
        self.buttons_widget.hide()

        self.current_object = None
        self._widgets = {}
        self._building = False

    def _form_widget_qss(self):
        return (
            "QWidget#PropertiesTable { background-color: #2b2b2b;"
            " border: 1px solid #555555; }"
            " QWidget#PropertiesTable QLabel { background: transparent; color: #a9b7c6; }"
            " QWidget#PropertiesTable QLineEdit {"
            "   background: transparent; color: #ffffff;"
            "   border: 1px solid transparent;"
            "   border-bottom: 1px solid #555555;"
            "   padding: 2px 6px;"
            " }"
            " QWidget#PropertiesTable QLineEdit:focus {"
            "   border-bottom: 1px solid #365880;"
            " }"
            " QWidget#PropertiesTable QComboBox {"
            "   background: transparent; color: #ffffff;"
            "   border: 1px solid transparent;"
            "   border-bottom: 1px solid #555555;"
            "   padding: 2px 6px;"
            " }"
            " QWidget#PropertiesTable QComboBox::drop-down {"
            "   border: none; width: 14px; }"
        )

    def apply_theme(self):
        self.header_label.setStyleSheet(Styles.SECTION_LABEL)
        self.form_widget.setStyleSheet(self._form_widget_qss())
        self.btn_apply.setStyleSheet(Styles.APPLY_BUTTON)
        self.btn_reset.setStyleSheet(Styles.BUTTON_CANCEL)

    def clear_panel(self):
        self.setUpdatesEnabled(False)
        try:
            while self.form_layout.rowCount() > 0:
                self.form_layout.removeRow(0)
            self._widgets.clear()
            self.current_object = None
            self.header_label.hide()
            self.form_widget.hide()
            self.buttons_widget.hide()
            self.header_label.setText("")
        finally:
            self.setUpdatesEnabled(True)

    def set_object(self, obj):
        self.setUpdatesEnabled(False)
        try:
            self._building = True
            try:
                while self.form_layout.rowCount() > 0:
                    self.form_layout.removeRow(0)
                self._widgets.clear()
            finally:
                self._building = False

            self.current_object = obj

            type_name = TYPE_NAMES_RU.get(type(obj), "Объект")
            self.header_label.setText(f"{type_name} #{obj.uid}")

            if isinstance(obj, Pad):
                self._build_pad_ui(obj)
            elif isinstance(obj, Line):
                self._build_line_ui(obj)
            elif isinstance(obj, Circle):
                self._build_circle_ui(obj)
            elif isinstance(obj, Arc):
                self._build_arc_ui(obj)
            elif isinstance(obj, Text):
                self._build_text_ui(obj)

            self.header_label.show()
            self.form_widget.show()
            self.buttons_widget.show()
            self.main_layout.invalidate()
        finally:
            self.setUpdatesEnabled(True)
            self.updateGeometry()
            self.update()

    def refresh_values(self):
        obj = self.current_object
        if not obj or self._building:
            return

        self.setUpdatesEnabled(False)
        try:
            if isinstance(obj, Pad):
                self._widgets["x"].setValue(obj.x)
                self._widgets["y"].setValue(obj.y)
                self._widgets["width"].setValue(obj.width)
                self._widgets["height"].setValue(obj.height)
                self._widgets["drill"].setValue(obj.drill or 0.0)
            elif isinstance(obj, Line):
                self._widgets["x"].setValue(obj.x)
                self._widgets["y"].setValue(obj.y)
                self._widgets["x2"].setValue(obj.x2)
                self._widgets["y2"].setValue(obj.y2)
                self._widgets["width"].setValue(obj.width)
            elif isinstance(obj, Circle):
                self._widgets["x"].setValue(obj.x)
                self._widgets["y"].setValue(obj.y)
                self._widgets["diameter"].setValue(obj.radius * 2)
                self._widgets["width"].setValue(obj.width)
            elif isinstance(obj, Arc):
                self._widgets["x"].setValue(obj.x)
                self._widgets["y"].setValue(obj.y)
                self._widgets["sx"].setValue(obj.sx)
                self._widgets["sy"].setValue(obj.sy)
                self._widgets["angle"].setValue(obj.angle)
                self._widgets["width"].setValue(obj.width)
            elif isinstance(obj, Text):
                self._widgets["x"].setValue(obj.x)
                self._widgets["y"].setValue(obj.y)
                self._widgets["size"].setValue(obj.size)
                self._widgets["text"].setText(obj.text)
                self._widgets["visible"].setChecked(obj.visible)
        finally:
            self.setUpdatesEnabled(True)

    def _make_label(self, text):
        label = QLabel(text)
        label.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; color: #a9b7c6;")
        label.setMinimumWidth(90)
        label.setWordWrap(False)
        return label

    def _add_row(self, label_text, field_widget, key):
        self.form_layout.addRow(self._make_label(label_text), field_widget)
        self._widgets[key] = field_widget

    def _add_layers_block(self, initial_layers, multi):
        header = QLabel("Слои:")
        header.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; color: #a9b7c6;")
        self.form_layout.addRow(header)

        layers_table = LayersTable(multi=multi)
        layers_table.set_layers(initial_layers)
        self.form_layout.addRow(layers_table)
        self._widgets["layers_table"] = layers_table
        return layers_table

    def _make_combo_view(self):
        view = QListView()
        view.setStyleSheet(
            "QListView { background-color: #2b2b2b; color: #a9b7c6;"
            " border: 1px solid #555555; outline: 0; }"
            "QListView::item { padding: 4px 8px; }"
            "QListView::item:selected {"
            "  background-color: #365880; color: white; }"
        )
        view.setMinimumWidth(140)
        view.setSpacing(0)
        return view

    def _make_combo(self, items, current_index=0):
        combo = QComboBox()
        for label, data in items:
            combo.addItem(label, data)
        combo.setCurrentIndex(current_index)
        combo.setView(self._make_combo_view())
        combo.setMinimumWidth(100)
        combo.setMinimumHeight(24)
        combo.setStyleSheet(
            "QComboBox { background: transparent; color: #ffffff;"
            " border: 1px solid transparent; border-bottom: 1px solid #555555;"
            " padding: 2px 6px; }"
            " QComboBox:focus { border-bottom: 1px solid #365880; }"
            " QComboBox::drop-down { border: none; width: 14px; }"
        )
        return combo

    def _build_pad_ui(self, pad):
        pad_type_combo = self._make_combo(
            [(PAD_TYPE_NAMES[pt], pt) for pt in PAD_TYPE_ORDER],
            PAD_TYPE_ORDER.index(pad.pad_type) if pad.pad_type in PAD_TYPE_ORDER else 0,
        )
        pad_type_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(
            lambda _i, p=pad, c=pad_type_combo: self._on_pad_type_changed(p, c)
        )
        self._add_row("Тип:", pad_type_combo, "pad_type")

        self._add_spinbox("X, мм:", pad.x, -500.0, 500.0, "x")
        self._add_spinbox("Y, мм:", pad.y, -500.0, 500.0, "y")

        text_input = QLineEdit(pad.number)
        text_input.setMinimumWidth(100)
        text_input.setMinimumHeight(24)
        self._add_row("Номер:", text_input, "number")

        self._add_spinbox("Ширина, мм:", pad.width, 0.1, 50.0, "width")
        self._add_spinbox("Высота, мм:", pad.height, 0.1, 50.0, "height")
        self._add_spinbox("Отверстие, мм:", pad.drill or 0.0, 0.0, 50.0, "drill")

        shape_combo = self._make_combo(
            [("Круг", PadShape.CIRCLE), ("Квадрат", PadShape.RECT)],
            0 if pad.shape == PadShape.CIRCLE else 1,
        )
        self._add_row("Форма:", shape_combo, "shape")

        self._add_layers_block(pad.layers, multi=True)

    def _build_line_ui(self, line):
        self._add_spinbox("X1, мм:", line.x, -500.0, 500.0, "x")
        self._add_spinbox("Y1, мм:", line.y, -500.0, 500.0, "y")
        self._add_spinbox("X2, мм:", line.x2, -500.0, 500.0, "x2")
        self._add_spinbox("Y2, мм:", line.y2, -500.0, 500.0, "y2")
        self._add_spinbox("Толщина, мм:", line.width, 0.01, 5.0, "width")
        self._add_layers_block([line.layer], multi=False)

    def _build_circle_ui(self, circle):
        self._add_spinbox("Центр X, мм:", circle.x, -500.0, 500.0, "x")
        self._add_spinbox("Центр Y, мм:", circle.y, -500.0, 500.0, "y")
        self._add_spinbox("Диаметр, мм:", circle.radius * 2, 0.1, 20.0, "diameter")
        self._add_spinbox("Толщина, мм:", circle.width, 0.01, 5.0, "width")
        self._add_layers_block([circle.layer], multi=False)

    def _build_arc_ui(self, arc):
        self._add_spinbox("Центр X, мм:", arc.x, -500.0, 500.0, "x")
        self._add_spinbox("Центр Y, мм:", arc.y, -500.0, 500.0, "y")
        self._add_spinbox("Начало X, мм:", arc.sx, -500.0, 500.0, "sx")
        self._add_spinbox("Начало Y, мм:", arc.sy, -500.0, 500.0, "sy")
        self._add_spinbox("Угол, °:", arc.angle, -359.0, 359.0, "angle", decimals=1)
        self._add_spinbox("Толщина, мм:", arc.width, 0.01, 5.0, "width")
        self._add_layers_block([arc.layer], multi=False)

    def _build_text_ui(self, text):
        self._add_spinbox("X, мм:", text.x, -500.0, 500.0, "x")
        self._add_spinbox("Y, мм:", text.y, -500.0, 500.0, "y")
        self._add_spinbox("Размер, мм:", text.size, 0.05, 10.0, "size")

        text_input = QLineEdit(text.text)
        text_input.setMinimumWidth(100)
        text_input.setMinimumHeight(24)
        self._add_row("Текст:", text_input, "text")

        visible_check = QCheckBox()
        visible_check.setChecked(text.visible)
        visible_check.setStyleSheet(CHECKBOX_QSS)
        self._add_row("Видимый:", visible_check, "visible")

        self._add_layers_block([text.layer], multi=False)

    def _add_spinbox(self, label, value, min_val, max_val, key, step=0.01, decimals=4, readonly=False):
        le = NumericLineEdit(value, decimals, min_val, max_val)
        le.setReadOnly(readonly)
        self._add_row(label, le, key)

    def _on_pad_type_changed(self, pad, combo):
        new_type = combo.currentData()
        if new_type == pad.pad_type:
            return
        pad.pad_type = new_type
        pad.layers = default_layers_for_pad_type(new_type)
        layers_table = self._widgets.get("layers_table")
        if layers_table is not None:
            layers_table.set_layers(pad.layers)
        if pad.graphics_item is not None:
            pad.graphics_item.update()

    def _reset_changes(self):
        if self.current_object is not None:
            self.set_object(self.current_object)

    def _apply_changes(self):
        obj = self.current_object
        if not obj:
            return

        if isinstance(obj, Pad):
            self._apply_pad(obj)
        elif isinstance(obj, Line):
            self._apply_line(obj)
        elif isinstance(obj, Circle):
            self._apply_circle(obj)
        elif isinstance(obj, Arc):
            self._apply_arc(obj)
        elif isinstance(obj, Text):
            self._apply_text(obj)

        obj.graphics_item.update_geometry()

    def _apply_pad(self, obj):
        obj.x = self._widgets["x"].value()
        obj.y = self._widgets["y"].value()
        obj.number = self._widgets["number"].text()
        obj.width = self._widgets["width"].value()
        obj.height = self._widgets["height"].value()
        obj.drill = self._widgets["drill"].value()
        obj.shape = self._widgets["shape"].currentData()
        obj.pad_type = self._widgets["pad_type"].currentData()
        new_layers = self._widgets["layers_table"].selected_layers()
        if new_layers:
            obj.layers = new_layers
        obj.graphics_item.setPos(obj.x, obj.y)

    def _apply_line(self, obj):
        obj.x = self._widgets["x"].value()
        obj.y = self._widgets["y"].value()
        obj.x2 = self._widgets["x2"].value()
        obj.y2 = self._widgets["y2"].value()
        obj.width = self._widgets["width"].value()
        self._apply_single_layer(obj)

    def _apply_circle(self, obj):
        obj.x = self._widgets["x"].value()
        obj.y = self._widgets["y"].value()
        obj.radius = self._widgets["diameter"].value() / 2
        obj.width = self._widgets["width"].value()
        self._apply_single_layer(obj)

    def _apply_arc(self, obj):
        obj.x = self._widgets["x"].value()
        obj.y = self._widgets["y"].value()
        obj.sx = self._widgets["sx"].value()
        obj.sy = self._widgets["sy"].value()
        obj.angle = self._widgets["angle"].value()
        obj.width = self._widgets["width"].value()
        self._apply_single_layer(obj)

    def _apply_text(self, obj):
        obj.x = self._widgets["x"].value()
        obj.y = self._widgets["y"].value()
        obj.size = self._widgets["size"].value()
        text_value = self._widgets["text"].text()
        obj.text = text_value if text_value else "Text"
        obj.visible = self._widgets["visible"].isChecked()
        self._apply_single_layer(obj)

    def _apply_single_layer(self, obj):
        layers = self._widgets["layers_table"].selected_layers()
        if layers:
            obj.layer = layers[0].

from constants import GRID_SERIES, GRID_SERIES_LABEL


class GridService:
    def _grid_series(self):
        return GRID_SERIES

    def _apply_grid_series(self, mode, initial=False):
        if mode not in GRID_SERIES:
            return
        self.grid_mode = mode
        self._persist_grid_mode(mode)

        for m, act in self._grid_actions.items():
            act.setChecked(m == mode)

        idx = self.grid_series_combo.findData(mode)
        if idx >= 0 and self.grid_series_combo.currentIndex() != idx:
            self.grid_series_combo.blockSignals(True)
            self.grid_series_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)
            self.grid_series_combo.blockSignals(False)

        values = GRID_SERIES[mode]
        self.grid_combo.blockSignals(True)
        self.grid_combo.clear()
        self.grid_combo.addItems(values)
        default = "1.000" if mode == "1x" else "1.270"
        self.grid_combo.setCurrentText(default if default in values else values[0])
        self.grid_combo.blockSignals(False)

        self.canvas.set_grid_size(float(self.grid_combo.currentText()))
        if not initial:
            self.show_log(f"Ряд сетки: {GRID_SERIES_LABEL[mode]}")

    def _toggle_grid_series(self):
        self._apply_grid_series("1.27x" if self.grid_mode == "1x" else "1x")

    def _on_grid_changed(self, text):
        if not text:
            return
        self.canvas.set_grid_size(float(text))
        self.canvas.setFocus()

    def _change_grid_step(self, direction):
        i = self.grid_combo.currentIndex() + direction
        if 0 <= i < self.grid_combo.count():
            self.grid_combo.setCurrentIndex(i)

    def _update_zoom_label(self, zoom):
        self.zoom_label.setText(f" {int(zoom * 100)}% ").

class HistoryService:
    def commit_history(self, *_args):
        self._modified = True
        self.unsaved_modules[self.current_module_name] = self.footprint
        self.refresh_module_list_display()

        self.history.push(self.footprint)
        self._update_history_buttons()

    def undo(self):
        snapshot = self.history.undo()
        if snapshot is None:
            return
        self._restore_footprint(snapshot)
        self._update_history_buttons()
        self.show_log("отменено")

    def redo(self):
        snapshot = self.history.redo()
        if snapshot is None:
            return
        self._restore_footprint(snapshot)
        self._update_history_buttons()
        self.show_log("повторено")

    def _restore_footprint(self, snapshot):
        name = self.current_module_name
        self.footprint = snapshot
        self.footprint.name = name
        self.manager.footprint = self.footprint

        self.canvas.scene.clear()
        self.manager.items.clear()
        self._populate_scene(self.footprint)
        self.commands.ensure_reference_and_value()
        self.right_panel.properties_panel.clear_panel()
        self.unsaved_modules[self.current_module_name] = self.footprint

    def _update_history_buttons(self):
        self.aux_bar.set_undo_enabled(self.history.can_undo())
        self.aux_bar.set_redo_enabled(self.history.can_redo()).
.

from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from footprint_editor.kicad.exporter import build_kicad_module_text
from footprint_editor.core.models import TextType


class ModuleLifecycleService:
    def _populate_scene(self, module):
        for bucket in (module.pads, module.lines, module.circles, module.arcs, module.texts):
            for model in bucket:
                self.manager.add_model(model)

    def load_footprint(self, module, mark_unsaved=None):
        self.footprint = module
        self.manager.footprint = self.footprint
        self.canvas.scene.clear()
        self.manager.items.clear()

        self._populate_scene(module)
        self.commands.ensure_reference_and_value()

        self.current_module_name = module.name
        if mark_unsaved is None:
            mark_unsaved = module.name in self.unsaved_modules
        self._modified = mark_unsaved
        self._update_window_title()
        self.refresh_module_list_display()
        self.history.reset(self.footprint)
        self._update_history_buttons()
        self.show_log(f"Загружен модуль: {module.name}")

    def stash_current_module(self):
        if self._modified:
            self.unsaved_modules[self.current_module_name] = self.footprint

    def mark_saved(self):
        self._modified = False
        self.refresh_module_list_display()

    def refresh_module_list_display(self):
        self.library_panel.mark_unsaved(set(self.unsaved_modules.keys()))
        self.library_panel.highlight_module(self.current_module_name)

    def build_kicad_module_text(self):
        return build_kicad_module_text(self.footprint)

    def export_to_kicad(self, filepath):
        lines = [
            f"PCBNEW-LibModule-V1  {datetime.now().strftime('%d.%m.%Y %H:%M:%S')}",
            "# encoding utf-8",
            "$INDEX", self.footprint.name, "$EndINDEX",
            build_kicad_module_text(self.footprint),
            "$EndLIBRARY",
        ]
        Path(filepath).write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding='utf-8')

    def _update_window_title(self):
        if self.current_library_path:
            lib_name = Path(self.current_library_path).stem
            title = f"TraceCAD: {lib_name}/{self.footprint.name}"
        else:
            title = f"TraceCAD: {self.footprint.name}"
        self.setWindowTitle(title)

    def get_next_module_name(self):
        existing = set()
        if self.current_library_path and self.current_library_path in self.library_modules:
            existing.update(self.library_modules[self.current_library_path].keys())
        
        i = 1
        while True:
            name = f"New_Footprint_{i}"
            if name not in existing:
                return name
            i += 1.
.

from pathlib import Path
from constants import (
    SETTINGS_KEY_LAST_LIB, SETTINGS_KEY_LAST_MODULE,
    SETTINGS_KEY_LIBS, SETTINGS_KEY_GRID_MODE,
)
from footprint_editor.kicad.importer import parse_kicad_mod
from footprint_editor.ui.dialogs.custom_question_dialog import CustomQuestionDialog


class SessionService:
    def _load_library_paths(self):
        saved = self._settings.value(SETTINGS_KEY_LIBS, "", type=str)
        if saved:
            self.library_paths = [p for p in saved.split("\n") if p.strip()]

    def _save_library_paths(self):
        self._settings.setValue(SETTINGS_KEY_LIBS, "\n".join(self.library_paths))
        self._settings.sync()

    def _load_grid_mode(self):
        mode = self._settings.value(SETTINGS_KEY_GRID_MODE, "1x", type=str)
        return mode if mode in self._grid_series() else "1x"

    def _persist_grid_mode(self, mode):
        self._settings.setValue(SETTINGS_KEY_GRID_MODE, mode)
        self._settings.sync()

    def _restore_last_session(self):
        last_lib = self._settings.value(SETTINGS_KEY_LAST_LIB, "", type=str)
        last_mod = self._settings.value(SETTINGS_KEY_LAST_MODULE, "", type=str)

        if not last_lib or last_lib not in self.library_paths:
            return

        try:
            if last_lib not in self.library_modules:
                footprints = parse_kicad_mod(last_lib)
                self.library_modules[last_lib] = {fp.name: fp for fp in footprints}

            self.current_library_path = last_lib
            names = list(self.library_modules[last_lib].keys())
            self.library_panel.set_modules(names)
            self.library_panel.highlight_library(self.current_library_path)

            if last_mod and last_mod in self.library_modules[last_lib]:
                import copy as _copy
                module = _copy.deepcopy(self.library_modules[last_lib][last_mod])
                self.load_footprint(module)
                self.show_log(f"Восстановлен: {Path(last_lib).stem}/{last_mod}")
            else:
                self._update_window_title()

            self.refresh_module_list_display()

        except Exception:
            pass

    def _save_last_session(self):
        if self.current_library_path:
            self._settings.setValue(SETTINGS_KEY_LAST_LIB, self.current_library_path)
            self._settings.setValue(SETTINGS_KEY_LAST_MODULE, self.current_module_name)
        else:
            self._settings.setValue(SETTINGS_KEY_LAST_LIB, "")
            self._settings.setValue(SETTINGS_KEY_LAST_MODULE, "")
        self._settings.sync()

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        if self._modified:
            dlg = CustomQuestionDialog(
                self,
                title="Несохранённые изменения",
                message=f"Сохранить изменения в корпусе '{self.current_module_name}' перед выходом?"
            )
            result = dlg.exec()
            if result == CustomQuestionDialog.ResultSave:
                self.commands.save_current()
            elif result == CustomQuestionDialog.ResultCancel:
                event.ignore()
                return

        self._save_last_session()
        super().closeEvent(event).
.

from PySide6.QtCore import QEvent, Qt
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsView
from constants import (
    Tool, PAN_KEYS,
    VK_ESCAPE, VK_DELETE, VK_F1, VK_F2, VK_F3, VK_F11,
    VK_C, VK_V, VK_S, VK_R, VK_Y, VK_Z, VK_G,
    VK_1, VK_2, VK_3, VK_4, VK_5,
)


class KeyboardService:
    def handle_global_key(self, event):
        vk = event.nativeVirtualKey()
        ctrl = bool(event.modifiers() & Qt.KeyboardModifier.ControlModifier)
        shift = bool(event.modifiers() & Qt.KeyboardModifier.ShiftModifier)

        if event.type() == QEvent.Type.KeyPress:
            if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_Escape:
                self._exit_to_select_mode()
                return True

            if self._handle_ctrl_key(vk, ctrl, shift):
                return True
            if self._handle_tool_key(vk):
                return True
            if self._handle_nav_key(vk):
                return True

        elif event.type() == QEvent.Type.KeyRelease:
            if vk in PAN_KEYS:
                self.canvas.pressed_keys.discard(vk)
                if not self.canvas.pressed_keys:
                    self.canvas.pan_timer.stop()
                return True

        return False

    def _handle_ctrl_key(self, vk, ctrl, shift):
        if not ctrl:
            return False
        if shift and vk == VK_Z:
            self.redo(); return True
        if vk == VK_Z:
            self.undo(); return True
        if vk == VK_Y:
            self.redo(); return True
        if vk == VK_C:
            self.commands.copy_selected(); return True
        if vk == VK_V:
            self.commands.paste(); return True
        if vk == VK_S:
            self.commands.save_current(); return True
        return True

    def _handle_tool_key(self, vk):
        tool_map = {
            VK_1: Tool.ADD_LINE,
            VK_2: Tool.ADD_PAD,
            VK_3: Tool.ADD_CIRCLE,
            VK_4: Tool.ADD_ARC,
            VK_5: Tool.ADD_TEXT,
        }
        if vk in tool_map:
            tool = tool_map[vk]
            self._on_drawing_tool(tool)
            self.tool_bar.set_active(tool)
            return True
        return False

    def _handle_nav_key(self, vk):
        if vk == VK_R:
            self.commands.rotate_selected(); return True
        if vk == VK_G:
            self.act_show_grid.setChecked(not self.act_show_grid.isChecked())
            self._toggle_grid()
            return True
        if vk == VK_F11:
            self.toggle_fullscreen(); return True
        if vk in PAN_KEYS:
            self.canvas.pressed_keys.add(vk)
            if not self.canvas.pan_timer.isActive():
                self.canvas.pan_timer.start()
            return True
        if vk == VK_DELETE:
            self.commands.delete_selected(); return True
        if vk == VK_F1:
            self._change_grid_step(-1); return True
        if vk == VK_F2:
            self._change_grid_step(1); return True
        if vk == VK_F3:
            self._toggle_grid_series(); return True
        return False

    def _exit_to_select_mode(self):
        self.finish_drawing()
        self.canvas.scene.clearSelection()
        self.current_tool = Tool.SELECT
        self.aux_bar.set_select_active(True)
        self.tool_bar.clear_active()
        self.canvas.set_show_snap(False)
        self.canvas.set_interaction_enabled(True)
        self.canvas.set_drag_mode(QGraphicsView.DragMode.RubberBandDrag)
        self.show_log(f"Инструмент: {Tool.SELECT}").
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsView
from constants import Tool


class ToolsService:
    def _on_drawing_tool(self, tool):
        self._set_tool(tool)
        self.aux_bar.set_select_active(False)

    def _on_right_tool(self, tool):
        self._set_tool(tool)
        if tool != Tool.SELECT:
            self.tool_bar.set_active(tool)

    def _set_tool(self, tool):
        self.current_tool = tool

        if tool == Tool.SELECT:
            self.aux_bar.set_select_active(True)
            self.tool_bar.clear_active()
        else:
            self.aux_bar.set_select_active(False)
            self.tool_bar.set_active(tool)

        self._reset_drawing_state()

        if tool == Tool.SELECT:
            self.canvas.set_show_snap(False)
            self.canvas.set_interaction_enabled(True)
            self.canvas.set_drag_mode(QGraphicsView.DragMode.RubberBandDrag)
        else:
            self.canvas.set_show_snap(True)
            self.canvas.set_interaction_enabled(False)
            self.canvas.set_drag_mode(QGraphicsView.DragMode.NoDrag)

        self.show_log(f"Инструмент: {tool}")

    def _reset_drawing_state(self):
        self.line_start_point = None
        self.circle_center_point = None
        self.arc_center_point = None
        self.arc_start_point = None
        self.text_position = None
        self.canvas.clear_ghost_line()
        self.canvas.clear_ghost_circle()
        self.canvas.clear_ghost_arc()
        self.canvas.clear_ghost_text()

    def finish_drawing(self):
        self._reset_drawing_state().
.

FILE: C:\Users\Nikitos\TraceCAD\src\pbe_editor\core\arc_geometry.py

import math


def arc_radius(cx, cy, sx, sy):
    return math.hypot(sx - cx, sy - cy)


def arc_start_angle(cx, cy, sx, sy):
    return math.atan2(sy - cy, sx - cx)


def arc_sample_points(cx, cy, sx, sy, angle_deg, segments=64):
    R = arc_radius(cx, cy, sx, sy)
    if R < 1e-9:
        return [(cx, cy), (sx, sy)]
    a1 = arc_start_angle(cx, cy, sx, sy)
    sweep = math.radians(angle_deg)
    pts = []
    for i in range(segments + 1):
        t = i / segments
        a = a1 + t * sweep
        pts.append((cx + R * math.cos(a), cy + R * math.sin(a)))
    return pts.
.

from PySide6.QtCore import QPointF

from pbe_editor.core.board_models import is_gnd_net, is_power_net
from pbe_editor.core.ratsnest_mst import build_mst_edges
from pbe_editor.graphics.module_item import BoardModuleGraphicsItem
from pbe_editor.graphics.board_line_item import (
    BoardLineGraphicsItem, BoardCircleGraphicsItem, BoardArcGraphicsItem,
    BoardTextGraphicsItem,
)
from pbe_editor.graphics.ratsnest_item import RatsnestGraphicsItem


class BoardManager:
    def __init__(self, board, scene, canvas):
        self.board = board
        self.scene = scene
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.items = {}
        self.line_items = {}
        self.circle_items = {}
        self.arc_items = {}
        self.text_items = {}
        self.ratsnest_items = []
        self.show_ratsnest = True

    def add_module(self, module):
        item = BoardModuleGraphicsItem(module, self.canvas)
        self.scene.addItem(item)
        self.items[module.uid] = item
        return item

    def add_line(self, line):
        item = BoardLineGraphicsItem(line, self.canvas)
        self.scene.addItem(item)
        self.line_items[line.uid] = item
        return item

    def add_circle(self, circle):
        item = BoardCircleGraphicsItem(circle, self.canvas)
        self.scene.addItem(item)
        self.circle_items[circle.uid] = item
        return item

    def add_arc(self, arc):
        item = BoardArcGraphicsItem(arc, self.canvas)
        self.scene.addItem(item)
        self.arc_items[arc.uid] = item
        return item

    def add_text(self, text):
        item = BoardTextGraphicsItem(text, self.canvas)
        self.scene.addItem(item)
        self.text_items[text.uid] = item
        return item

    def remove_module(self, module):
        item = self.items.pop(module.uid, None)
        if item is not None and item.scene() is self.scene:
            self.scene.removeItem(item)

    def remove_line(self, line):
        item = self.line_items.pop(line.uid, None)
        if item is not None and item.scene() is self.scene:
            self.scene.removeItem(item)

    def remove_circle(self, circle):
        item = self.circle_items.pop(circle.uid, None)
        if item is not None and item.scene() is self.scene:
            self.scene.removeItem(item)

    def remove_arc(self, arc):
        item = self.arc_items.pop(arc.uid, None)
        if item is not None and item.scene() is self.scene:
            self.scene.removeItem(item)

    def remove_text(self, text):
        item = self.text_items.pop(text.uid, None)
        if item is not None and item.scene() is self.scene:
            self.scene.removeItem(item)

    def clear(self):
        all_items = (list(self.items.values()) + list(self.line_items.values())
                     + list(self.circle_items.values()) + list(self.arc_items.values())
                     + list(self.text_items.values()))
        for item in all_items:
            try:
                if item.scene() is self.scene:
                    self.scene.removeItem(item)
            except RuntimeError:
                pass
        self.items.clear()
        self.line_items.clear()
        self.circle_items.clear()
        self.arc_items.clear()
        self.text_items.clear()
        self.clear_ratsnest()

    def clear_ratsnest(self):
        for item in self.ratsnest_items:
            try:
                if item.scene() is self.scene:
                    self.scene.removeItem(item)
            except RuntimeError:
                pass
        self.ratsnest_items.clear()

    def rebuild_ratsnest(self):
        self.clear_ratsnest()
        if not self.show_ratsnest:
            return
        net_pins = self.board.collect_net_pins()
        for code, pins in net_pins.items():
            if len(pins) < 2:
                continue
            net_name = pins[0][0].pad_nets.get(pins[0][1])
            net_name = net_name.net_name if net_name else ""
            is_gnd = is_gnd_net(net_name)
            is_power = is_power_net(net_name) and not is_gnd
            points = [p[2] for p in pins]
            edges = build_mst_edges(points)
            for a_idx, b_idx in edges:
                module_a, pad_a, pos_a = pins[a_idx]
                module_b, pad_b, pos_b = pins[b_idx]
                item = RatsnestGraphicsItem(
                    QPointF(*pos_a), QPointF(*pos_b),
                    net_code=code, net_name=net_name,
                    is_power=is_power, is_gnd=is_gnd,
                    label_a=f"{module_a.reference}.{pad_a}",
                    label_b=f"{module_b.reference}.{pad_b}",
                )
                self.scene.addItem(item)
                self.ratsnest_items.append(item)

    def refresh_ratsnest_for_module(self, module):
        self.rebuild_ratsnest().
.
