from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
import copy
import math
import re

from pbe_editor.core.models import Footprint


POWER_NET_NAMES = {"GND", "VCC", "VDD", "VSS", "+5V", "+3V3", "+3.3V", "-5V", "-12V", "+12V"}
GND_NET_NAMES = {"GND"}


def is_power_net(name: str) -> bool:
    if not name:
        return False
    return name.upper() in {n.upper() for n in POWER_NET_NAMES}


def is_gnd_net(name: str) -> bool:
    if not name:
        return False
    return name.upper() in {n.upper() for n in GND_NET_NAMES}


_PREFIX_HINTS = [
    (re.compile(r"^diode", re.IGNORECASE), "VD"),
    (re.compile(r"^d_bridge", re.IGNORECASE), "VD"),
    (re.compile(r"^tran", re.IGNORECASE), "VT"),
    (re.compile(r"^cap", re.IGNORECASE), "C"),
    (re.compile(r"^res|^r$", re.IGNORECASE), "R"),
    (re.compile(r"^con", re.IGNORECASE), "X"),
    (re.compile(r"^7seg", re.IGNORECASE), "HG"),
    (re.compile(r"^k\d", re.IGNORECASE), "DD"),
    (re.compile(r"^led", re.IGNORECASE), "LED"),
]


def prefix_from_footprint_name(name: str) -> str:
    for rx, prefix in _PREFIX_HINTS:
        if rx.search(name or ""):
            return prefix
    letters = re.findall(r"[A-Za-zА-Яа-я]+", name or "")
    return (letters[0][:3] if letters else "U").upper()


def _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo):
    cls = self.__class__
    result = cls.__new__(cls)
    memo[id(self)] = result
    for k, v in self.__dict__.items():
        if k == 'graphics_item':
            setattr(result, k, None)
        else:
            setattr(result, k, copy.deepcopy(v, memo))
    return result


@dataclass
class PadNet:
    pad_number: str
    net_code: int
    net_name: str


@dataclass
class Net:
    code: int
    name: str


@dataclass
class BoardModule:
    footprint: Footprint
    reference: str
    value: str
    x: float = 0.0
    y: float = 0.0
    angle: float = 0.0
    layer: str = "F.Cu"
    pad_nets: Dict[str, PadNet] = field(default_factory=dict)
    uid: int = 0
    graphics_item: object = None

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo):
        return _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo)

    def get_pad_absolute(self, pad_number: str):
        pad = None
        for p in self.footprint.pads:
            if p.number == pad_number:
                pad = p
                break
        if pad is None:
            return None
        rad = math.radians(self.angle)
        cos_a = math.cos(rad)
        sin_a = math.sin(rad)
        lx = pad.x
        ly = pad.y
        gx = self.x + lx * cos_a - ly * sin_a
        gy = self.y + lx * sin_a + ly * cos_a
        return gx, gy

    def get_footprint_copy(self) -> Footprint:
        return copy.deepcopy(self.footprint)


@dataclass
class BoardLine:
    x1: float
    y1: float
    x2: float
    y2: float
    layer: str = "Edge.Cuts"
    width: float = 0.381
    uid: int = 0
    graphics_item: object = None

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo):
        return _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo)


@dataclass
class BoardCircle:
    x: float
    y: float
    radius: float
    layer: str = "Edge.Cuts"
    width: float = 0.381
    uid: int = 0
    graphics_item: object = None

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo):
        return _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo)


@dataclass
class BoardArc:
    x: float
    y: float
    sx: float
    sy: float
    angle: float = 90.0
    layer: str = "Edge.Cuts"
    width: float = 0.381
    uid: int = 0
    graphics_item: object = None

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo):
        return _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo)


@dataclass
class BoardText:
    x: float
    y: float
    text: str = "Text"
    size: float = 1.0
    layer: str = "Edge.Cuts"
    visible: bool = True
    uid: int = 0
    graphics_item: object = None

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo):
        return _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo)


@dataclass
class Board:
    name: str = "Untitled"
    modules: List[BoardModule] = field(default_factory=list)
    lines: List[BoardLine] = field(default_factory=list)
    circles: List[BoardCircle] = field(default_factory=list)
    arcs: List[BoardArc] = field(default_factory=list)
    texts: List[BoardText] = field(default_factory=list)
    nets: Dict[int, Net] = field(default_factory=dict)
    filename: Optional[str] = None

    def get_net_by_name(self, name: str) -> Optional[Net]:
        for net in self.nets.values():
            if net.name == name:
                return net
        return None

    def get_or_create_net(self, name: str) -> Net:
        net = self.get_net_by_name(name)
        if net is not None:
            return net
        code = max(self.nets.keys()) + 1 if self.nets else 1
        net = Net(code=code, name=name)
        self.nets[code] = net
        return net

    def get_net_by_code(self, code: int) -> Optional[Net]:
        return self.nets.get(code)

    def collect_net_pins(self):
        result = {}
        for module in self.modules:
            for pad_number, pad_net in module.pad_nets.items():
                if pad_net.net_code <= 0:
                    continue
                if not pad_net.net_name:
                    continue
                pos = module.get_pad_absolute(pad_number)
                if pos is None:
                    continue
                result.setdefault(pad_net.net_code, []).append((module, pad_number, pos))
        for code in result:
            result[code].sort(key=lambda x: (x[0].reference, x[1]))
        return result

    def auto_reference(self, prefix: str = "U") -> str:
        used = set()
        for m in self.modules:
            ref = m.reference or ""
            mm = re.match(r"^([A-Za-zА-Яа-я]+)(\d+)$", ref)
            if mm and mm.group(1).upper() == prefix.upper():
                used.add(int(mm.group(2)))
        i = 1
        while i in used:
            i += 1
        return f"{prefix}{i}"

    def find_pad_at(self, scene_x: float, scene_y: float, tolerance: float):
        best = None
        best_dist = tolerance
        for module in self.modules:
            for pad in module.footprint.pads:
                pos = module.get_pad_absolute(pad.number)
                if pos is None:
                    continue
                dx = scene_x - pos[0]
                dy = scene_y - pos[1]
                dist = math.hypot(dx, dy)
                if dist <= best_dist:
                    best_dist = dist
                    best = (module, pad.number)
        return best.
.

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from typing import List, Optional
import copy
from constants import Sizes


class PadShape(Enum):
    CIRCLE = "circle"
    RECT = "rect"
    OVAL = "oval"


class PadType(Enum):
    STANDARD = "standard"
    SMD = "smd"
    CONN = "conn"


class TextType(Enum):
    NORMAL = "normal"
    REFERENCE = "reference"
    VALUE = "value"


AVAILABLE_LAYERS = [
    "F.Cu", "B.Cu",
    "F.Adhes", "B.Adhes",
    "F.Paste", "B.Paste",
    "F.SilkS", "B.SilkS",
    "F.Mask", "B.Mask",
    "Cmts.User", "Eco1.User", "Eco2.User",
    "Edge.Cuts",
]

DEFAULT_LAYERS = {
    PadType.STANDARD: ["F.Cu", "B.Cu", "F.Mask", "B.Mask"],
    PadType.SMD:      ["F.Cu", "F.Paste", "F.Mask"],
    PadType.CONN:     ["F.Cu", "F.Mask"],
}


def default_layers_for_pad_type(pad_type):
    return list(DEFAULT_LAYERS.get(pad_type, DEFAULT_LAYERS[PadType.STANDARD]))


_next_uid = [1]


def new_uid():
    value = _next_uid[0]
    _next_uid[0] += 1
    return value


def _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo):
    cls = self.__class__
    result = cls.__new__(cls)
    memo[id(self)] = result
    for k, v in self.__dict__.items():
        if k == 'graphics_item':
            setattr(result, k, None)
        else:
            setattr(result, k, copy.deepcopy(v, memo))
    return result


@dataclass
class BaseObject:
    x: float
    y: float
    uid: int = field(default_factory=new_uid, kw_only=True)
    locked: bool = field(default=False, kw_only=True)

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo):
        return _deepcopy_no_graphics(self, memo)

    shape: PadShape
    pad_type: PadType
    width: float
    height: float
    drill: Optional[float] = None
    layers: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def __post_init__(self):
        if not self.layers:
            self.layers = default_layers_for_pad_type(self.pad_type)

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List


@dataclass
class NetlistComponent:
    timestamp: str
    footprint: str
    reference: str
    value: str
    pins: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)


def parse_netlist(filepath: str) -> List[NetlistComponent]:
    content = Path(filepath).read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore')
    return parse_netlist_content(content)


def parse_netlist_content(content: str) -> List[NetlistComponent]:
    components: List[NetlistComponent] = []
    lines = content.splitlines()
    i = 0
    n = len(lines)
    while i < n:
        line = lines[i].strip()
        if not line.startswith('( /'):
            i += 1
            continue

        match = re.match(
            r'^\(\s+(/\S+)\s+(\S+)\s+(\S+)\s+(.*?)\s*$',
            line
        )
        if not match:
            i += 1
            continue

        timestamp = match.group(1)
        footprint = match.group(2)
        reference = match.group(3)
        value = match.group(4).strip()

        component = NetlistComponent(
            timestamp=timestamp,
            footprint=footprint,
            reference=reference,
            value=value,
        )

        i += 1
        while i < n:
            pin_line = lines[i].strip()
            if pin_line == ')':
                break
            pin_match = re.match(r'^\(\s*(\S+)\s+(.+?)\s*\)\s*$', pin_line)
            if pin_match:
                pin_number = pin_match.group(1)
                net_name = pin_match.group(2).strip().strip('"')
                component.pins[pin_number] = net_name
            i += 1

        components.append(component)
        i += 1

    return components.
.

import math


def build_mst_edges(points):
    n = len(points)
    if n < 2:
        return []

    in_tree = [False] * n
    in_tree[0] = True
    edges = []

    best_dist = [float('inf')] * n
    best_from = [0] * n
    for j in range(1, n):
        dx = points[j][0] - points[0][0]
        dy = points[j][1] - points[0][1]
        best_dist[j] = math.hypot(dx, dy)
        best_from[j] = 0

    for _ in range(n - 1):
        u = -1
        best = float('inf')
        for j in range(n):
            if not in_tree[j] and best_dist[j] < best:
                best = best_dist[j]
                u = j
        if u == -1:
            break

        edges.append((best_from[u], u))
        in_tree[u] = True

        ux, uy = points[u][0], points[u][1]
        for v in range(n):
            if in_tree[v]:
                continue
            vx, vy = points[v][0], points[v][1]
            d = math.hypot(vx - ux, vy - uy)
            if d < best_dist[v]:
                best_dist[v] = d
                best_from[v] = u

    return edges.
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem, QStyle
from PySide6.QtGui import QPen, QColor, QPainterPath, QPainterPathStroker, QBrush
from PySide6.QtCore import QLineF, QRectF, QPointF, Qt

from constants import Colors, Sizes
from constants.board_settings import LAYER_COLORS
from pbe_editor.core.arc_geometry import arc_sample_points
from pbe_editor.graphics.text_path import build_text_path


def _layer_color(layer):
    if layer in LAYER_COLORS:
        return QColor(*LAYER_COLORS[layer])
    return QColor(*Colors.TEXT_VISIBLE)


def _snap(value, grid):
    if grid <= 0:
        return value
    return round(value / grid) * grid


class BoardLineGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    HANDLE_RADIUS_PX = 4.0
    HOVER_RADIUS_PX = 5.0
    ACTIVE_RADIUS_PX = 6.0

    def __init__(self, line, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.model = line
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.model.graphics_item = self

        self._active_handle = None
        self._hover_handle = None
        self._drag_start_scene = None
        self._drag_start_point = None

        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemSendsGeometryChanges, True)
        self.setAcceptHoverEvents(True)
        self.setZValue(2)
        self.setPos(line.x1, line.y1)

    def _local_points(self):
        return [QPointF(0.0, 0.0),
                QPointF(self.model.x2 - self.model.x1,
                        self.model.y2 - self.model.y1)]

    def get_handles(self):
        return self._local_points()

    def _hit_test_handle(self, scene_pos):
        if not self.isSelected():
            return None
        scale = self.canvas.transform().m11() if self.canvas else 1.0
        if scale <= 0:
            scale = 1.0
        radius = self.HANDLE_RADIUS_PX * 3.0 / scale
        best_idx = None
        best_dist = float('inf')
        for i, local_pt in enumerate(self.get_handles()):
            scene_pt = self.mapToScene(local_pt)
            dx = scene_pos.x() - scene_pt.x()
            dy = scene_pos.y() - scene_pt.y()
            dist = (dx * dx + dy * dy) ** 0.5
            if dist < radius and dist < best_dist:
                best_idx = i
                best_dist = dist
        return best_idx

    def boundingRect(self):
        w = self.model.width + 0.5
        x2 = self.model.x2 - self.model.x1
        y2 = self.model.y2 - self.model.y1
        return QRectF(QPointF(0.0, 0.0), QPointF(x2, y2)).normalized().adjusted(-w, -w, w, w)

    def shape(self):
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.moveTo(0.0, 0.0)
        path.lineTo(self.model.x2 - self.model.x1, self.model.y2 - self.model.y1)
        stroker = QPainterPathStroker()
        stroker.setWidth(max(0.4, self.model.width * 2.0))
        return stroker.createStroke(path)

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        selected = bool(option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected)
        color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED) if selected else _layer_color(self.model.layer)
        pen = QPen(color, max(0.05, self.model.width))
        pen.setCosmetic(False)
        pen.setCapStyle(Qt.PenCapStyle.RoundCap)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.drawLine(QLineF(0.0, 0.0,
                                self.model.x2 - self.model.x1,
                                self.model.y2 - self.model.y1))

        if selected:
            scale = self.canvas.transform().m11() if self.canvas else 1.0
            if scale <= 0:
                scale = 1.0
            for i, pt in enumerate(self.get_handles()):
                active = (i == self._active_handle)
                hover = (i == self._hover_handle)
                if active:
                    fill = QColor(0, 200, 0)
                    border = QColor(255, 255, 255)
                    r_px = self.ACTIVE_RADIUS_PX
                elif hover:
                    fill = QColor(255, 255, 255)
                    border = QColor(0, 150, 255)
                    r_px = self.HOVER_RADIUS_PX
                else:
                    fill = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
                    border = QColor(255, 255, 255)
                    r_px = self.HANDLE_RADIUS_PX
                radius = r_px / scale
                p = QPen(border, 0)
                p.setCosmetic(True)
                painter.setPen(p)
                painter.setBrush(QBrush(fill))
                painter.drawEllipse(pt, radius, radius)

    def hoverMoveEvent(self, event):
        scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.pos())
        idx = self._hit_test_handle(scene_pos)
        if idx != self._hover_handle:
            self._hover_handle = idx
            self.update()
        if idx is not None:
            self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.SizeAllCursor)
        else:
            self.unsetCursor()
        super().hoverMoveEvent(event)

    def hoverLeaveEvent(self, event):
        if self._hover_handle is not None:
            self._hover_handle = None
            self.update()
        self.unsetCursor()
        super().hoverLeaveEvent(event)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.pos())
            handle_idx = self._hit_test_handle(scene_pos)
            if handle_idx is not None:
                self._active_handle = handle_idx
                self._drag_start_scene = scene_pos
                local_pt = self.get_handles()[handle_idx]
                self._drag_start_point = (local_pt.x(), local_pt.y())
                self.update()
                event.accept()
                return
            super().mousePressEvent(event)
            if self.isSelected():
                self._drag_start_scene = scene_pos
                self._drag_start_point = (self.pos().x(), self.pos().y())
        else:
            super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        if self._active_handle is not None and (event.buttons() & Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton):
            current = self.mapToScene(event.pos())
            grid = self.canvas.grid_size
            sx = _snap(current.x(), grid)
            sy = _snap(current.y(), grid)
            m = self.model
            if self._active_handle == 0:
                m.x1 = sx
                m.y1 = sy
                self.prepareGeometryChange()
                self.update()
            elif self._active_handle == 1:
                m.x2 = sx
                m.y2 = sy
                self.prepareGeometryChange()
                self.update()
            event.accept()
            return
        super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        if self._active_handle is not None:
            self._active_handle = None
            self._drag_start_scene = None
            self._drag_start_point = None
            self.update()
            if hasattr(self.canvas, 'content_changed'):
                self.canvas.content_changed.emit()
            event.accept()
            return
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)

    def itemChange(self, change, value):
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionChange:
            if getattr(self, '_active_handle', None) is not None:
                return value
            grid = self.canvas.grid_size
            return QPointF(_snap(value.x(), grid), _snap(value.y(), grid))
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionHasChanged:
            if getattr(self, '_active_handle', None) is not None:
                return super().itemChange(change, value)
            dx = value.x() - self.model.x1
            dy = value.y() - self.model.y1
            self.model.x1 = value.x()
            self.model.y1 = value.y()
            self.model.x2 += dx
            self.model.y2 += dy
            self.prepareGeometryChange()
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemSelectedHasChanged:
            self.update()
        return super().itemChange(change, value)


class BoardCircleGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, circle, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.model = circle
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.model.graphics_item = self
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemSendsGeometryChanges, True)
        self.setZValue(2)
        self.setPos(circle.x, circle.y)

    def boundingRect(self):
        r = self.model.radius
        extra = self.model.width + 0.5
        return QRectF(-r, -r, 2 * r, 2 * r).adjusted(-extra, -extra, extra, extra)

    def shape(self):
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.addEllipse(QPointF(0.0, 0.0), self.model.radius, self.model.radius)
        stroker = QPainterPathStroker()
        stroker.setWidth(max(0.4, self.model.width * 2.0))
        return stroker.createStroke(path)

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED) if (option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected) else _layer_color(self.model.layer)
        pen = QPen(color, max(0.05, self.model.width))
        pen.setCosmetic(False)
        pen.setCapStyle(Qt.PenCapStyle.RoundCap)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
        painter.drawEllipse(QPointF(0.0, 0.0), self.model.radius, self.model.radius)

    def itemChange(self, change, value):
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionChange:
            grid = self.canvas.grid_size
            return QPointF(_snap(value.x(), grid), _snap(value.y(), grid))
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionHasChanged:
            self.model.x = value.x()
            self.model.y = value.y()
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemSelectedHasChanged:
            self.update()
        return super().itemChange(change, value)


class BoardArcGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, arc, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.model = arc
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.model.graphics_item = self
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemSendsGeometryChanges, True)
        self.setZValue(2)
        self.setPos(arc.x, arc.y)

    def _local_path(self):
        pts = arc_sample_points(
            0.0, 0.0,
            self.model.sx - self.model.x,
            self.model.sy - self.model.y,
            self.model.angle, 64,
        )
        if len(pts) < 2:
            return QPainterPath()
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1])
        for px, py in pts[1:]:
            path.lineTo(px, py)
        return path

    def boundingRect(self):
        b = self._local_path().boundingRect()
        extra = self.model.width + 0.5
        return b.adjusted(-extra, -extra, extra, extra)

    def shape(self):
        stroker = QPainterPathStroker()
        stroker.setWidth(max(0.4, self.model.width * 2.0))
        return stroker.createStroke(self._local_path())

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED) if (option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected) else _layer_color(self.model.layer)
        pen = QPen(color, max(0.05, self.model.width))
        pen.setCosmetic(False)
        pen.setCapStyle(Qt.PenCapStyle.RoundCap)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
        painter.drawPath(self._local_path())

    def itemChange(self, change, value):
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionChange:
            grid = self.canvas.grid_size
            return QPointF(_snap(value.x(), grid), _snap(value.y(), grid))
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionHasChanged:
            dx = value.x() - self.model.x
            dy = value.y() - self.model.y
            self.model.x = value.x()
            self.model.y = value.y()
            self.model.sx += dx
            self.model.sy += dy
            self.prepareGeometryChange()
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemSelectedHasChanged:
            self.update()
        return super().itemChange(change, value)


class BoardTextGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, text, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.model = text
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.model.graphics_item = self
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemSendsGeometryChanges, True)
        self.setZValue(2)
        self.setPos(text.x, text.y)
        self._path_cache = None
        self._path_key = None

    def _path(self):
        target_h = self.model.size * Sizes.TEXT_FONT_MULTIPLIER
        key = (self.model.text, target_h)
        if self._path_key != key:
            self._path_cache = build_text_path(self.model.text, target_h, center=True)
            self._path_key = key
        return self._path_cache

    def boundingRect(self):
        path = self._path()
        if path.isEmpty():
            return QRectF(-1, -1, 2, 2)
        b = path.boundingRect()
        return QRectF(b.left(), b.top(), b.width(), b.height()).adjusted(-1, -1, 1, 1)

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        path = self._path()
        if path.isEmpty():
            return
        color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED) if (option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected) else _layer_color(self.model.layer)
        painter.save()
        painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
        painter.setBrush(color)
        painter.drawPath(path)
        painter.restore()

    def itemChange(self, change, value):
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionChange:
            grid = self.canvas.grid_size
            return QPointF(_snap(value.x(), grid), _snap(value.y(), grid))
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemPositionHasChanged:
            self.model.x = value.x()
            self.model.y = value.y()
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemSelectedHasChanged:
            self.update()
        return super().itemChange(change, value).

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem, QStyle
from PySide6.QtGui import QPen, QBrush, QColor, QPainterPath
from PySide6.QtCore import QRectF, QPointF, Qt

from constants import Colors, Sizes
from pbe_editor.core.arc_geometry import arc_sample_points
from pbe_editor.core.models import PadShape, PadType
from pbe_editor.graphics.text_path import build_text_path


STANDARD_COLOR = (180, 160, 80)
SMD_COLOR = (220, 90, 90)
CONN_COLOR = (235, 150, 60)


class BoardModuleGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    def __init__(self, module, canvas, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.module = module
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.module.graphics_item = self
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsMovable, False)
        self.setPos(module.x, module.y)
        self.setRotation(module.angle)
        self.setZValue(10)
        self._drag_start_scene = None
        self._drag_start_pos = None
        self._drag_moved = False
        self._is_drag_leader = False
        self._group_drag_start = {}

    def boundingRect(self):
        fp = self.module.footprint
        xs = []
        ys = []
        for pad in fp.pads:
            xs.append(pad.x - pad.width / 2)
            xs.append(pad.x + pad.width / 2)
            ys.append(pad.y - pad.height / 2)
            ys.append(pad.y + pad.height / 2)
        for line in fp.lines:
            xs.extend([line.x, line.x2])
            ys.extend([line.y, line.y2])
        for circle in fp.circles:
            xs.extend([circle.x - circle.radius, circle.x + circle.radius])
            ys.extend([circle.y - circle.radius, circle.y + circle.radius])
        for arc in fp.arcs:
            for px, py in arc_sample_points(arc.x, arc.y, arc.sx, arc.sy, arc.angle, 32):
                xs.append(px)
                ys.append(py)
        if not xs:
            return QRectF(-10, -10, 20, 20)
        left = min(xs); right = max(xs); top = min(ys); bottom = max(ys)
        extra = 1.0
        return QRectF(left - extra, top - extra,
                      right - left + 2 * extra, bottom - top + 2 * extra)

    def shape(self):
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.addRect(self.boundingRect())
        return path

    def _pad_color(self, pad, selected):
        if selected:
            return QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
        if pad.pad_type == PadType.SMD:
            return QColor(*SMD_COLOR)
        if pad.pad_type == PadType.CONN:
            return QColor(*CONN_COLOR)
        return QColor(*STANDARD_COLOR)

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        fp = self.module.footprint
        selected = bool(option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected)
        if selected:
            outline_color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
        else:
            outline_color = QColor(200, 200, 200)
        pen = QPen(outline_color, 0.15)
        pen.setCosmetic(False)
        pen.setCapStyle(Qt.PenCapStyle.RoundCap)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
        for line in fp.lines:
            painter.drawLine(QPointF(line.x, line.y), QPointF(line.x2, line.y2))
        for circle in fp.circles:
            painter.drawEllipse(QPointF(circle.x, circle.y), circle.radius, circle.radius)
        for arc in fp.arcs:
            pts = arc_sample_points(arc.x, arc.y, arc.sx, arc.sy, arc.angle, 32)
            if len(pts) >= 2:
                path = QPainterPath()
                path.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1])
                for px, py in pts[1:]:
                    path.lineTo(px, py)
                painter.drawPath(path)
        for pad in fp.pads:
            pad_color = self._pad_color(pad, selected)
            w = pad.width; h = pad.height
            rect = QRectF(pad.x - w / 2, pad.y - h / 2, w, h)
            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            painter.setBrush(pad_color)
            if pad.shape == PadShape.RECT:
                painter.drawRect(rect)
            elif pad.shape == PadShape.OVAL:
                painter.drawRoundedRect(rect, w / 2, h / 2)
            else:
                painter.drawEllipse(rect)
            drill = pad.drill or 0.0
            if drill > 0:
                painter.setBrush(QColor(*Colors.BACKGROUND))
                painter.drawEllipse(QRectF(pad.x - drill / 2, pad.y - drill / 2, drill, drill))
            if pad.number:
                target_h = min(pad.width, pad.height) * Sizes.PAD_NUMBER_FONT_RATIO
                text_path = build_text_path(pad.number, target_h, bold=True, center=True)
                painter.save()
                painter.translate(pad.x, pad.y)
                painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
                painter.setBrush(QColor(*Colors.PAD_NUMBER))
                painter.drawPath(text_path)
                painter.restore()
        if self.module.reference:
            ref_path = build_text_path(self.module.reference, 1.5, bold=True, center=True)
            if not ref_path.isEmpty():
                bounds = self.boundingRect()
                ref_y = bounds.top() + 0.5
                painter.save()
                painter.translate(0.0, ref_y)
                painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
                painter.setBrush(QColor(150, 220, 150))
                painter.drawPath(ref_path)
                painter.restore()

    def _collect_selected_modules(self):
        self._group_drag_start = {}
        scene = self.scene()
        if scene is None:
            return
        for item in scene.selectedItems():
            if isinstance(item, BoardModuleGraphicsItem):
                self._group_drag_start[item] = (item.pos().x(), item.pos().y())

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            super().mousePressEvent(event)
            if self.isSelected():
                self._drag_start_scene = self.mapToScene(event.pos())
                self._drag_start_pos = QPointF(self.pos())
                self._drag_moved = False
                self._is_drag_leader = True
                self._collect_selected_modules()
                event.accept()
                return
        else:
            super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        if self._is_drag_leader and self._drag_start_scene is not None and \
                (event.buttons() & Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton):
            current = self.mapToScene(event.pos())
            delta = current - self._drag_start_scene
            grid = self.canvas.grid_size
            snapped_dx = round(delta.x() / grid) * grid
            snapped_dy = round(delta.y() / grid) * grid
            if snapped_dx != 0 or snapped_dy != 0:
                self._drag_moved = True
            for item, (sx, sy) in self._group_drag_start.items():
                new_x = sx + snapped_dx
                new_y = sy + snapped_dy
                item.setPos(new_x, new_y)
                item.module.x = new_x
                item.module.y = new_y
            if hasattr(self.canvas, 'ratsnest_update_requested'):
                self.canvas.ratsnest_update_requested.emit()
            self.canvas.viewport().update()
            event.accept()
        else:
            self._drag_start_scene = None
            super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        was_dragging = self._drag_moved
        self._drag_start_scene = None
        self._drag_start_pos = None
        self._drag_moved = False
        self._is_drag_leader = False
        self._group_drag_start = {}
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)
        if was_dragging and hasattr(self.canvas, 'ratsnest_update_requested'):
            self.canvas.ratsnest_update_requested.emit()

    def itemChange(self, change, value):
        if change == QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemChange.ItemSelectedHasChanged:
            self.update()
        return super().itemChange(change, value).
.

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsItem, QStyle
from PySide6.QtGui import QPen, QColor, QPainterPath, QPainterPathStroker
from PySide6.QtCore import QLineF, QRectF, QPointF, Qt

from constants import Colors


class RatsnestGraphicsItem(QGraphicsItem):
    _next_uid = [1]

    def __init__(self, p1: QPointF, p2: QPointF, net_code: int = 0,
                 net_name: str = "", is_power: bool = False,
                 is_gnd: bool = False,
                 label_a: str = "", label_b: str = "", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.p1 = QPointF(p1)
        self.p2 = QPointF(p2)
        self.net_code = net_code
        self.net_name = net_name
        self.is_power = is_power
        self.is_gnd = is_gnd
        self.label_a = label_a
        self.label_b = label_b
        self.uid = RatsnestGraphicsItem._next_uid[0]
        RatsnestGraphicsItem._next_uid[0] += 1
        self.setZValue(1)
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)
        self.setAcceptedMouseButtons(Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton | Qt.MouseButton.RightButton)

    def boundingRect(self):
        rect = QRectF(self.p1, self.p2).normalized()
        return rect.adjusted(-0.5, -0.5, 0.5, 0.5)

    def shape(self):
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.moveTo(self.p1)
        path.lineTo(self.p2)
        stroker = QPainterPathStroker()
        stroker.setWidth(0.6)
        return stroker.createStroke(path)

    def paint(self, painter, option, widget):
        if option.state & QStyle.StateFlag.State_Selected:
            color = QColor(*Colors.SELECTED)
        elif self.is_gnd:
            color = QColor(220, 70, 70)
        elif self.is_power:
            color = QColor(235, 150, 60)
        else:
            color = QColor(80, 200, 120)
        pen = QPen(color, 0)
        pen.setCosmetic(True)
        pen.setStyle(Qt.PenStyle.SolidLine)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.drawLine(QLineF(self.p1, self.p2)).
.

from PySide6.QtGui import QFont, QPainterPath, QTransform


def build_text_path(text, target_height, bold=False, center=False):
    path = QPainterPath()
    if not text or target_height <= 0:
        return path
    font = QFont()
    font.setPointSizeF(20.0)
    font.setBold(bold)
    path.addText(0, 0, font, text)
    bounds = path.boundingRect()
    if bounds.height() > 0:
        scale = target_height / bounds.height()
        t = QTransform()
        t.scale(scale, scale)
        path = t.map(path)
        if center:
            b = path.boundingRect()
            path.translate(-b.center().x(), -b.center().y())
    return path.
.

from datetime import datetime

from pbe_editor.core.models import PadShape, PadType, TextType
from pbe_editor.kicad.units import mm_to_kicad
from pbe_editor.kicad.pbe_layers import layer_to_id, drawseg_layer_to_id


def _pad_block(pad, net_code, net_name):
    shape_map = {PadShape.RECT: 'R', PadShape.CIRCLE: 'C', PadShape.OVAL: 'O'}
    shape_char = shape_map.get(pad.shape, 'C')
    if pad.pad_type == PadType.STANDARD:
        drill_val = pad.drill if pad.drill else 0.8
        dr_line = f"Dr {mm_to_kicad(drill_val)} 0 0"
        at_attr = 'STD'; at_mask = '00E0FFFF'
    elif pad.pad_type == PadType.SMD:
        dr_line = "Dr 0 0 0"; at_attr = 'SMD'; at_mask = '00888000'
    else:
        dr_line = "Dr 0 0 0"; at_attr = 'CONN'; at_mask = '00808000'
    ne_line = f'Ne {net_code} "{net_name}"' if net_code > 0 and net_name else 'Ne 0 ""'
    return [
        "$PAD",
        f'Sh "{pad.number}" {shape_char} '
        f'{mm_to_kicad(pad.width)} {mm_to_kicad(pad.height)} 0 0 0',
        dr_line,
        f"At {at_attr} N {at_mask}",
        ne_line,
        f"Po {mm_to_kicad(pad.x)} {mm_to_kicad(pad.y)}",
        "$EndPAD",
    ]


def _module_block(module, board):
    fp = module.footprint
    lines = [
        f"$MODULE {fp.name}",
        f"Po {mm_to_kicad(module.x)} {mm_to_kicad(module.y)} "
        f"{int(round(module.angle * 10))} 15 00000000 00000000 ~~",
        f"Li {fp.name}",
        "Sc 00000000",
        "AR",
        "Op 0 0 0",
    ]
    for text in fp.texts:
        if text.text_type == TextType.REFERENCE:
            idx = 0; value = module.reference
        elif text.text_type == TextType.VALUE:
            idx = 1; value = module.value
        else:
            continue
        x_k = mm_to_kicad(text.x); y_k = mm_to_kicad(text.y)
        size_k = mm_to_kicad(text.size)
        vis = 'V' if text.visible else 'I'
        lid = layer_to_id(text.layer)
        lines.append(f'T{idx} {x_k} {y_k} {size_k} {size_k} 0 120 N {vis} {lid} N "{value}"')
    for line in fp.lines:
        lid = layer_to_id(line.layer)
        lines.append(
            f"DS {mm_to_kicad(line.x)} {mm_to_kicad(line.y)} "
            f"{mm_to_kicad(line.x2)} {mm_to_kicad(line.y2)} "
            f"{mm_to_kicad(line.width)} {lid}"
        )
    for circle in fp.circles:
        x_c = mm_to_kicad(circle.x); y_c = mm_to_kicad(circle.y)
        r_k = mm_to_kicad(circle.radius); lid = layer_to_id(circle.layer)
        lines.append(f"DC {x_c} {y_c} {x_c + r_k} {y_c} {mm_to_kicad(circle.width)} {lid}")
    for arc in fp.arcs:
        lid = layer_to_id(arc.layer)
        lines.append(
            f"DA {mm_to_kicad(arc.x)} {mm_to_kicad(arc.y)} "
            f"{mm_to_kicad(arc.sx)} {mm_to_kicad(arc.sy)} "
            f"{int(round(arc.angle * 10))} {mm_to_kicad(arc.width)} {lid}"
        )
    for pad in fp.pads:
        pad_net = module.pad_nets.get(pad.number)
        net_code = pad_net.net_code if pad_net else 0
        net_name = pad_net.net_name if pad_net else ""
        lines.extend(_pad_block(pad, net_code, net_name))
    lines.append(f"$EndMODULE  {fp.name}")
    return lines


def _drawsegment_line(line):
    lid = drawseg_layer_to_id(line.layer)
    return [
        "$DRAWSEGMENT",
        f"Po 0 {mm_to_kicad(line.x1)} {mm_to_kicad(line.y1)} "
        f"{mm_to_kicad(line.x2)} {mm_to_kicad(line.y2)} {mm_to_kicad(line.width)}",
        f"De {lid} 0 900 0 0",
        "$EndDRAWSEGMENT",
    ]


def _drawsegment_circle(circle):
    lid = drawseg_layer_to_id(circle.layer)
    xc = mm_to_kicad(circle.x); yc = mm_to_kicad(circle.y)
    xp = mm_to_kicad(circle.x + circle.radius); yp = mm_to_kicad(circle.y)
    return [
        "$DRAWSEGMENT",
        f"Po 3 {xc} {yc} {xp} {yp} {mm_to_kicad(circle.width)}",
        f"De {lid} 0 900 0 0",
        "$EndDRAWSEGMENT",
    ]


def _drawsegment_arc(arc):
    lid = drawseg_layer_to_id(arc.layer)
    angle10 = int(round(arc.angle * 10))
    return [
        "$DRAWSEGMENT",
        f"Po 2 {mm_to_kicad(arc.x)} {mm_to_kicad(arc.y)} "
        f"{mm_to_kicad(arc.sx)} {mm_to_kicad(arc.sy)} {mm_to_kicad(arc.width)}",
        f"De {lid} 0 {angle10} 0 0",
        "$EndDRAWSEGMENT",
    ]


def _textpcb_block(text):
    lid = drawseg_layer_to_id(text.layer)
    x_k = mm_to_kicad(text.x); y_k = mm_to_kicad(text.y)
    size_k = mm_to_kicad(text.size)
    return [
        "$TEXTPCB",
        f'Te "{text.text}"',
        f"Po {x_k} {y_k} {size_k} {size_k} 120 0",
        f"De {lid} 1 0 Normal",
        "$EndTEXTPCB",
    ]


def build_brd_text(board):
    lines = []
    lines.append(f"PCBNEW-BOARD Version 1 date {datetime.now().strftime('%d.%m.%Y %H:%M:%S')}")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("# Created by TraceCAD")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("$GENERAL")
    lines.append("encoding utf-8")
    lines.append("LayerCount 2")
    lines.append("Ly 1FFF8001")
    lines.append("EnabledLayers 1FFF8001")
    lines.append("Links 0")
    lines.append("NoConn 0")
    lines.append("Di 0 0 117000 82670")
    lines.append(f"Ndraw {len(board.lines) + len(board.circles) + len(board.arcs)}")
    lines.append("Ntrack 0")
    lines.append("Nzone 0")
    lines.append("BoardThickness 630")
    lines.append(f"Nmodule {len(board.modules)}")
    lines.append(f"Nnets {len(board.nets) + 1}")
    lines.append("$EndGENERAL")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("$SHEETDESCR")
    lines.append("Sheet A4 11700 8267")
    lines.append('Title ""')
    lines.append(f'Date "{datetime.now().strftime("%d %b %Y")}"')
    lines.append('Rev ""')
    lines.append('Comp ""')
    lines.append('Comment1 ""')
    lines.append('Comment2 ""')
    lines.append('Comment3 ""')
    lines.append('Comment4 ""')
    lines.append("$EndSHEETDESCR")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("$SETUP")
    lines.append("InternalUnit 0.000100 INCH")
    lines.append("Layers 2")
    lines.append("Layer[0] Back signal")
    lines.append("Layer[15] Front signal")
    lines.append("TrackWidth 80")
    lines.append("TrackClearence 100")
    lines.append("ZoneClearence 200")
    lines.append("TrackMinWidth 80")
    lines.append("DrawSegmWidth 150")
    lines.append("EdgeSegmWidth 150")
    lines.append("ViaSize 350")
    lines.append("ViaDrill 250")
    lines.append("ViaMinSize 350")
    lines.append("ViaMinDrill 200")
    lines.append("MicroViaSize 200")
    lines.append("MicroViaDrill 50")
    lines.append("MicroViasAllowed 0")
    lines.append("MicroViaMinSize 200")
    lines.append("MicroViaMinDrill 50")
    lines.append("TextPcbWidth 120")
    lines.append("TextPcbSize 600 800")
    lines.append("EdgeModWidth 150")
    lines.append("TextModSize 600 600")
    lines.append("TextModWidth 120")
    lines.append("PadSize 600 600")
    lines.append("PadDrill 320")
    lines.append("Pad2MaskClearance 100")
    lines.append("AuxiliaryAxisOrg 0 0")
    lines.append("$EndSETUP")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("$EQUIPOT")
    lines.append('Na 0 ""')
    lines.append("St ~")
    lines.append("$EndEQUIPOT")
    for code in sorted(board.nets.keys()):
        net = board.nets[code]
        lines.append("$EQUIPOT")
        lines.append(f'Na {net.code} "{net.name}"')
        lines.append("St ~")
        lines.append("$EndEQUIPOT")
    lines.append("$NCLASS")
    lines.append('Name "Default"')
    lines.append('Desc "Это класс цепей по умолчанию."')
    lines.append("Clearance 100")
    lines.append("TrackWidth 80")
    lines.append("ViaDia 350")
    lines.append("ViaDrill 250")
    lines.append("uViaDia 200")
    lines.append("uViaDrill 50")
    lines.append('AddNet ""')
    for code in sorted(board.nets.keys()):
        lines.append(f'AddNet "{board.nets[code].name}"')
    lines.append("$EndNCLASS")
    lines.append("")
    for module in board.modules:
        lines.extend(_module_block(module, board))
    for text in board.texts:
        lines.extend(_textpcb_block(text))
    for line in board.lines:
        lines.extend(_drawsegment_line(line))
    for circle in board.circles:
        lines.extend(_drawsegment_circle(circle))
    for arc in board.arcs:
        lines.extend(_drawsegment_arc(arc))
    lines.append("$TRACK")
    lines.append("$EndTRACK")
    lines.append("$ZONE")
    lines.append("$EndZONE")
    lines.append("$EndBOARD")
    return "\n".join(lines).
.

import re
import math
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

from pbe_editor.core.models import (
    Footprint, Pad, PadShape, PadType, Line, Circle, Arc, Text, TextType,
    default_layers_for_pad_type,
)
from pbe_editor.kicad.units import kicad_to_mm
from pbe_editor.kicad.pbe_layers import (
    LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL, layer_from_id, drawseg_layer_from_id,
)
from pbe_editor.core.board_models import (
    Board, BoardModule, BoardLine, BoardCircle, BoardArc, BoardText, PadNet, Net,
)


def parse_brd(filepath: str) -> Board:
    content = Path(filepath).read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore')
    return parse_brd_content(content)


def _parse_de_body(body, default_layer=28):
    m = re.search(r'^De\s+(\d+)([^\r\n]*)', body, re.MULTILINE)
    if not m:
        return drawseg_layer_from_id(default_layer), 0.0
    lid = int(m.group(1))
    tail = m.group(2).split()
    angle = 0.0
    if len(tail) >= 2:
        try:
            angle = float(tail[1]) / 10.0
        except ValueError:
            pass
    return drawseg_layer_from_id(lid), angle


def parse_brd_content(content: str) -> Board:
    board = Board()
    board.filename = None

    for code_str, name in re.findall(r'\$EQUIPOT\s+Na\s+(\d+)\s+"([^"]*)"', content):
        code = int(code_str)
        if code > 0:
            board.nets[code] = Net(code=code, name=name)

    line_uid = 1
    circle_uid = 1
    arc_uid = 1
    text_uid = 1

    for ds_match in re.finditer(r'\$DRAWSEGMENT(.*?)\$EndDRAWSEGMENT', content, re.DOTALL):
        body = ds_match.group(1)
        layer_name, de_angle = _parse_de_body(body)

        po_match = re.search(r'^Po\s+([-\d\s]+)', body, re.MULTILINE)
        if not po_match:
            continue
        nums = [int(x) for x in po_match.group(1).split() if x]
        if not nums:
            continue
        shape = nums[0]
        rest = nums[1:]

        if shape == 0:
            if len(rest) == 6 and rest[0] == 0:
                x1, y1, x2, y2, thick = rest[1], rest[2], rest[3], rest[4], rest[5]
            elif len(rest) >= 5:
                x1, y1, x2, y2, thick = rest[0], rest[1], rest[2], rest[3], rest[4]
            else:
                continue
            board.lines.append(BoardLine(
                x1=kicad_to_mm(x1), y1=kicad_to_mm(y1),
                x2=kicad_to_mm(x2), y2=kicad_to_mm(y2),
                layer=layer_name, width=kicad_to_mm(thick), uid=line_uid,
            ))
            line_uid += 1
            continue

        if shape in (1, 3):
            if len(rest) == 6 and rest[0] == 0:
                xc, yc, xp, yp, thick = rest[1], rest[2], rest[3], rest[4], rest[5]
            elif len(rest) >= 5:
                xc, yc, xp, yp, thick = rest[0], rest[1], rest[2], rest[3], rest[4]
            else:
                continue
            xc_m = kicad_to_mm(xc); yc_m = kicad_to_mm(yc)
            xp_m = kicad_to_mm(xp); yp_m = kicad_to_mm(yp)
            r = math.hypot(xp_m - xc_m, yp_m - yc_m)
            board.circles.append(BoardCircle(
                x=xc_m, y=yc_m, radius=r,
                layer=layer_name, width=kicad_to_mm(thick), uid=circle_uid,
            ))
            circle_uid += 1
            continue

        if shape == 2:
            if len(rest) == 7 and rest[0] == 0:
                cx, cy, sx, sy, thick = rest[1], rest[2], rest[3], rest[4], rest[6]
            elif len(rest) >= 5:
                cx, cy, sx, sy, thick = rest[0], rest[1], rest[2], rest[3], rest[4]
            else:
                continue
            board.arcs.append(BoardArc(
                x=kicad_to_mm(cx), y=kicad_to_mm(cy),
                sx=kicad_to_mm(sx), sy=kicad_to_mm(sy),
                angle=de_angle if de_angle != 0 else 90.0,
                layer=layer_name, width=kicad_to_mm(thick), uid=arc_uid,
            ))
            arc_uid += 1

    for tp_match in re.finditer(r'\$TEXTPCB(.*?)\$EndTEXTPCB', content, re.DOTALL):
        body = tp_match.group(1)
        layer_name, _ = _parse_de_body(body)
        te_match = re.search(r'^Te\s+"([^"]*)"', body, re.MULTILINE)
        text_value = te_match.group(1) if te_match else "Text"
        po_match = re.search(
            r'^Po\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
            body, re.MULTILINE
        )
        if po_match:
            x = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(1))
            y = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(2))
            w = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(3))
            h = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(4))
        else:
            x = y = 0.0
            w = h = kicad_to_mm(400)
        size = max(w, h) if max(w, h) > 0 else 1.0
        board.texts.append(BoardText(
            x=x, y=y, text=text_value, size=size,
            layer=layer_name, visible=True, uid=text_uid,
        ))
        text_uid += 1

    modules = re.findall(r'\$MODULE\s+(\S+)(.*?)\$EndMODULE', content, re.DOTALL)
    next_uid = 1
    for name, body in modules:
        module = parse_module(name, body, next_uid)
        if module is None:
            continue
        next_uid += 1
        board.modules.append(module)

    return board


def parse_module(name: str, body: str, uid: int) -> Optional[BoardModule]:
    po_match = re.search(r'^Po\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)', body, re.MULTILINE)
    if not po_match:
        return None
    x = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(1))
    y = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(2))
    angle = float(po_match.group(3)) / 10.0

    fp = Footprint(name=name)
    reference = "REF**"
    value = "VAL**"

    t0_match = re.search(r'^T0\s+([^\r\n]+)', body, re.MULTILINE)
    t1_match = re.search(r'^T1\s+([^\r\n]+)', body, re.MULTILINE)
    if t0_match:
        ref_text = _parse_text_line(t0_match.group(1), TextType.REFERENCE)
        ref_text.locked = True
        fp.texts.append(ref_text)
        reference = ref_text.text
        fp.reference = reference
    if t1_match:
        val_text = _parse_text_line(t1_match.group(1), TextType.VALUE)
        val_text.locked = True
        fp.texts.append(val_text)
        value = val_text.text
        fp.value = value

    for ds_match in re.finditer(
        r'^DS\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
        body, re.MULTILINE
    ):
        x1, y1, x2, y2, thickness, layer_id = ds_match.groups()
        fp.lines.append(Line(
            x=kicad_to_mm(x1), y=kicad_to_mm(y1),
            x2=kicad_to_mm(x2), y2=kicad_to_mm(y2),
            width=kicad_to_mm(thickness),
            layer=layer_from_id(layer_id),
        ))

    for dc_match in re.finditer(
        r'^DC\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
        body, re.MULTILINE
    ):
        xc, yc, xp, yp, thickness, layer_id = dc_match.groups()
        xc_mm = kicad_to_mm(xc); yc_mm = kicad_to_mm(yc)
        xp_mm = kicad_to_mm(xp); yp_mm = kicad_to_mm(yp)
        radius = math.hypot(xp_mm - xc_mm, yp_mm - yc_mm)
        fp.circles.append(Circle(
            x=xc_mm, y=yc_mm, radius=radius,
            width=kicad_to_mm(thickness),
            layer=layer_from_id(layer_id),
        ))

    for da_match in re.finditer(
        r'^DA\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
        body, re.MULTILINE
    ):
        cx, cy, sx, sy, angle_a, thickness, layer_id = da_match.groups()
        fp.arcs.append(Arc(
            x=kicad_to_mm(cx), y=kicad_to_mm(cy),
            sx=kicad_to_mm(sx), sy=kicad_to_mm(sy),
            angle=float(angle_a) / 10.0,
            width=kicad_to_mm(thickness),
            layer=layer_from_id(layer_id),
        ))

    module = BoardModule(
        footprint=fp, reference=reference, value=value,
        x=x, y=y, angle=angle, layer="F.Cu", uid=uid,
    )

    for pad_match in re.finditer(r'\$PAD(.*?)\$EndPAD', body, re.DOTALL):
        pad, pad_net = _parse_pad(pad_match.group(1))
        if pad is not None:
            fp.pads.append(pad)
            if pad_net is not None:
                module.pad_nets[pad.number] = pad_net

    return module


def _parse_text_line(text_str: str, text_type: TextType) -> Text:
    text_match = re.search(r'"([^"]*)"', text_str)
    text = text_match.group(1) if text_match else ""
    params_str = text_str[:text_match.start()].strip() if text_match else text_str
    params = params_str.split()
    x = kicad_to_mm(params[0]) if len(params) > 0 else 0
    y = kicad_to_mm(params[1]) if len(params) > 1 else 0
    w = kicad_to_mm(params[2]) if len(params) > 2 else kicad_to_mm(400)
    h = kicad_to_mm(params[3]) if len(params) > 3 else w
    size = max(w, h) if max(w, h) > 0 else 1.0
    visible = 'I' not in params[4:8]
    layer = "F.SilkS"
    for tok in params:
        try:
            layer_id = int(tok)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if layer_id in LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL:
            layer = layer_from_id(layer_id)
            break
    return Text(
        x=x, y=y, text=text, size=size,
        visible=visible, text_type=text_type, locked=True,
        layer=layer,
    )


def _parse_pad(pad_body: str):
    sh_match = re.search(r'Sh\s+"([^"]*)"\s+(\w+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)', pad_body)
    dr_match = re.search(r'Dr\s+(\d+)', pad_body)
    po_match = re.search(r'Po\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)', pad_body)
    at_match = re.search(r'At\s+(\w+)', pad_body)
    ne_match = re.search(r'^Ne\s+(\d+)\s+"([^"]*)"', pad_body, re.MULTILINE)
    if not sh_match or not po_match:
        return None, None

    number = sh_match.group(1)
    shape_str = sh_match.group(2)
    size_x = kicad_to_mm(sh_match.group(3))
    size_y = kicad_to_mm(sh_match.group(4))
    x = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(1))
    y = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(2))
    drill = kicad_to_mm(dr_match.group(1)) if dr_match else 0

    shape_map = {'R': PadShape.RECT, 'C': PadShape.CIRCLE, 'O': PadShape.OVAL}
    shape = shape_map.get(shape_str, PadShape.CIRCLE)

    pad_type = PadType.STANDARD
    if at_match:
        attr = at_match.group(1)
        if attr == 'SMD':
            pad_type = PadType.SMD
        elif attr == 'CONN':
            pad_type = PadType.CONN

    pad = Pad(
        x=x, y=y, number=number, shape=shape, pad_type=pad_type,
        width=size_x, height=size_y,
        drill=drill if drill > 0 else None,
        layers=default_layers_for_pad_type(pad_type),
    )

    pad_net = None
    if ne_match:
        code = int(ne_match.group(1))
        name = ne_match.group(2)
        if code > 0 and name:
            pad_net = PadNet(pad_number=number, net_code=code, net_name=name)

    return pad, pad_net.
.

import re
import math
from pathlib import Path

from pbe_editor.core.models import (
    Footprint, Pad, PadShape, PadType, Line, Circle, Arc, Text, TextType,
    default_layers_for_pad_type,
)
from pbe_editor.kicad.units import kicad_to_mm
from pbe_editor.kicad.pbe_layers import KICAD_LAYER_TO_INTERNAL, LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL, layer_from_id


TEXT_PATTERN_SIMPLE = re.compile(r'^T(\d+)\s+([^\r\n"]+?)\s+"([^"]*)"', re.MULTILINE)


def parse_kicad_mod(filepath):
    content = Path(filepath).read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore')
    return parse_kicad_content(content)


def parse_kicad_content(content):
    footprints = []
    pattern = re.compile(r'\$MODULE\s+(\S+)(.*?)\$EndMODULE', re.DOTALL)
    for match in pattern.finditer(content):
        name = match.group(1)
        body = match.group(2)
        footprints.append(parse_module(name, body))
    return footprints


def parse_module(name, body):
    fp = Footprint(name=name)

    t0_match = re.search(r'^T0\s+([^\r\n]+)', body, re.MULTILINE)
    t1_match = re.search(r'^T1\s+([^\r\n]+)', body, re.MULTILINE)
    if t0_match:
        ref_text = parse_text_line(t0_match.group(1), TextType.REFERENCE)
        ref_text.locked = True
        fp.texts.append(ref_text)
        fp.reference = ref_text.text
    if t1_match:
        val_text = parse_text_line(t1_match.group(1), TextType.VALUE)
        val_text.locked = True
        fp.texts.append(val_text)
        fp.value = val_text.text

    for text_match in TEXT_PATTERN_SIMPLE.finditer(body):
        idx = int(text_match.group(1))
        if idx in (0, 1):
            continue
        params = text_match.group(2).split()
        text_value = text_match.group(3)
        x = kicad_to_mm(params[0]) if len(params) > 0 else 0
        y = kicad_to_mm(params[1]) if len(params) > 1 else 0
        w = kicad_to_mm(params[2]) if len(params) > 2 else kicad_to_mm(400)
        h = kicad_to_mm(params[3]) if len(params) > 3 else w
        size = max(w, h) if max(w, h) > 0 else 1.0
        visible = 'I' not in params[4:8]
        layer = "F.SilkS"
        for tok in params:
            try:
                layer_id = int(tok)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            if layer_id in LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL:
                layer = layer_from_id(layer_id)
                break
        fp.texts.append(Text(
            x=x, y=y,
            text=text_value if text_value else "Text",
            size=size, visible=visible,
            text_type=TextType.NORMAL, layer=layer,
        ))

    for ds_match in re.finditer(
        r'^DS\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
        body, re.MULTILINE
    ):
        x1, y1, x2, y2, thickness, layer_id = ds_match.groups()
        fp.lines.append(Line(
            x=kicad_to_mm(x1), y=kicad_to_mm(y1),
            x2=kicad_to_mm(x2), y2=kicad_to_mm(y2),
            width=kicad_to_mm(thickness),
            layer=layer_from_id(layer_id),
        ))

    for dc_match in re.finditer(
        r'^DC\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
        body, re.MULTILINE
    ):
        xc, yc, xp, yp, thickness, layer_id = dc_match.groups()
        xc_mm = kicad_to_mm(xc); yc_mm = kicad_to_mm(yc)
        xp_mm = kicad_to_mm(xp); yp_mm = kicad_to_mm(yp)
        radius = math.hypot(xp_mm - xc_mm, yp_mm - yc_mm)
        fp.circles.append(Circle(
            x=xc_mm, y=yc_mm, radius=radius,
            width=kicad_to_mm(thickness),
            layer=layer_from_id(layer_id),
        ))

    for da_match in re.finditer(
        r'^DA\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)',
        body, re.MULTILINE
    ):
        cx, cy, sx, sy, angle, thickness, layer_id = da_match.groups()
        fp.arcs.append(Arc(
            x=kicad_to_mm(cx), y=kicad_to_mm(cy),
            sx=kicad_to_mm(sx), sy=kicad_to_mm(sy),
            angle=float(angle) / 10.0,
            width=kicad_to_mm(thickness),
            layer=layer_from_id(layer_id),
        ))

    for pad_match in re.finditer(r'\$PAD(.*?)\$EndPAD', body, re.DOTALL):
        pad = parse_pad(pad_match.group(1))
        if pad:
            fp.pads.append(pad)

    return fp


def parse_text_line(text_str, text_type):
    text_match = re.search(r'"([^"]*)"', text_str)
    text = text_match.group(1) if text_match else ""
    params_str = text_str[:text_match.start()].strip() if text_match else text_str
    params = params_str.split()
    x = kicad_to_mm(params[0]) if len(params) > 0 else 0
    y = kicad_to_mm(params[1]) if len(params) > 1 else 0
    w = kicad_to_mm(params[2]) if len(params) > 2 else kicad_to_mm(400)
    h = kicad_to_mm(params[3]) if len(params) > 3 else w
    size = max(w, h) if max(w, h) > 0 else 1.0
    visible = 'I' not in params[4:8]
    layer = "F.SilkS"
    for tok in params:
        try:
            layer_id = int(tok)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if layer_id in LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL:
            layer = layer_from_id(layer_id)
            break
    return Text(
        x=x, y=y, text=text, size=size,
        visible=visible, text_type=text_type, locked=True,
        layer=layer,
    )


def parse_pad(pad_body):
    sh_match = re.search(r'Sh\s+"([^"]*)"\s+(\w+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)', pad_body)
    dr_match = re.search(r'Dr\s+(\d+)', pad_body)
    po_match = re.search(r'Po\s+(-?\d+)\s+(-?\d+)', pad_body)
    at_match = re.search(r'At\s+(\w+)', pad_body)
    la_match = re.search(r'^La\s+([^\r\n]+)', pad_body, re.MULTILINE)
    if not sh_match or not po_match:
        return None
    number = sh_match.group(1)
    shape_str = sh_match.group(2)
    size_x = kicad_to_mm(sh_match.group(3))
    size_y = kicad_to_mm(sh_match.group(4))
    x = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(1))
    y = kicad_to_mm(po_match.group(2))
    drill = kicad_to_mm(dr_match.group(1)) if dr_match else 0
    shape_map = {'R': PadShape.RECT, 'C': PadShape.CIRCLE, 'O': PadShape.OVAL}
    shape = shape_map.get(shape_str, PadShape.CIRCLE)
    pad_type = PadType.STANDARD
    if at_match:
        attr = at_match.group(1)
        if attr == 'SMD':
            pad_type = PadType.SMD
        elif attr == 'CONN':
            pad_type = PadType.CONN
    layers = []
    if la_match:
        for token in la_match.group(1).split():
            internal = KICAD_LAYER_TO_INTERNAL.get(token)
            if internal and internal not in layers:
                layers.append(internal)
    if not layers:
        layers = default_layers_for_pad_type(pad_type)
    return Pad(
        x=x, y=y,
        number=number, shape=shape, pad_type=pad_type,
        width=size_x, height=size_y,
        drill=drill if drill > 0 else None,
        layers=layers,
    ).
.

def layer_from_id(layer_id):
    try:
        lid = int(layer_id)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return "F.SilkS"
    return LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL.get(lid, "F.SilkS")


def layer_to_id(layer):
    return LAYER_ID.get(layer, 40)


def drawseg_layer_from_id(layer_id):
    try:
        lid = int(layer_id)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return "Edge.Cuts"
    return DRAWSEG_LAYER_ID_TO_INTERNAL.get(lid, "Edge.Cuts")


def drawseg_layer_to_id(layer):
    return DRAWSEG_LAYER_ID.get(layer, 28).

KICAD_UNIT_MM = 0.00254


def kicad_to_mm(value):
    return float(value) * KICAD_UNIT_MM


def mm_to_kicad(value):
    return int(round(float(value) / KICAD_UNIT_MM)).

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsView, QGraphicsScene, QGraphicsItem
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QPointF, Signal, QTimer
from PySide6.QtGui import QColor, QWheelEvent, QMouseEvent, QPainter, QPen, QPainterPath

from constants import Colors, Sizes, Steps, VK_W, VK_A, VK_S, VK_D
from pbe_editor.core.arc_geometry import arc_sample_points
from pbe_editor.graphics.text_path import build_text_path


class BoardCanvas(QGraphicsView):
    zoom_changed = Signal(float)
    mouse_moved = Signal(float, float)
    ratsnest_update_requested = Signal()
    canvas_clicked = Signal(float, float)
    canvas_right_clicked = Signal()
    selection_changed = Signal()
    content_changed = Signal()

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._panning = False
        self._pan_start = None
        self.show_grid = True
        self.show_snap = True
        self._highlight_pad_pos = None
        self._highlight_pad_radius = 0.0

        self.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.setMouseTracking(True)
        self.viewport().setMouseTracking(True)
        self.setDragMode(QGraphicsView.DragMode.RubberBandDrag)
        self.setRubberBandSelectionMode(Qt.ItemSelectionMode.IntersectsItemShape)
        self.setViewportUpdateMode(QGraphicsView.ViewportUpdateMode.FullViewportUpdate)
        self.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)
        self.setFrameShape(QGraphicsView.Shape.Box)
        self.setFrameShadow(QGraphicsView.Shadow.Plain)
        self.setLineWidth(1)
        self.setStyleSheet("QGraphicsView { border: 1px solid #ffffff; }")
        self.setFocusPolicy(Qt.FocusPolicy.StrongFocus)

        self.scene = QGraphicsScene(self)
        self.setScene(self.scene)
        self.scene.setSceneRect(-2000, -2000, 4000, 4000)
        self.scene.selectionChanged.connect(self.selection_changed.emit)

        self.grid_size = float(Steps.DEFAULT_GRID)
        self.grid_color = QColor(*Colors.GRID)
        self.background_color = QColor(*Colors.BACKGROUND)
        self.setBackgroundBrush(self.background_color)

        self.base_scale = Sizes.BASE_SCALE / 4.0
        self.scale(self.base_scale, self.base_scale)
        self.zoom_steps = Steps.ZOOM
        self.zoom_index = self.zoom_steps.index(1.0)
        self.zoom_level = 1.0

        self._snap_pos = None

        self.pressed_keys = set()
        self.pan_speed_px = Sizes.PAN_SPEED_PX
        self.pan_timer = QTimer(self)
        self.pan_timer.setInterval(Sizes.PAN_TIMER_INTERVAL)
        self.pan_timer.timeout.connect(self._pan_step)
        self._scroll_acc_x = 0.0
        self._scroll_acc_y = 0.0

        self._ghost_line_start = None
        self._ghost_line_end = None
        self._ghost_circle_center = None
        self._ghost_circle_radius = 0.0
        self._ghost_arc = None
        self._ghost_text_pos = None
        self._ghost_text_content = ""
        self._ghost_text_size = 1.0
        self._ghost_text_visible = True

    def apply_theme(self):
        self.grid_color = QColor(*Colors.GRID)
        self.background_color = QColor(*Colors.BACKGROUND)
        self.setBackgroundBrush(self.background_color)
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_grid_size(self, size_mm):
        self.grid_size = size_mm
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_show_snap(self, show):
        self.show_snap = show
        if not show:
            self._snap_pos = None
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_highlight_pad(self, pos, radius):
        self._highlight_pad_pos = QPointF(pos)
        self._highlight_pad_radius = radius
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_highlight_pad(self):
        if self._highlight_pad_pos is not None:
            self._highlight_pad_pos = None
            self._highlight_pad_radius = 0.0
            self.viewport().update()

    def snap_to_grid(self, x, y):
        g = self.grid_size
        return QPointF(round(x / g) * g, round(y / g) * g)

    def set_ghost_line(self, x1, y1, x2, y2):
        self._ghost_line_start = QPointF(x1, y1)
        self._ghost_line_end = QPointF(x2, y2)
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_ghost_line(self):
        self._ghost_line_start = None
        self._ghost_line_end = None
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_ghost_circle(self, cx, cy, r):
        self._ghost_circle_center = QPointF(cx, cy)
        self._ghost_circle_radius = r
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_ghost_circle(self):
        self._ghost_circle_center = None
        self._ghost_circle_radius = 0.0
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_ghost_arc(self, cx, cy, sx, sy, angle):
        self._ghost_arc = (cx, cy, sx, sy, angle)
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_ghost_arc(self):
        self._ghost_arc = None
        self.viewport().update()

    def set_ghost_text(self, x, y, text, size, visible):
        self._ghost_text_pos = QPointF(x, y)
        self._ghost_text_content = text
        self._ghost_text_size = size
        self._ghost_text_visible = visible
        self.viewport().update()

    def clear_ghost_text(self):
        self._ghost_text_pos = None
        self._ghost_text_content = ""
        self._ghost_text_size = 1.0
        self._ghost_text_visible = True
        self.viewport().update()

    def drawBackground(self, painter, rect):
        super().drawBackground(painter, rect)
        if not self.show_grid:
            return
        g = self.grid_size
        scale = self.transform().m11()
        if g * scale < Sizes.MIN_CELL_PX:
            return

        left = int(rect.left() // g) * g
        right = int(rect.right() // g) * g
        top = int(rect.top() // g) * g
        bottom = int(rect.bottom() // g) * g

        step_x = g
        step_y = g
        max_points = 60
        if (right - left) / g > max_points:
            factor = 1
            while (right - left) / (g * factor) > max_points:
                factor *= 2
            step_x = g * factor
        if (bottom - top) / g > max_points:
            factor = 1
            while (bottom - top) / (g * factor) > max_points:
                factor *= 2
            step_y = g * factor

        pen = QPen(self.grid_color, 0)
        pen.setCosmetic(True)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        y = top
        while y <= bottom:
            x = left
            while x <= right:
                painter.drawPoint(QPointF(x, y))
                x += step_x
            y += step_y

    def drawForeground(self, painter, rect):
        super().drawForeground(painter, rect)
        pen = QPen(QColor(*Colors.AXIS), 0)
        pen.setCosmetic(True)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.drawLine(QPointF(rect.left(), 0), QPointF(rect.right(), 0))
        painter.drawLine(QPointF(0, rect.top()), QPointF(0, rect.bottom()))

        if self._snap_pos and self.show_snap:
            cross = Sizes.CROSS_SIZE / self.transform().m11()
            p = QPen(QColor(*Colors.SNAP_CROSS), 0)
            p.setCosmetic(True)
            painter.setPen(p)
            x = self._snap_pos.x()
            y = self._snap_pos.y()
            painter.drawLine(QPointF(x - cross, y), QPointF(x + cross, y))
            painter.drawLine(QPointF(x, y - cross), QPointF(x, y + cross))

        if self._highlight_pad_pos is not None and self._highlight_pad_radius > 0:
            hp = QPen(QColor(255, 200, 0), 0)
            hp.setCosmetic(True)
            hp.setWidth(2)
            painter.setPen(hp)
            painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
            painter.drawEllipse(self._highlight_pad_pos,
                                self._highlight_pad_radius,
                                self._highlight_pad_radius)

        ghost_pen = QPen(QColor(*Colors.GHOST_LINE), 0)
        ghost_pen.setCosmetic(True)
        ghost_pen.setStyle(Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)
        painter.setPen(ghost_pen)
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)

        if self._ghost_line_start and self._ghost_line_end:
            painter.drawLine(self._ghost_line_start, self._ghost_line_end)

        if self._ghost_circle_center and self._ghost_circle_radius > 0:
            painter.drawEllipse(self._ghost_circle_center,
                                self._ghost_circle_radius,
                                self._ghost_circle_radius)

        if self._ghost_arc is not None:
            cx, cy, sx, sy, angle = self._ghost_arc
            pts = arc_sample_points(cx, cy, sx, sy, angle, 64)
            if len(pts) >= 2:
                path = QPainterPath()
                path.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1])
                for px, py in pts[1:]:
                    path.lineTo(px, py)
                painter.drawPath(path)

        if self._ghost_text_pos and self._ghost_text_content:
            path = build_text_path(
                self._ghost_text_content,
                self._ghost_text_size * Sizes.TEXT_FONT_MULTIPLIER,
                center=True,
            )
            if not path.isEmpty():
                color = QColor(*Colors.GHOST_TEXT)
                if not self._ghost_text_visible:
                    color = color.lighter(50)
                painter.save()
                painter.translate(self._ghost_text_pos)
                painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
                painter.setBrush(color)
                painter.drawPath(path)
                painter.restore()

    def wheelEvent(self, event: QWheelEvent):
        if event.angleDelta().y() > 0:
            if self.zoom_index < len(self.zoom_steps) - 1:
                self.zoom_index += 1
        elif self.zoom_index > 0:
            self.zoom_index -= 1
        new_zoom = self.zoom_steps[self.zoom_index]
        factor = (self.base_scale * new_zoom) / self.transform().m11()
        self.scale(factor, factor)
        self.zoom_level = new_zoom
        self.zoom_changed.emit(self.zoom_level)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.MiddleButton:
            self._panning = True
            self._pan_start = event.position().toPoint()
            self.viewport().setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.ClosedHandCursor)
            event.accept()
            return
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.RightButton:
            self.canvas_right_clicked.emit()
            event.accept()
            return
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            if self.dragMode() == QGraphicsView.DragMode.RubberBandDrag:
                items = self.items(event.position().toPoint())
                selectable = [it for it in items if it.flags() & QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable]
                if selectable and (event.modifiers() & Qt.KeyboardModifier.ControlModifier):
                    selectable[0].setSelected(not selectable[0].isSelected())
                    event.accept()
                    return
                if not selectable:
                    scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.position().toPoint())
                    snapped = self.snap_to_grid(scene_pos.x(), scene_pos.y())
                    self.canvas_clicked.emit(snapped.x(), snapped.y())
                super().mousePressEvent(event)
                return
            items = self.items(event.position().toPoint())
            selectable = [it for it in items if it.flags() & QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable]
            if not selectable:
                scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.position().toPoint())
                snapped = self.snap_to_grid(scene_pos.x(), scene_pos.y())
                self.canvas_clicked.emit(snapped.x(), snapped.y())
                event.accept()
                return
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.MiddleButton and self._panning:
            self._panning = False
            self.viewport().unsetCursor()
            event.accept()
            return
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        if self._panning:
            current_pos = event.position().toPoint()
            delta = current_pos - self._pan_start
            self._pan_start = current_pos
            self.horizontalScrollBar().setValue(self.horizontalScrollBar().value() - delta.x())
            self.verticalScrollBar().setValue(self.verticalScrollBar().value() - delta.y())
            event.accept()
            return
        scene_pos = self.mapToScene(event.position().toPoint())
        snapped = self.snap_to_grid(scene_pos.x(), scene_pos.y())
        if self.show_snap:
            self._snap_pos = QPointF(snapped.x(), snapped.y())
        else:
            self._snap_pos = None
        self.mouse_moved.emit(snapped.x(), snapped.y())
        self.viewport().update()
        super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def _pan_step(self):
        dx = dy = 0
        if VK_W in self.pressed_keys:
            dy -= 1
        if VK_S in self.pressed_keys:
            dy += 1
        if VK_A in self.pressed_keys:
            dx -= 1
        if VK_D in self.pressed_keys:
            dx += 1
        if dx == 0 and dy == 0:
            return
        self._scroll_acc_x += dx * self.pan_speed_px
        self._scroll_acc_y += dy * self.pan_speed_px
        ix = int(self._scroll_acc_x)
        iy = int(self._scroll_acc_y)
        self._scroll_acc_x -= ix
        self._scroll_acc_y -= iy
        if ix or iy:
            self.horizontalScrollBar().setValue(self.horizontalScrollBar().value() + ix)
            self.verticalScrollBar().setValue(self.verticalScrollBar().value() + iy).
.

    def _apply_startup_geometry(self):
        screen = QGuiApplication.primaryScreen()
        if screen is None:
            self.resize(1280, 800)
            return
        avail = screen.availableGeometry()
        self.setGeometry(avail)
        self.setWindowState(self.windowState() | Qt.WindowState.WindowMaximized)

    def _init_canvas(self):

    def _init_docks(self):
        self.module_dock = QDockWidget("", self)
        self.module_dock.setAllowedAreas(Qt.LeftDockWidgetArea | Qt.RightDockWidgetArea)

        panel = QWidget()

        panel.setMaximumWidth(320)
        layout = QVBoxLayout(panel)
